01

机器学习辅助发现金刚石中与碳相关的缺陷形成机制#

原题：Machine Learning-Assisted Discovery of Carbon-Related Defect Formation Mechanisms in Diamond
作者：Grace Huang、Christopher Fivecoat、Xun Xu、Mengen Wang。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：金刚石是固态自旋量子位等功能缺陷的重要主体材料。在通过辐射和随后的热退火产生缺陷的过程中，会产生与碳相关的缺陷，包括空位和填隙，并且可以迁移形成新的缺陷复合体。为了研究这种动力学驱动的过程并揭示中性电荷态双填隙和双空位的形成机制，我们使用密度泛函理论计算生成的小数据集对通用机器学习原子间势进行了微调。我们获得了单填隙迁移的势垒为 1.84 eV，并发现两个最初分离的单填隙只需克服较低的势垒即可形成双填隙构型。除了训练数据之外，MLIP…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金刚石是固态自旋量子位等功能缺陷的重要主体材料。在通过辐射和随后的热退火产生缺陷的过程中，会产生与碳相关的缺陷，包括空位和填隙，并且可以迁移形成新的缺陷复合体。为了研究这种动力学驱动的过程并揭示中性电荷态双填隙和双空位的形成机制，我们使用密度泛函理论计算生成的小数据集对通用机器学习原子间势进行了微调。我们获得了单填隙迁移的势垒为 1.84 eV，并发现两个最初分离的单填隙只需克服较低的势垒即可形成双填隙构型。除了训练数据之外，MLIP 还确定了额外的双间隙构型和逐步双空位解离机制的形成途径。这些结果揭示了迁移辅助缺陷复合体形成机制，并证明了有针对性的 MLIP 训练如何识别反应途径。

English Abstract

Diamond is an important host material for functional defects such as solid-state spin qubits. During the defect creation by irradiation and subsequent thermal annealing, carbon-related defects, including vacancies and interstitials, are generated and can migrate to form new defect complexes. To investigate this kinetically driven process and reveal the formation mechanisms of the di-interstitials and di-vacancies in the neutral charge state, we fine-tuned a universal machine learning interatomic potential using a small dataset generated from density functional theory calculations. We obtained a barrier of 1.84 eV for mono-interstitial migration and found that two initially separated mono-interstitials only need to overcome a lower barrier to form di-interstitial configurations. Beyond the training data, the MLIP also identified the formation pathway for an additional di-interstitial configuration and a stepwise divacancy dissociation mechanism. These results reveal migration-assisted defect complex formation mechanisms and demonstrate how targeted MLIP training can identify reaction pathways.

02

可扩展的远程机器学习原子间势与原子材料建模的电响应#

原题：Scalable long-range machine learning interatomic potentials with electrical response for atomistic material modeling
作者：Yicheng Chen、Xiaoyun Yu、Hyejeong Song、Xintian Wang、Zihui Li、Zixi Xia 等 8 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-19
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在电场下对材料进行精确的原子模拟需要在长度尺度上进行长程静电和电响应建模，这超出了传统第一原理方法的范围。然而，这些功能仍然难以与机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的可扩展性相协调。严格的局部模型可以实现高效的空间并行化，但忽略了远程交互，而现有的静电扩展引入了阻碍分布式计算的全局依赖性。在这里，我们通过分析将长程静电力和玻恩有效电荷（BEC）分离为全局静电计算和学习潜电荷的严格局部导数来克服这一限制。该公式保留了等变 MLIP 的空间可分解…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、born effective charges。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在电场下对材料进行精确的原子模拟需要在长度尺度上进行长程静电和电响应建模，这超出了传统第一原理方法的范围。然而，这些功能仍然难以与机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的可扩展性相协调。严格的局部模型可以实现高效的空间并行化，但忽略了远程交互，而现有的静电扩展引入了阻碍分布式计算的全局依赖性。在这里，我们通过分析将长程静电力和玻恩有效电荷（BEC）分离为全局静电计算和学习潜电荷的严格局部导数来克服这一限制。该公式保留了等变 MLIP 的空间可分解性，同时能够跨不同边界条件对能量、力、应力和 BEC 进行分布式评估。该框架在 textttnvalchemi 平台上以 DPA4C-LR 的形式实现，可在四个 GPU 上扩展到 520 万个原子，计算成本约为其短程主干的两倍。经过能量和力的训练，DPA4C-LR 可以准确推断 BEC 并再现水的场相关红外光谱。我们通过恢复弛豫剂 cePb(Mg₁/₃Nb2/₃)O3 特有的微小偏振迟滞来进一步证明其能力，该迟滞在较小的模拟中被有限尺寸效应所掩盖；预测非晶 ceLi₀.₉NbO₀.₉Cl4.₁ 中的室温场驱动离子电导率为 6.8 ± 0.7~mS/cm；并利用平板校正静电对跨硅/金刚石界面的界面热传输进行建模。通过结合远程物理、电响应推理和分布式计算，该框架将预测原子建模扩展到与设备相关的长度尺度。

English Abstract

Accurate atomistic simulations of materials under electric fields require long-range electrostatics and electrical-response modeling at length scales beyond the reach of conventional first-principles methods. Yet these capabilities remain difficult to reconcile with the scalability of machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs). Strictly local models enable efficient spatial parallelization but neglect long-range interactions, whereas existing electrostatic extensions introduce global dependencies that hinder distributed computation. Here, we overcome this limitation by analytically separating the long-range electrostatic forces and Born effective charges (BECs) into a global electrostatics calculation and strictly local derivatives of learned latent charges. This formulation preserves the spatial decomposability of equivariant MLIPs while enabling distributed evaluation of energies, forces, stresses, and BECs across different boundary conditions. Implemented as DPA4C-LR on the textttnvalchemi platform, the framework scales to 5.2 million atoms on four GPUs at approximately twice the computational cost of its short-range backbone. Trained on energies and forces, DPA4C-LR accurately infers BECs and reproduces the field-dependent infrared spectra of water. We further demonstrate its capabilities by recovering the characteristic slim polarization hysteresis of relaxor cePb(Mg₁/₃Nb₂/₃)O3, which is obscured by finite-size effects in smaller simulations; predicting a room-temperature field-driven ionic conductivity of 6.8 ± 0.7~mS/cm in amorphous ceLi₀.₉NbO₀.₉Cl₄.₁; and modeling interfacial thermal transport across Si/diamond interfaces with slab-corrected electrostatics. By combining long-range physics, electrical-response inference, and distributed computation, this framework extends predictive atomistic modeling toward device-relevant length scales.

03

基于晶体结构的图神经网络和LLM多模态方法用于材料磁有序预测#

原题：Crystal Structure Based Graph Neural Network And LLM Multi-Modal Approach for Material Magnetic Ordering Prediction
作者：Adam Zheng、April Zheng、Sheng Ran。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：AI/ML 模型对于预测材料特性非常有用。最近，在开发神经网络模型来预测固体、分子和晶体的特性方面取得了巨大进展，避免了昂贵且耗时的实验室测试。本研究提出了一种基于 AI/ML 的新型多模态方法，使用材料晶体结构信息作为图神经网络模型 (GNN) 的输入，将材料属性和结构的语义文本描述作为大语言模型 (LLM) 的输入，然后对 GNN 和 LLM 输出进行信息融合，以预测四类（非磁性、铁磁性、反铁磁性、亚铁磁性）的材料磁排序。该模型在数…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

AI/ML 模型对于预测材料特性非常有用。最近，在开发神经网络模型来预测固体、分子和晶体的特性方面取得了巨大进展，避免了昂贵且耗时的实验室测试。本研究提出了一种基于 AI/ML 的新型多模态方法，使用材料晶体结构信息作为图神经网络模型 (GNN) 的输入，将材料属性和结构的语义文本描述作为大语言模型 (LLM) 的输入，然后对 GNN 和 LLM 输出进行信息融合，以预测四类（非磁性、铁磁性、反铁磁性、亚铁磁性）的材料磁排序。该模型在数据集上进行训练、验证和测试，该数据集是材料项目数据集、Magndata 数据集和一组在实验室测试中确定磁序的铀化合物的组合，数据分割为 80/10/10（训练/验证/测试）。所提出的 GNN 和 LLM 多模态方法比具有三种融合策略的独立 GNN 和 LLM 方法实现了更好的整体精度。

English Abstract

AI/ML models are useful for the prediction of materials' properties. Recently, there has been great progress in developing Neural Network models to predict the properties of solids, molecules, and crystals, avoiding expensive and time-consuming laboratory testing. This study presents a novel AI/ML-based multi-modal approach to predict material magnetic ordering using material crystal structure information as input to a Graph Neural Network model (GNN) and semantic text descriptions of the material's properties and structure as input to a Large Language Model (LLM), before performing information fusion of the GNN and LLM outputs to predict material magnetic ordering in four classes (Non-magnetic, Ferromagnetic, Antiferromagnetic, Ferrimagnetic). The model is trained, validated, and tested on a dataset which is a combination of Materials Project dataset, Magndata dataset, and a set of uranium compounds with magnetic ordering determined in laboratory testing, with an 80/10/10 (training/validation/testing) data split. The proposed GNN and LLM multi-modal approach achieves better overall accuracy than both the standalone GNN and LLM approaches with three fusion strategies.

SU(2)-分子磁学的等变旋量消息传递#

原题：SU(2)-Equivariant Spinor Message Passing for Molecular Magnetism
作者：Erh-Wei Sheng、Beom Seok Kang、Yuchao Lin、Danish Khan、Anima Anandkumar。arXiv · physics，2026-10-22
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：预测分子磁性通常需要使用自旋轨道耦合 (SOC) 进行电子结构计算，而在许多分子中重复计算的成本很高。底层电子态可以转换为旋量，但等变代理通常仅使用整数秩特征。受到这些电子态的旋量性质的启发，我们开发了 eSpinor，一种传播旋量特征的 SU(2) 等变图神经网络。我们从近似 SOC 哈密顿量的特征向量初始化旋量特征，并使用规范协变消息传递和规范不变读数来进行独立于任意特征基选择的预测。对于钴单分子磁体候选物，eSpinor 相对于…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、equivariant graph neural network、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

预测分子磁性通常需要使用自旋轨道耦合 (SOC) 进行电子结构计算，而在许多分子中重复计算的成本很高。底层电子态可以转换为旋量，但等变代理通常仅使用整数秩特征。受到这些电子态的旋量性质的启发，我们开发了 eSpinor，一种传播旋量特征的 SU(2) 等变图神经网络。我们从近似 SOC 哈密顿量的特征向量初始化旋量特征，并使用规范协变消息传递和规范不变读数来进行独立于任意特征基选择的预测。对于钴单分子磁体候选物，eSpinor 相对于 NequIP 改进了零场分裂预测，包括对看不见的配体的预测。对于镝磁共振成像探针，尽管存在类似的静态晶体场误差和较少的参数，eSpinor 仍可在哈密顿量基线上改善真实的电子自旋相关性，同时准确预测水中和转移至甲醇后的低场核弛豫，而无需重新训练。总的来说，这些结果表明 SU(2) 等变旋量消息传递对于分子磁性来说是一种有用的归纳偏置。我们还发现，类似的静态张量误差可能会产生不同的动态误差，从而促使基于预期物理可观测值的准确性进行模型选择。

English Abstract

Predicting molecular magnetism often requires electronic-structure calculations with spin-orbit coupling (SOC), which are costly to repeat across many molecules. The underlying electronic states can transform as spinors, yet equivariant surrogates typically use only integer-rank features. Inspired by the spinor nature of these electronic states, we develop eSpinor, an SU(2)-equivariant graph neural network that propagates spinor features. We initialize spinor features from eigenvectors of an approximate SOC Hamiltonian and use gauge-covariant message passing with a gauge-invariant readout to make predictions independent of the arbitrary eigenbasis choice. On cobalt single-molecule magnet candidates, eSpinor improves zero-field-splitting predictions relative to NequIP, including for unseen ligands. For a dysprosium magnetic resonance imaging probe, eSpinor improves real electron-spin correlations over a Hamiltonian-informed baseline despite similar static crystal-field errors and fewer parameters, while accurately predicting low-field nuclear relaxation in water and after transfer to methanol without retraining. Overall, these results demonstrate that SU(2)-equivariant spinor message passing is a useful inductive bias for molecular magnetism. We also find that similar static tensor errors can yield different dynamical errors, motivating model selection based on accuracy for the intended physical observables.

神经网络损失景观中关键轨道的通用拓扑对称指数#

原题：A General Topological Symmetry Index for Critical Orbits In Neural Network Loss Landscapes
作者：Jingzhou Liu。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-22
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：由于其高维性，对神经网络损失景观的全局结构的系统理解仍然具有挑战性。网络架构和学习目标产生的对称性提供了一种研究该结构各个方面的方法。尽管越来越多的研究引起了人们对这些对称性的关注，但对它们以及它们产生的临界轨道的系统表征仍然不完整。为了解决这一差距，在这项工作中，我们基于等变度理论家族的方法建立了拓扑对称指数来解码这些对称性。该框架适用于任意网络架构，并包括适用于简并临界轨道的方法。该指数检测临界点的各向同性并量化相应的净拓扑贡献。提…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

由于其高维性，对神经网络损失景观的全局结构的系统理解仍然具有挑战性。网络架构和学习目标产生的对称性提供了一种研究该结构各个方面的方法。尽管越来越多的研究引起了人们对这些对称性的关注，但对它们以及它们产生的临界轨道的系统表征仍然不完整。为了解决这一差距，在这项工作中，我们基于等变度理论家族的方法建立了拓扑对称指数来解码这些对称性。该框架适用于任意网络架构，并包括适用于简并临界轨道的方法。该指数检测临界点的各向同性并量化相应的净拓扑贡献。提供了几个示例来说明计算，包括 softplus MLP、Transformers 和等变神经网络。

English Abstract

A systematic understanding of the global structure of neural-network loss landscapes remains challenging due to their high dimensionality. Symmetries arising from network architectures and learning objectives provide a way to investigate aspects of this structure. Although a growing number of studies have drawn attention to these symmetries, a systematic characterization of them, as well as of the critical orbits they give rise to, remains incomplete. To address this gap, in this work we establish a topological symmetry index, based on the methodologies from the family of equivariant degree theories, to decode these symmetries. The frameworks apply to arbitrary network architectures and include methods applicable to degenerate critical orbits. The index detects the isotropy of the critical points and quantifies the corresponding net topological contributions. Several examples, including softplus MLPs, Transformers, and equivariant neural networks, are presented to illustrate the computations.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经网络分数量子霍尔 (FQH) 态为捕获朗道级 (LL) 混合和理解 FQH 物理提供了一种有前途的替代方案，但迄今为止，物理先验的结合仅限于 ansatz 中内置的最小精确约束。在球面几何中，我们表明，当仅提供最小的附加物理先验时，以及当严格地施加诸如通量附加之类的先验时，网络的灵活性都会受到影响。在前一种情况下，单极子谐波积轨道（MHPO）模拟很难优化，而在后一种情况下，神经网络的表达能力受到限制。因此，我们提出了轨道对矩阵因子 MHPO (MHPO-M) ansatz，它不仅用可学习的对因子替换固定的 Laughlin 结构，而且使它们依赖于电子构型和轨道，从而平衡物理先验与灵活性。在 ν=1/5、1/3 和 2/5 范围内，MHPO-M 在测试的变体中实现了最低的变分能量，并且基于它的因式分解轨道读数可以扩展到 35 个电子，其能量与从 3-LL 精确对角化结果推断出的趋势具有竞争力。这些结果表明，可以在不牺牲神经网络波函数灵活性的情况下适当地合并物理先验，从而为具有 LL 混合的 FQH 系统的精确、可扩展建模开辟了一条有希望的道路。

English Abstract

Neural network fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states offer a promising alternative for capturing Landau level (LL) mixing and for understanding FQH physics, yet the incorporation of physical priors has so far been limited to the minimal exact constraints built into the ansatz. Working in spherical geometry, we show that the network's flexibility suffers both when only minimal additional physical priors are supplied and when priors such as flux attachment are imposed rigidly. In the former case, the monopole harmonic product orbital (MHPO) ansatz is difficult to optimize, whereas in the latter, the expressivity of the neural network is restricted. We therefore propose the orbital pair matrix factor MHPO (MHPO-M) ansatz, which balances physical priors against flexibility by not only replacing the fixed Laughlin structure with learnable pair factors but also making them dependent on both the electron configurations and the orbitals. Across ν=1/5, 1/3, and 2/5, MHPO-M achieves the lowest variational energy among the tested variants, and a factorized orbital readout based on it enables scaling to 35 electrons, with energies competitive with trends inferred from three-LL exact-diagonalization results. These results demonstrate that physical priors can be appropriately incorporated without sacrificing the flexibility of neural network wavefunctions, opening a promising path toward accurate, scalable modeling of FQH systems with LL mixing.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从磁性纹理预测响应张量是非共线磁体研究中的一个关键挑战。用于构造给定响应张量的描述符的现有方法仅限于一阶多极展开或仅限于简单的磁结构。在这项工作中，我们为任意晶体结构和磁性纹理构建了完整的对称不变描述符基础，为响应张量的机器学习建模提供了可解释的特征。我们修改标准主成分分析框架并导出层次稳定的不相关阶次参数：扩展阶次参数空间以包含更高阶的自旋阶次引入与先前的阶次参数不相关的额外阶次参数。我们将该框架应用于磁 kagome 晶格中反常霍尔电导率 (AHC) 的回归和分类。特别是，在 AHC 通过对称不变阶参数的线性回归很好地建模的情况下，我们发现平方回归系数（按其大小排序）服从类似 Zipf 的幂律：βᵣ² propto r⁻α。我们的方法提供了一种系统的途径来构造所有自旋顺序的可解释的顺序参数，这些参数将磁性晶体的对称性与其响应张量联系起来。

English Abstract

The prediction of response tensors from magnetic textures is a crucial challenge in the study of noncollinear magnets. Existing methods for constructing descriptors of a given response tensor are limited to first-order multipole expansions or restricted to simple magnetic structures. In this work, we construct a complete symmetry-invariant descriptor basis for arbitrary crystal structures and magnetic textures, providing interpretable features for machine learning modeling of response tensors. We modify the standard principal component analysis framework and derive uncorrelated order parameters that are hierarchically stable: extending the order parameter space to include higher-degree spin order introduces additional order parameters that are uncorrelated to the previous ones. We apply this framework to regression and classification of the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) in a magnetic kagome lattice. In particular, in the regime where AHC is well modeled by linear regression of the symmetry-invariant order parameters, we find that the squared regression coefficients, ranked by their magnitude, obey a Zipf-like power law: βᵣ² propto r⁻α. Our method provides a systematic route to construct interpretable order parameters of all spin orders that connect the symmetry of a magnetic crystal to its response tensors.

English Abstract

Accurate reconstruction of magnetic fields and their gradients is essential for assessing and controlling systematic effects in precision measurement experiments. We have developed a physics-informed neural network (PINN) method which reconstructs three-dimensional magnetic fields subject to the source-free Maxwell equations, nablacdotB=0 and nablatimesB=0. In an extension of our previous work on PINN methods for reconstruction of magnetic fields in inaccessible interior regions via measurements at exterior locations, we now demonstrate the feasibility of applying PINN methods to the extraction of magnetic field gradient terms from discrete measurement points subject to constraints from Maxwell's equations.

半导体量子阱中的电子晶体和准晶体：人工智能驱动的发现#

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：均质电子气是量子凝聚态物理学的基石，为发展密度泛函理论和理解半导体中的电子相奠定了基础。然而，对现实半导体系统中强相关电子的理论理解仍然有限。在这项工作中，我们开发了一种基于神经网络的变分方法来研究各种电子密度和阱厚度的三维几何中的量子阱。从第一原理开始，我们的无偏人工智能驱动方法揭示了具有单层和双层电荷分布的金属相和结晶相。在出现的双层中，我们发现了一种新的物质量子相：电子准晶体。

中文摘要

均质电子气是量子凝聚态物理学的基石，为发展密度泛函理论和理解半导体中的电子相奠定了基础。然而，对现实半导体系统中强相关电子的理论理解仍然有限。在这项工作中，我们开发了一种基于神经网络的变分方法来研究各种电子密度和阱厚度的三维几何中的量子阱。从第一原理开始，我们的无偏人工智能驱动方法揭示了具有单层和双层电荷分布的金属相和结晶相。在出现的双层中，我们发现了一种新的物质量子相：电子准晶体。

English Abstract

中文摘要

即使线性霍尔响应消失，贝里曲率偶极子也可以产生非线性霍尔电流，但仅对称性并不能决定其大小或磁可调谐性。在蜂窝模型中检查结构对称性、力矩方向和载流子占据的相互作用。曲率图和能带求和偶极子被积函数揭示了费米能级附近的速度和占据窗口如何选择符号贡献。消除三重旋转允许非磁性偶极子垂直于幸存的镜子。通用磁重定向激活第二个组件并通过正值和负值调整两个组件。全局反转保留了偶极子，同时反转了固有的线性霍尔电导率，从而允许在均匀场平均下来自同等数量的反转域的有限体偶极子贡献。对称约束多项式模型可以预测 256 个保留自旋配置的两个偶极子分量，在参考化学势下实现确定系数 R² > 0.9995。在固定载流子密度下，化学势的方向相关变化会修改响应，并为所选模型产生稍大的预测误差。在化学势扫描过程中，D_y 的预测精度下降得更厉害，揭示了替代物性能的能量依赖性。在具有恒定弛豫时间的半经典输运中，计算出的偶极子确定了二次电流及其整流和二次谐波分量的角度依赖性。这些结果提供了磁取向相关的贝里曲率偶极子的定量表示，并显示了载流子占据如何影响响应及其可预测性。

English Abstract

A Berry-curvature dipole can produce a nonlinear Hall current even when the linear Hall response vanishes, but symmetry alone does not determine its magnitude or magnetic tunability. The interplay of structural symmetry, moment orientation, and carrier occupation is examined in a honeycomb model. Curvature maps and the band-summed dipole integrand reveal how velocity and the occupation window near the Fermi level select signed contributions. Removing threefold rotation permits a nonmagnetic dipole perpendicular to the surviving mirror. Generic magnetic reorientation activates the second component and tunes both components through positive and negative values. Global reversal preserves the dipole while reversing the intrinsic linear Hall conductivity, allowing a finite bulk dipole contribution from equally populated reversed domains under uniform-field averaging.A symmetry-constrained polynomial model predicts both dipole components for 256 held-out spin configurations, achieving coefficients of determination R² > 0.9995 at the reference chemical potential. At fixed carrier density, orientation-dependent shifts of the chemical potential modify the response and yield slightly larger prediction errors for the chosen model. Across the chemical-potential sweep,the prediction accuracy decreases more strongly for D_y,revealing an energy dependence of the surrogate's performance. Within semiclassical transport with a constant relaxation time, the calculated dipole determines the angular dependence of the quadratic current and its rectified and second-harmonic components. These results provide a quantitative representation of magnetic-orientation-dependent Berry-curvature dipoles and show how carrier occupation influences both the response and its predictability.

中文摘要

数据驱动的材料发现插值比推断更可靠，并且很少揭示新的结构类型。我们提出了 MatEvolve，一个代理 AI 框架，它以可解释的主题语言设计晶体，其中每个晶体都被编写为一个 emphmotif 配置文件，描述了组成晶体的重复几何图案（emphmotif）。主题轮廓不仅可以作为材料的描述，而且可以作为材料设计的媒介：代理以规定的理由编辑轮廓，并根据修改后的轮廓构造出晶体；然后通过第一性原理计算来验证最有希望的候选者。 MatEvolve 建立在最先进的语言模型 Claude Fable~5 之上，无需进行微调。它应用于贫稀土永磁化合物的设计，并发现新颖的（亚）稳定晶体，这些晶体既是新的结构原型，又是磁体化合物；在相同的验证预算下，它的频率比生成模型高一个数量级。除了设计之外，分析所发现的晶体的人类可读的轮廓揭示了它们的结构-性质关系。

English Abstract

Data-driven materials discovery interpolates more reliably than it extrapolates and seldom reveals new structure types. We present MatEvolve, an agentic-AI framework that designs crystals in an interpretable emphlanguage of motifs, in which each crystal is written as a emphmotif profile that describes the recurring geometric patterns---the emphmotifs---composing the crystal. The motif profile serves not merely as a description of a material but as the medium for material design: the agent edits the profile with a stated rationale and constructs a crystal from the modified one; the most promising candidates are then validated by first-principles calculations. MatEvolve is built on the state-of-the-art language model Claude Fable~5 without fine-tuning. It is applied to the design of rare-earth-lean permanent-magnet compounds and finds novel (meta)stable crystals that are both new structural prototypes and magnet compounds; under an equal validation budget, it does so an order of magnitude more often than generative models. Beyond design, analyzing the human-readable profiles of the discovered crystals reveals their structure--property relationships.

原题：Phase transitions of the antiferromagnetic J₁-J₂-J₃ Ising model on the cubic lattice

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

受先前研究中关于立方晶格上 J₁ = J2 = J₃ = 1.0 的三维反铁磁 J₁-J2-J₃ Ising 模型相变性质的相互矛盾的结果的启发，在本研究中，我们使用蒙特卡洛模拟和神经网络 (NN) 对此模型进行了详细研究。其中 J₁ 和 J2 分别是层内最近邻耦合和次近邻耦合，J₃ 是最近邻层间相互作用。通过研究从比之前研究中使用的大得多的晶格获得的数据，我们发现 J₁ = J2 = J₃ = 1.0 的相变是二阶的，没有任何歧义。因此，我们对这场辩论提出了解决方案。除了J₁=J2=J₃=1.0之外，我们还研究了与J2/J₁=4/7和J₃=J₁=1.0相关的临界现象。此外，通过采用专门的训练和测试策略，我们的神经网络计算不仅比文献中常用的标准程序高效得多，而且当考虑大线性系统尺寸 L 时，仅需要传统神经网络计算所需存储容量的千分之一。这里采用的方法为应用神经网络技术来研究高维系统的关键行为提供了一种极其资源有效的途径。

English Abstract

Motivated by the conflicting findings in prior studies regarding the nature of the phase transition for the three-dimensional antiferromagnetic J₁-J₂-J₃ Ising model on the cubic lattice with J₁ = J₂ = J₃ = 1.0, in this study we conduct a detailed investigation of this model using both Monte Carlo simulations and neural networks (NN). Here J₁ and J₂ are the intralayer nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings, respectively, and J₃ is the nearest-neighbor interlayer interaction. By investigating the data obtained from much larger lattices than those used in previous studies, we find that the phase transition for J₁ = J₂ = J₃ = 1.0 is second order without any ambiguity. Hence we bring a resolution to this debate. Apart from J₁ = J₂ = J₃ = 1.0, we study the critical phenomenon associated with J₂/J₁ = 4/7 and J₃ = J₁ = 1.0 as well. In addition, by employing a dedicated training and testing strategy, our NN computation not only is much more efficient than those utilizing the standard procedures commonly used in the literature, but also requires only a per-mille fraction of the storage capacity needed by a conventional NN calculation when a large linear system size L is considered. The method employed here provides an extremely resource-efficient route for applying NN techniques to study the critical behaviors of higher-dimensional systems.

LatticeMind：密度泛函理论工作流程的验证门代理框架#

原题：LatticeMind: A Validation-Gated Agentic Framework for Density Functional Theory Workflows
作者：Nathaniel M. Vegh、Yu Zhu、Raphaël J. Prentki、Félix Beaudoin、Shuaishuai Yuan、Hong Guo。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-21

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：第一原理计算很少是单个求解器调用：它们是多阶段工作流程，其中结构、设置和保存的密度必须在相关阶段之间正确传递。语言模型可以编写有效的单个输入，但一旦阶段链接起来，可靠性就会崩溃：即使在求解器运行时，丢失的依赖项、错误的文件切换或幻觉的关键字也可能使结果无效，并且此类错误只有在昂贵的执行后才会出现。我们引入了 LatticeMind，这是一个验证门控代理框架，可将自然语言请求转换为可执行、可检查的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 项目。工作流合同…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、first-principles calculations。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

第一原理计算很少是单个求解器调用：它们是多阶段工作流程，其中结构、设置和保存的密度必须在相关阶段之间正确传递。语言模型可以编写有效的单个输入，但一旦阶段链接起来，可靠性就会崩溃：即使在求解器运行时，丢失的依赖项、错误的文件切换或幻觉的关键字也可能使结果无效，并且此类错误只有在昂贵的执行后才会出现。我们引入了 LatticeMind，这是一个验证门控代理框架，可将自然语言请求转换为可执行、可检查的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 项目。工作流合同指定所需的阶段、文件和依赖项；确定性验证器在启动任何求解器之前检查项目；失败的检查触发有限修复；报告仍然可以追溯到项目文件。在涵盖总能量、态密度、能带结构、磁性、声子工作流程的 44 例 RESCU 基准测试中，三个非旗舰 LLM 后端再现了每个参考值； 132 次运行中的 119 次通过了第一个验证计划，其余的则在一个修复周期后通过。 Ablations 将工作流契约和执行前验证确定为主要的可靠性机制；传统的试验和修复基线达到 71%，每次成功的代币成本是两倍。相同的架构重现了 19 个案例 VASP 基准上的每个参考值，展示了跨求解器的传输。除了这些任务系列之外，LatticeMind 还生成并验证了 Rh₆/InₓGa₁₋ₓN 光催化剂界面处 Rh 诱导电子响应的多阶段研究的 23 个输入。这些结果建立了工作流契约、确定性验证、有界修复和可追溯记录，作为可靠的人工智能辅助科学计算的实践基础，在执行之前捕获可预防的错误，并且求解器永远不是第一个验证器。

English Abstract

First-principles calculations are rarely single solver calls: they are multistage workflows in which structures, settings, and saved densities must pass correctly between dependent stages. Language models can write a valid single input, but reliability collapses once stages are chained: a dropped dependency, wrong file handoff, or hallucinated keyword can invalidate results even when the solver runs, and such errors surface only after costly execution. We introduce LatticeMind, a validation-gated agentic framework that converts natural-language requests into executable, checkable density functional theory (DFT) projects. A workflow contract specifies required stages, files, and dependencies; deterministic validators inspect the project before any solver launch; failed checks trigger bounded repair; and reports remain traceable to project files. On a 44-case RESCU benchmark spanning total-energy, density-of-states, band-structure, magnetic, phonon workflows, three non-flagship LLM backends reproduced every reference value; 119 of 132 runs passed on the first validated plan and the rest after one repair cycle. Ablations identify the workflow contract and pre-execution validation as the principal reliability mechanisms; a conventional trial-and-repair baseline reaches 71% at twice the token cost per success. The same architecture reproduces every reference value on a 19-case VASP benchmark, demonstrating transfer across solvers. Beyond these task families, LatticeMind generated and validated the 23 inputs of a multistage study of Rh-induced electronic responses at a Rh₆/InₓGa₁₋ₓN photocatalyst interface. These results establish workflow contracts, deterministic validation, bounded repair, and traceable records as a practical foundation for reliable AI-assisted scientific computing, where preventable errors are caught before execution and the solver is never the first validator.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：artificial neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

数值模拟量子多体系统是现代物理学的核心挑战之一，因为希尔伯特空间随系统尺寸呈指数增长，使得大规模精确解决方案变得棘手。在过去的十年中，生成人工神经网络已经成为强大的波函数分析，推动了寻找基态表示、从数据重建量子态以及量子多体系统动力学建模方面的进步。虽然循环神经网络 (RNN) 的计算效率很高，但循环性质会限制其捕获远程相关性的能力。具有底层自注意力机制的 Transformer 模型擅长捕获这些远程相关性，但会增加计算要求，从而限制了小型物理系统的应用。在这项工作中，我们通过探索作为波函数模拟的多层感知器（MLP）混合器模型来解决这些限制。通过计算轻量级混合策略，通过系统全局和本地混合信息，该架构有望将捕获远程相关性的能力与低计算成本结合起来。我们对模型进行基准测试，以寻找二维量子多体系统在物质各个相中的基态表示，并研究不同物理系统大小的表示准确性和计算效率的行为。通过将其性能与具有单站点和修补输入定义的 RNN 和 Transformer 模型进行比较，我们证明 MLP 混合器架构的性能与最先进的网络模型具有竞争力。我们进一步表明，该模型与变压器模型的分层组合优于大规模量子系统的其他最先进的架构，为研究物质的量子相开辟了新途径。

English Abstract

Numerically simulating quantum many-body systems is one of the central challenges in modern physics, as the exponential growth of the Hilbert space with system size renders large-scale exact solutions intractable. Over the past decade, generative artificial neural networks have emerged as powerful wave function ansätze that have powered advances in finding ground state representations, reconstructing quantum states from data, and modeling dynamics in quantum many-body systems. While recurrent neural networks (RNNs) are computationally efficient, the recurrent nature can limit their capacity to capture long-range correlations. Transformer models with their underlying self-attention mechanism excel at capturing these long-range correlations, but incur increased computational requirements, restricting applications to small physical systems. In this work, we address these limitations by exploring the multi-layer perceptron (MLP) mixer model as a wave function ansatz. By mixing information globally and locally through the system via a computationally lightweight mixing strategy, this architecture promises to combine the ability to capture long-range correlations with low computational costs. We benchmark the model on finding ground state representations of two-dimensional quantum many-body systems in various phases of matter and investigate the behavior of representational accuracy and computational efficiency for different physical system sizes. By comparing its performance to RNNs and transformer models with single-site and patched input definitions, we demonstrate that the MLP mixer architecture performs competitively with state-of-the-art network models. We further show that a hierarchical combination of this model with the transformer model outperforms other state-of-the-art architectures for large-scale quantum systems, opening new avenues for investigating quantum phases of matter.

磁波法布里-珀罗纳米谐振器中自旋波散射和增强的有限尺寸缩放#

原题：Finite-size scaling of spin-wave scattering and enhancement in magnonic Fabry-Pérot nanoresonators
作者：Anton Lutsenko、Sebastiaan van Dijken。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配3

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：Magnonic neural networks require compact resonant nodes that scatter spin waves efficiently, concentrate spin-wave energy and can be reconfigured. Hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonators, formed by a ferromagnetic-metal s…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Magnonic neural networks require compact resonant nodes that scatter spin waves efficiently, concentrate spin-wave energy and can be reconfigured. Hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonators, formed by a ferromagnetic-metal stripe dipolar-coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film, provide low-loss and programmable control of spin-wave transmission, but have so far been studied only for stripes that are long compared with the spin-wave wavelength. Here we use micromagnetic simulations to determine how the response of a 200-nm-wide CoFeB stripe on a 55-nm-thick YIG film evolves as the stripe length is reduced from infinity to 640 nm. An infinitely long stripe acts as a one-dimensional cavity that opens a transmission gap at the first-order resonance (1.70 GHz and 1.47 GHz for the parallel and antiparallel magnetic configuration) and enhances the out-of-plane precession amplitude in the YIG film by factors of about 5 and 12, without distorting the transmitted wavefront. For lengths of a few micrometers, backward-volume spin waves emitted from the stripe ends modulate the precession along the stripe and fragment the reflected and transmitted wavefronts. Below about 1.2 micrometer, the resonator scatters incident spin waves over the entire isofrequency contour of the film, with caustic beams that are most pronounced in the antiparallel configuration. In this regime the parallel configuration supports weakly enhanced quantized modes, whereas the antiparallel configuration supports a single mode with an out-of-plane enhancement of about 15. The width, thickness and shape of the stripe tune this mode by 50-100 MHz, and its strong enhancement lowers the onset of nonlinear frequency shift and fold-over to incident amplitudes of a few percent of the saturation magnetization.因此，亚微米磁波谐振器是用于磁波网络的有前途的可重构非线性节点。

English Abstract

Magnonic neural networks require compact resonant nodes that scatter spin waves efficiently, concentrate spin-wave energy and can be reconfigured. Hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonators, formed by a ferromagnetic-metal stripe dipolar-coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film, provide low-loss and programmable control of spin-wave transmission, but have so far been studied only for stripes that are long compared with the spin-wave wavelength. Here we use micromagnetic simulations to determine how the response of a 200-nm-wide CoFeB stripe on a 55-nm-thick YIG film evolves as the stripe length is reduced from infinity to 640 nm. An infinitely long stripe acts as a one-dimensional cavity that opens a transmission gap at the first-order resonance (1.70 GHz and 1.47 GHz for the parallel and antiparallel magnetic configuration) and enhances the out-of-plane precession amplitude in the YIG film by factors of about 5 and 12, without distorting the transmitted wavefront. For lengths of a few micrometers, backward-volume spin waves emitted from the stripe ends modulate the precession along the stripe and fragment the reflected and transmitted wavefronts. Below about 1.2 micrometer, the resonator scatters incident spin waves over the entire isofrequency contour of the film, with caustic beams that are most pronounced in the antiparallel configuration. In this regime the parallel configuration supports weakly enhanced quantized modes, whereas the antiparallel configuration supports a single mode with an out-of-plane enhancement of about 15. The width, thickness and shape of the stripe tune this mode by 50-100 MHz, and its strong enhancement lowers the onset of nonlinear frequency shift and fold-over to incident amplitudes of a few percent of the saturation magnetization. Sub-micrometer magnonic resonators are therefore promising reconfigurable nonlinear nodes for magnonic networks.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大型语言模型 (LLM) 越来越多地被集成到第一原理计算工作流程中，但人们对它们配置科学计算的能力仍然知之甚少。在这里，我们介绍 INCARBench，这是一个通过配置生成和修复任务评估维也纳从头算仿真包 (VASP) 输入配置的法学硕士的基准。对 19 种模型配置的评估揭示了当前前沿模型之间的巨大功能差异。虽然一些模型实现了较高的语义和策略准确性，但任务关键正确性仍然大大降低，这表明参数级正确性并不一定意味着科学有效的配置。失效分析表明，错误集中在涉及 DFT+U、磁性和相关材料的物理耦合设置中，其中必须同时满足多个约束。修复评估进一步表明，纠正不正确的设置和保留已经有效的配置是不同的功能，而配置保存仍然是一个主要挑战。这些发现将科学配置确立为大型语言模型的可测量能力，并为开发更可靠的计算材料科学人工智能系统奠定了基础。

English Abstract

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into first-principles computational workflows, yet their ability to configure scientific calculations remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce INCARBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on input configuration for the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) through both configuration generation and repair tasks. Evaluating 19 model configurations reveals substantial capability differences among current frontier models. While several models achieve high semantic and policy accuracy, task-critical correctness remains substantially lower, demonstrating that parameter-level correctness does not necessarily imply scientifically valid configurations. Failure analysis shows that errors concentrate in physically coupled settings involving DFT+U, magnetism, and correlated materials, where multiple constraints must be satisfied simultaneously. Repair evaluation further reveals that correcting incorrect settings and preserving already-valid configurations are distinct capabilities, with configuration preservation remaining a major challenge. These findings establish scientific configuration as a measurable capability of large language models and provide a foundation for developing more reliable AI systems for computational materials science.

Orbitol：威科夫轨道上的晶体生成#

原题：Orbitol: Crystal Generation on Wyckoff Orbits
作者：Ivan Žugec、Joshua Rosenthal、Ivor Lončarić、Jan-Willem van de Meent、Tin Hadži Veljković。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-22

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

生成模型通过学习成分和几何形状来提出晶体材料，但无条件采样无法直接控制空间群，这限制了压电等响应是否允许对称。我们推出了 Orbitol，一种基于 Crystalite 扩散变压器构建的晶体生成器，用威科夫轨道令牌取代了原子令牌。给定一个空间群和占据威科夫位置的集合，Orbitol 会生成每个轨道的原子类型和自由坐标以及兼容的晶格，因此每个有效结构在所请求的群的操作下都是不变的。在评估的设置下，它生成结构的速度比对称感知基线快两到三个数量级。在 MP-20 上，Orbitol 的尝试中有 13.8% 产生了稳定或亚稳定、独特且新颖的结构，这些结构在弛豫后保留了所需的空间群，而次优模型 SGFM 的这一比例为 10.8%。我们还引入了 SPARC，该分数将原始检测到的空间群的保留与保留组和训练分布之间的一致性结合起来。最后，我们使用 Orbitol 生成压电晶体类别的候选者。在内部筛选选择的结构中，89% 通过了我们的密度泛函理论检查，79% 也没有与具有相同空间群的材料项目或亚历山大条目相匹配。他们的平均品质因数是材料项目中计算的压电材料的九倍，并且 24% 的新候选者排名在该组的前 10% 之内。

English Abstract

Generative models propose crystalline materials by learning composition and geometry, but unconditional sampling gives no direct control over the space group, which constrains whether responses such as piezoelectricity are symmetry-allowed. We present Orbitol, a crystal generator built on the Crystalite diffusion Transformer that replaces atom tokens with Wyckoff-orbit tokens. Given a space group and a collection of occupied Wyckoff positions, Orbitol generates the atom type and free coordinates of each orbit together with a compatible lattice, so every valid structure is invariant under the operations of the requested group. It generates structures two to three orders of magnitude faster than the symmetry-aware baselines at the evaluated settings. On MP-20, 13.8% of Orbitol's attempts yielded stable or metastable, unique and novel structures that retained the requested space group after relaxation, compared with 10.8% for SGFM, the next best model. We also introduce SPARC, a score that combines retention of the raw detected space group with agreement between the retained-group and training distributions. Finally, we used Orbitol to generate candidates in piezoelectric crystal classes. Of the structures selected by internal screening, 89% passed our density functional theory checks, and 79% also matched no Materials Project or Alexandria entry with the same space group. Their median figure of merit was nine times that of the piezoelectric materials computed in the Materials Project, and 24% of the new candidates ranked in that set's top 10%.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

噪声监督如何改变神经网络学习的表示？训练精度衡量与训练标签的一致性，但提供的有关表示几何的信息很少。我们使用持久同源性来跟踪神经激活中跨距离阈值的循环结构。实验涵盖图像分类、文本分类和模运算。在固定的 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 训练探针上，大小相同的干净标签和损坏标签子集在第 10 轮时没有显示出可检测到的持久性差异，而损坏标签子集在第 50 轮时具有更高的持久性。即使在该检查点只有 7-11% 的损坏探针示例的预测与指定的噪声标签相匹配，对比度仍然存在于统一规模的控制中。归一化保留了 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上干净和噪声训练轨迹之间的交叉，而 CIFAR-100/ResNet-18 在五种评估结构中的任何一种下都没有显示交叉。在五个管道的单独噪声率校准比较中，平均激活范数实现了比原始持久性更高的平均样本内 R2（0.936 与 0.908）。这些发现表明，即使与损坏标签的一致性较低，嘈杂的监督也会留下可测量的几何特征。将尺度控制下的持久性与标签拟合测量进行比较提供了一种方法来研究表示如何响应训练准确性所揭示的标签损坏。

English Abstract

How does noisy supervision change the representations learned by a neural network? Training accuracy measures agreement with training labels but provides little information about representation geometry. We use persistent homology to track loop structure across distance thresholds in neural activations. Experiments span image classification, text classification, and modular arithmetic. On a fixed CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 training probe, equally sized clean-label and corrupted-label subsets show no detectable persistence difference at epoch 10, whereas corrupted-label subsets have higher persistence at epoch 50. The contrast survives uniform-scale controls, even though predictions match the assigned noisy labels for only 7-11% of corrupted probe examples at that checkpoint. Normalisation preserves the crossover between clean and noisy training trajectories on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18, whereas CIFAR-100/ResNet-18 shows no crossover under any of the five evaluated constructions. In a separate noise-rate calibration comparison across five pipelines, mean activation norm achieves a higher mean in-sample R2 than raw persistence (0.936 versus 0.908). These findings show that noisy supervision leaves a measurable geometric signature even when agreement with corrupted labels is low. Comparing persistence under scale controls with label-fitting measurements offers a way to study how representations respond to label corruption beyond what training accuracy reveals.

中文摘要

准确有效地预测晶格热导率 (LTC) 对于开发热电、微电子和光伏应用材料至关重要。在这里，我们提出了一种在具有共同晶体对称性的化合物的大型数据集中高通量计算 LTC 的方法。我们的方法将 MatterSim 1M 通用机器学习原子间势的微调与基于玻尔兹曼输运方程解的 LTC 计算相结合。我们引入了一种基于低秩自适应的基于对称性的小样本微调策略（SBFS-FT）。在这种方法中，MatterSim 1M 对一小部分精心挑选的具有共同晶体对称性的化合物进行微调。由此产生的电势可用于具有这种晶体对称性的化合物的精确 LTC 计算，即使它们不包含在微调集中。我们在 half-Heusler 化合物（空间群 216）、空间群 225 材料和尖晶石（空间群 227）的数据集上测试了我们的方法。我们证明，使用 SBFS-FT 微调的 MatterSim 1M 进行的 LTC 计算与参考结果（使用矩张量势和 PolyMLP 获得）的平均绝对相对差异比使用非微调 MatterSim 1M 势实现的小 2-3 倍。此外，SBFS-FT 后使用 MatterSim 1M 进行 LTC 计算的精度与使用针对每种化合物单独微调的 MatterSim 1M 电位所获得的精度相当。重要的是，SBFS-FT 能够生成适合跨大型化合物数据集进行精确 LTC 计算的电势，其速度比单独对每种化合物微调 MatterSim 1M 的速度要快得多，对于包含 100 种化合物的数据集，计算时间减少了大约 5-7 倍。

English Abstract

Accurate and efficient prediction of lattice thermal conductivity (LTC) is essential for developing materials for thermoelectric, microelectronic, and photovoltaic applications. Here, we present a methodology for high-throughput calculation of LTC across large datasets of compounds sharing a common crystal symmetry. Our approach combines fine-tuning of the MatterSim 1M universal machine-learned interatomic potential with LTC calculations based on the solution of the Boltzmann transport equation. We introduce a symmetry-based few-shot fine-tuning strategy (SBFS-FT) based on low-rank adaptation. In this approach, MatterSim 1M is fine-tuned on a small, carefully selected set of compounds that share a common crystal symmetry. The resulting potential is then applicable for accurate LTC calculations for compounds with this crystal symmetry, even if they were not included in the fine-tuning set. We tested our methodology on datasets of half-Heusler compounds (space group 216), materials with space group 225, and spinels (space group 227). We demonstrated that LTC calculations with MatterSim 1M fine-tuned using SBFS-FT exhibited a mean absolute relative difference from the reference results (obtained using moment tensor potentials and polyMLPs) 2-3 times smaller than that achieved with the non-fine-tuned MatterSim 1M potential. Moreover, the accuracy of LTC calculations with MatterSim 1M after SBFS-FT was comparable to that achieved using the MatterSim 1M potentials individually fine-tuned for each compound. Importantly, SBFS-FT enabled generation of the potential suitable for accurate LTC calculations across large compound datasets substantially faster than fine-tuning of MatterSim 1M for each compound individually, reducing the computational time by approximately a factor of 5-7 for a dataset of 100 compounds.

通过具有辅助谱函数的去噪扩散模型加速 CTQMC#

原题：Acceleration of CTQMC via Denoising Diffusion Models with Auxiliary Spectral Functions

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

连续时间量子蒙特卡罗 (CTQMC) 杂质求解器是求解动态平均场理论 (DMFT) 方程的数值精确但昂贵的选择，欠采样产生的统计噪声给低温研究带来了重大挑战。这种噪声在采样的自能中尤其严重，在插入戴森方程时可能会破坏自洽环的稳定性。在这项工作中，我们基于在合成数据上训练的扩散模型架构，引入了费米子虚数时间格林函数的神经降噪器。该网络预测与噪声虚轴 CTQMC 数据一致的正和归一化辅助谱函数，然后通过自微分进行细化，以匹配测量的自能、前两个高频矩以及类似于约束残差最小化 (CRM) 方法的杂质占据。这导致了格林函数和自能是设计上的因果关系。通过这个去噪框架，我们能够将所需的 CTQMC 测量步骤减少 10-100 倍。我们使用 Bethe 晶格上的半填充 Hubbard 模型、二维 Hubbard 模型的两面片动态聚类逼近 (DCA) 以及 SrVO₃ 系统对我们的方法进行基准测试。我们通过仅 5000 个 CTQMC 测量的半填充 Hubbard 模型再现了增加库仑相互作用的莫特跃迁，仅用 5×10⁵ 测量的 DCA 二维 Hubbard 模型的赝能隙跃迁，并以比参考少 50 倍的测量来匹配 SrVO₃ 的谱函数。由于我们生成辅助谱函数，因此我们可以免费恢复系统的近似谱函数，而无需执行解析延拓。

English Abstract

Continuous-time Quantum Monte Carlo (CTQMC) impurity solvers are a numerically exact, but expensive, option for solving the Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) equations, with statistical noise from undersampling presenting a major challenge to low temperature studies. This noise is especially egregious within the sampled self-energy, which can destabilize the self-consistency loop upon insertion into the Dyson equation. In this work, we introduce a neural denoiser for fermionic imaginary time Green's functions based on the diffusion model architecture trained on synthetic data. The network predicts positive and normalized auxiliary spectral functions consistent with the noisy imaginary axis CTQMC data, which is then refined via autodifferentiation to match the measured self-energy, its first two high-frequency moments, and impurity occupation similar to the Constrained Residual Minimization (CRM) method. This leads to Green's functions and self-energies that are causal by design. Through this denoising framework we are able to reduce the required CTQMC measurement steps by a factor of 10-100. We benchmark our method with a half-filled Hubbard model on a Bethe lattice, a two-patch Dynamical Cluster Approximation (DCA) of the two-dimensional Hubbard model, and the SrVO₃ system. We reproduce the Mott transition upon increasing Coulomb interaction for the half-filled Hubbard model with only 5000 CTQMC measurements, the pseudogap transition for the DCA two-dimensional Hubbard model with only 5×10⁵ measurements, and match the spectral function of SrVO₃ with 50 times fewer measurements than the reference. Since we generate auxiliary spectral functions, we recover the approximate spectral functions of the systems for free without performing analytic continuation.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

近年来，分子约束水的流动引起了人们极大的兴趣。然而，尽管其精致的特性，当前的纳米流体装置无法探测超出线性响应范围的分子水流，而基于纳米机械力的方法产生了明显不一致的结果。在这里，我们使用基于音叉的原子力显微镜，通过测量各种二维材料界面处分子约束水的完整线性到非线性、剪切相关的粘弹性流变学来解决这些限制——证明了水流响应中明显的速率效应。观察到的流变行为与宏观软玻璃状屈服材料的流变行为非常相似，并由非线性模型完全捕获，该模型解释了剪切引起的动力学加速——这是无序过冷和亚稳态系统的标志。我们进一步揭示了纳米约束水动力学中由压力引起的明显减慢，弛豫时间延伸至微秒范围并具有明显的材料依赖性。这些结果揭示了非线性、玻璃状流变学作为界面水的一般特征，并为可调、非线性纳米流体元件提供了一条途径。

English Abstract

The flow of molecularly confined water has attracted considerable interest in recent years. Yet despite their exquisite character, current nanofluidic devices cannot probe molecular water flow beyond the linear-response regime, while nanomechanical force- based approaches have produced markedly inconsistent results. Here, using tuning- fork-based atomic force microscopy, we tackle these limitations by measuring the full linear-to-nonlinear, shear-dependent viscoelastic rheology of molecularly confined water at various two-dimensional material interfaces -- demonstrating pronounced rate effects in water flow response. The observed rheological behaviors are remarkably analogous to that of macroscopic soft glassy yielding materials and fully captured by a non-linear model accounting for the shear-induced acceleration of the dynamics -- a hallmark of disordered supercooled and metastable systems. We further unveil a pronounced pressure-induced slowdown in nanoconfined water dynamics, with relaxation times extending into the microsecond range and marked material dependence. These results reveal non-linear, glassy rheology as a general feature of interfacial water and offer a route to tunable, non-linear nanofluidic elements.

中文摘要

蛋白质分类器对评估协议高度敏感：随机训练/测试分裂会导致系统发育泄漏，使模型能够利用分类单元特定的相关性并大大高估分布外的性能。这个问题对于按离子通道目标（Ca2+、K+、Na+）进行毒液化合物分类尤其严重，其中报告的 80-90% 的准确度并不反映对看不见的类群的概括。我们将跨物种预测制定为一种结构化的分布变化，其中分类学身份充当混杂因素，需要捕获跨系统发育的结构特征不变的表示。使用将 ESM-2 序列嵌入与二硫键感知图注意网络融合的共享多模态主干，我们在留一分类单元评估协议下，以跨五个类群的 1,373 种 ColabFold 预测的毒液化合物为基准，系统地比较了五种结构归纳偏差（光谱、黎曼、SE(3) 不变、统计流形和持久同源表示）。持久同源表示通过对局部结构扰动具有鲁棒性的拓扑描述符总结了主干几何结构，为系统发育不变学习提供了归纳偏差。在传统的 i.i.d. 下分裂，所有模型都达到相似的精度（81-86%），掩盖了归纳偏差的差异。在分类转变下，这种奇偶性崩溃了：仅序列基线下降到大约 30%，剩余的几何先验聚类在 54-55%，而我们的持久同源模型 (PH-GNN) 达到 63.4%，比次优方法高出 8 个百分点以上。这些结果表明，随机分割评估掩盖了表示质量的有意义的差异，并将拓扑表示确定为结构化生物分布转移下学习的有效归纳偏差。

English Abstract

Protein classifiers are highly sensitive to evaluation protocol: random train/test splits induce phylogenetic leakage, allowing models to exploit taxon-specific correlations and substantially overestimate out-of-distribution performance. This issue is particularly acute for venom compound classification by ion-channel target (Ca2+, K+, Na+), where reported accuracies of 80-90% do not reflect generalization to unseen taxa. We formulate cross-species prediction as a structured distribution shift in which taxonomic identity acts as a confounder, requiring representations that capture structural features invariant across phylogeny. Using a shared multimodal backbone that fuses ESM-2 sequence embeddings with a disulfide-aware graph attention network, we systematically compare five structural inductive biases (spectral, Riemannian, SE(3)-invariant, statistical-manifold, and persistent-homology representations) under a leave-one-taxon-out evaluation protocol on a benchmark of 1,373 ColabFold-predicted venom compounds spanning five taxa. The persistent-homology representation summarizes backbone geometry through topological descriptors that are robust to local structural perturbations, providing an inductive bias toward phylogenetically invariant learning. Under conventional i.i.d. splits, all models achieve similar accuracy (81-86%), masking differences in inductive bias. Under taxonomic shift, this parity collapses: a sequence-only baseline drops to approximately 30%, the remaining geometric priors cluster at 54-55%, while our persistent-homology model (PH-GNN) achieves 63.4%, outperforming the next-best method by over 8 percentage points. These results demonstrate that random-split evaluation obscures meaningful differences in representation quality and identify topological representations as an effective inductive bias for learning under structured biological distribution shift.

阅读原文 ↗

English Abstract

Physics-Informed Neural Networks present a novel approach in SciML that integrates physical laws in the form of partial differential equations directly into the NN through soft constraints in the loss function. This work studies the application of PINNs to solve a one dimensional coupled electro-elastodynamic system modeling linear piezoelectricity in stress-charge form, governed by elastodynamic and electrodynamic equations. Our simulation employs a feedforward architecture, mapping space-time coordinates to mechanical displacement and electric potential. Our PINN model achieved global relative L2 errors of 2.34 and 4.87 percent for displacement and electric potential respectively. The results validate PINNs as effective mesh free solvers for coupled time-dependent PDE systems, though challenges remain regarding error accumulation and stiffness in coupled eigenvalue systems.

Closing the Loop in Epitaxy with Machine Learning: Joint Optimization of Growth and Geometry in On-Chip Lasers#

作者：Mihir R. Athavale、Stephen A. Church、Wei Wen Wong、Andre KY Low、Hark Hoe Tan、Kedar Hippalgaonkar 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-22
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Achieving device-to-device reproducibility is a critical bottleneck for scalable photonic integrated circuits, as subtle variations in bottom-up epitaxial growth and fabrication severely limit yield. We present a machine…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Achieving device-to-device reproducibility is a critical bottleneck for scalable photonic integrated circuits, as subtle variations in bottom-up epitaxial growth and fabrication severely limit yield. We present a machine learning workflow for III-V multi-quantum well microring lasers that first optimizes growth and geometry parameters via multi-objective Bayesian optimization, then leverages variational autoencoders (VAEs) to attribute residual device-to-device variability to its underlying sources. By explicitly targeting threshold variance alongside absolute performance, we demonstrate 100% lasing yield across all designs. The optimized multi-quantum well microring laser fields achieved a median lasing threshold of 16~muJ,cm⁻²,pulse⁻¹, a 73% reduction in threshold variance relative to the previously reported best values, and a median emission wavelength of 1333~nm, in the telecommunications O-band. Furthermore, to diagnose residual performance dispersion under nominally identical conditions, VAEs were used to isolate the key components of device morphology that impact performance. This analysis successfully decoupled geometric from material disorder, quantitatively linking previously unmeasured morphological variations to population-level threshold fluctuations. This data-driven workflow bridges the gap between fundamental epitaxy and reliable manufacturing, establishing a generalizable blueprint for designing and yield-optimizing complex, non-linear optoelectronic devices.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

本文描述了一种基于 SuperDARN 和 SECIRA 雷达数据和空间天气参数构建高纬十米电离层不规则现象的临近预报和预报预测模型的方法。该方法依赖于影响不规则形成的空间天气参数动态的自学习模式识别。所提出的方法是一种数据驱动的多尺度方法，其中受图神经网络的启发，使用原始架构的神经网络来近似不规则性的空间分布。该模型使用 2010-2025 年期间 11 个雷达的数据进行训练和测试（SuperDARN：CVW、ICE、ICW、INV、PGR、PYK、RKN、SAS、STO；SECIRA：EKB、MAGW）。结果表明，当使用地磁坐标MLT、MLAT时，模型达到最佳性能。在研究的参数组中，最佳临近预报和预报的最佳参数组是来自地面观测的地磁和太阳扰动指数（aₚ、Kₚ、AE、F10.7）以及卫星测量的太阳风和行星际磁场参数（Pₛw、Eₛw、widetildeBz、Tₛw）的组合。分析中使用了所有参数的每小时平均值。结果表明，在长达 7-9 小时的提前时间内即可实现可接受的预报质量，其中临近预报的质量达到最高（零提前时间）。结果表明，参数 WidetildeBz 和 F10.7 仅对临近预报有意义，对于其他提前期可以忽略不计。结果表明，AE 和 Tₛw 对于中期预测（文本提前 2 小时）显着，而对于短期预测可以忽略不计。还讨论了不规则现象赤道边界最大纬度与空间天气参数之间的统计关系。

English Abstract

This paper describes an approach to constructing nowcast and forecast predictive models for the occurrence of high-latitude decameter ionospheric irregularities based on SuperDARN and SECIRA radar data and space weather parameters. The method relies on self-learning pattern recognition in the dynamics of space weather parameters, that influence the irregularities formation. The proposed approach is a data-driven, multiscale method in which the spatial distribution of irregularities is approximated using a neural network of an original architecture, inspired by graph neural networks. The model was trained and tested using data from 11 radars during the period 2010-2025 (SuperDARN: CVW, ICE, ICW, INV, PGR, PYK, RKN, SAS, STO; SECIRA: EKB, MAGW). It is shown that the model achieves optimal performance when using geomagnetic coordinates MLT,MLAT. The optimal set of parameters for the best nowcast and forecast over studied set of parameters is a combination of geomagnetic and solar disturbance indices (aₚ, Kₚ, AE, F10.7) from ground-based observations, and satellite-measured solar wind and interplanetary magnetic field parameters (Pₛw, Eₛw, widetildeBz, Tₛw). Hourly mean values for all the parameters were used in the analysis. The results indicate that acceptable forecast quality is achieved with a lead time of up to 7-9 hours, with the highest quality reached for nowcast (zero lead time). It is shown that the parameters widetildeBz and F10.7 are significant for nowcasting only and can be neglected for other lead times. It is shown that AE and Tₛw are significant for mid-term forecast (textgreater 2hours ahead) and can be neglected for short-term predictions. Statistical relationships between the maximum latitude of the equatorial boundary of the irregularities and space weather parameters are also discussed.

Stabilizing Filters for Graph Signal Generation#

作者：Martin Schmidt、Gonzalo Mateos。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-10-21

📄 摘要：Generating graph signals is an important task in machine learning, and incorporating the underlying graph into a generative model enables structurally-informed distribution modeling. However, said graph is often noisy or…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Generating graph signals is an important task in machine learning, and incorporating the underlying graph into a generative model enables structurally-informed distribution modeling. However, said graph is often noisy or imperfectly estimated, and structural errors can affect the generative process and the resulting signal distribution. While recent work has established stability guarantees for graph neural ordinary differential equations, the robustness of graph-aware reference dynamics, as used in topological Schrödinger bridge models, remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate the structural stability of continuous-time generative models whose dynamics combine a graph filter with a learned graph neural network. We first derive a Wasserstein stability bound that quantifies the effect of graph perturbations on the generated signal distributions. Informed by this analysis, we then propose a principled framework for designing graph filters that balance structural stability with induced signal smoothing. We characterize the optimal filter design and provide an explicit family of polynomial filters with provable stability guarantees. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world fMRI signals support our theoretical findings, illustrating the enhanced structural stability of the proposed filters while preserving generative performance.

中文摘要

我们提出了一种基于变压器的变分自回归网络，用于对具有连续自由度的晶格系统进行采样，并将其应用于 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 转变附近的二维 XY 模型。该架构与物理信息成分相结合：局部信息注意力和近似概率。由此产生的采样器明显优于现有的神经采样器。我们在高达 96×96 的晶格上训练模型，使用模式下降估计器验证配置空间的任何部分都不会被遗漏，并重现使用传统马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗算法获得的能量、磁化强度和螺旋模量。

English Abstract

We present a transformer-based variational autoregressive network for sampling lattice systems with continuous degrees of freedom and apply it to the two-dimensional XY model near the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition. The architecture is combined with physics-informed ingredients: locality-informed attention and approximate probabilities. The resulting sampler significantly outperforms existing neural samplers. We train the model on lattices up to 96×96, verify with a mode-dropping estimator that no parts of the configuration space are missed, and reproduce the energy, magnetization and helicity modulus obtained with the traditional Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一个与 Ising 兼容的框架，用于在有界输入扰动下进行形式神经网络鲁棒性验证。对于分段激活（连续分段线性和离散分段常数），精确对数分段模型 (Log-PW) 提供了具有状态最优对数编码的精确、可靠且完整的公式，通过对数 SOS2 (Log-PWL) 和SOS1 (Log-PWC) 约束。对于一般的逐元素激活，渐近阶梯包络模型 (Step-Env) 利用 Log-PWC 来构造合理的、可细化的端点包络。基于乔丹分解的精确单调约简进一步将该框架扩展到任意非单调激活。我们进一步开发了一种具有输出敏感迭代边界的混合 Benders 求解器，利用基于 Helly 的冲突压缩理论来避免通用指数切割爆炸。特别是，对于具有固定输入维度的浅层网络，这在网络大小上建立了无条件多项式 Benders 迭代复杂度。间隔剪枝、剪枝网络的证书传输以及分层经典伊辛分区进一步降低了旋转要求。实验证明了分段线性网络的精确认证保真度和紧凑自旋预算下非线性激活的接近参考精度。在 MNIST 基准测试中，Log-PW 将 One-Hot 公式的二元决策旋转减半，而基于 Helly 的削减与标准基线相比实现了 13.41 倍的加速和主查询减少了 87.6%。

English Abstract

We present an Ising-compatible framework for formal neural-network robustness verification under bounded input perturbations. For piecewise activations (both continuous piecewise-linear and discrete piecewise-constant), the Exact Logarithmic Piecewise Model (Log-PW) provides an exact, sound, and complete formulation with a state-optimal logarithmic encoding, reducing the binary variables per neuron from linear O(κ) to information-theoretically minimal logarithmic complexity O(lceil log₂ κ rceil) via logarithmic SOS2 (Log-PWL) and SOS1 (Log-PWC) constraints. For general element-wise activations, the Asymptotic Step-Envelope Model (Step-Env) leverages Log-PWC to construct sound, refinable endpoint enclosures. An exact monotonic reduction based on Jordan decomposition further extends this framework to arbitrary non-monotonic activations. We further develop a hybrid Benders solver with output-sensitive iteration bounds, leveraging a Helly-based conflict compression theory to avoid generic exponential cut explosion. In particular, for shallow networks with fixed input dimension, this establishes an unconditionally polynomial Benders iteration complexity in network size. Interval pruning, certificate transfer for pruned networks, and layerwise classical--Ising partitioning further reduce spin requirements. Experiments demonstrate exact certification fidelity for piecewise-linear networks and near-reference accuracy for non-linear activations under compact spin budgets. On MNIST benchmarks, Log-PW halves binary decision spins over One-Hot formulations, while Helly-based cuts achieve up to a 13.41× speedup and an 87.6% reduction in master queries over standard baselines.

Physics-constrained graph closures and working-point conditioning for a fast surrogate of linac beam dynamics#

作者：Bowen Zhang、Dazhang Huang、Nengyuan Zhang、Duan Gu、Chao Feng。arXiv · physics，2026-10-21
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配5

📄 摘要：When a graph neural network estimates a physical quantity, its frame, edge direction, aggregation and feature normalization decide which laws it can break. Wrong choices leave systematic errors the loss never registers.…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

When a graph neural network estimates a physical quantity, its frame, edge direction, aggregation and feature normalization decide which laws it can break. Wrong choices leave systematic errors the loss never registers. We build a linac beam-dynamics surrogate on this observation. Space charge, short-range wakefields and coherent synchrotron radiation enter as bounded closures on analytic kernels, on graphs whose frame and direction follow the interaction and whose symmetry makes the net space-charge self-force vanish for any weights. Verified without training, with randomized weights, the invariants break by five to eight orders of magnitude once the responsible feature is inverted. Per-working-point models constrained by the rms envelope equation are distilled into one model conditioned on 15 machine settings. On the 18.4~m L0+L1 section of the Shanghai soft x-ray free-electron laser linac, this model predicts the transverse beam envelope at 50 unseen settings to about 7%, with the centroid trajectory and emittance, in 0.38~s on a single Quadro RTX 6000 GPU. Training is instead dominated by a normalization choice, a data-derived output range for the beam size: at all 16 working points it lowers the starting error 2.2- to 8.5-fold and fixes the convergence speed. The remaining accuracy differences between hybrid and data-driven architectures vary with the working point and with the density of observation. The held-out error scales roughly as N⁻⁰.⁶ with the corpus size N. Whether the physics pays off where collective effects are strong, as in a magnetic bunch compressor, is in the next phase of our study.

Learning without relearning physical symmetry: an SO(3)-equivariant convolutional network (CNN) on the sphere#

作者：Joshua Williamson、Niall Jeffrey、Lorne Whiteway。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-10-19
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Many of the most important datasets in the physical sciences live natively on the sphere, and these data often carry no preferred position or orientation. Using methods that do not reflect this symmetry leads to far high…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Many of the most important datasets in the physical sciences live natively on the sphere, and these data often carry no preferred position or orientation. Using methods that do not reflect this symmetry leads to far higher simulation requirements, which can be catastrophic for scientific analyses (forcing reliance on cheap, imperfect models that bias results). We present a novel spherical implementation of a gauge equivariant mesh architecture that is specifically designed for the Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization (healpix{}) of the sphere. The SO(3)-equivariant convolution is built from steerable filters, parallel transport and spin-weighted features. The architecture supplies the exact symmetry, so the network does not spend training data learning it. The convolution operations are constructed to give provably exact equivariance, given a band-limit. Our full CNNs can achieve SO(3)-invariance error at per-cent-level and lower, which is controlled through well-understood hyperparameters. On benchmarks, our technique gives the most accurate dense gradient regression and competitive cosmic microwave background foreground removal and denoising. The improved simulation efficiency matters most in simulation-based inference (SBI), where high-fidelity simulations are expensive and neural compressors require large training sets, so a fixed budget forces analyses with less accurate simulators. In cosmology lensing SBI, our technique yields the tightest posteriors at matched simulation count. This approach is a novel method that will enable new convolutional neural network analyses, including generative and inference tasks, for processes and data on the sphere.

Cross-Area Transfer Learning for VLC-based Indoor Localization with a Transfer Efficiency Score#

作者：Masood Jan、Wafa Njima、Xun Zhang、Alexander Artemenko。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-22
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Indoor localization systems are critical for Industry-4.0 applications. Visible Light Communication (VLC)-based systems offer advantages such as immunity to electromagnetic interference, high accuracy, and energy efficie…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Indoor localization systems are critical for Industry-4.0 applications. Visible Light Communication (VLC)-based systems offer advantages such as immunity to electromagnetic interference, high accuracy, and energy efficiency, but their performance is hindered by environmental variability across deployment areas. This paper presents a transfer learning (TL) framework for VLC-based indoor localization that enhances cross-area robustness while reducing data requirements. Using real-world measurements from four distinct areas of a Bosch manufacturing facility, we show that fine-tuning a deep neural network (DNN) pre-trained on source area data with only 40% of target-area measurements recovers 97% of full-data performance, thereby reducing the amount of target data needed. We further introduce the Transfer Efficiency Score (TES), a composite metric that identifies the most effective source models for transfer without exhaustive evaluation. Guided by TES, the framework selects the best source model, yielding localization errors as low as 40.9 cm with success rates above 86%. Validation on an unseen area confirms its generalization capability, with a 3.28 cm mean error achieved using the TES-selected source model. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and data-efficient pathway for industrial VLC localization, substantially reducing deployment costs and computational burden while maintaining high accuracy in diverse and dynamic environments.

GPU-Accelerated Construction of the Gabriel Graph: Parallelization Strategies and Applications to Large-Margin Classification and Graph-Based Clustering#

作者：Diego Sanches Nere dos Santos、Luiz Carlos Bambirra Torres。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-22
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：The Gabriel graph is a proximity graph widely used in computational geometry and geometric machine learning, underlying large-margin classifiers and, as we show in this work, density-aware clustering methods. Its constru…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The Gabriel graph is a proximity graph widely used in computational geometry and geometric machine learning, underlying large-margin classifiers and, as we show in this work, density-aware clustering methods. Its construction, however, has cubic time complexity O(N³) in the naive formulation, which severely limits its use on datasets beyond a few thousand samples. This paper presents a set of GPU parallelization strategies, implemented in CUDA, that make Gabriel graph construction practical at scale. We introduce (i) an implicit closed-form mapping from a linear thread index to the corresponding vertex pair (i,j), which removes the need for an explicit O(N²) adjacency structure and lets every thread locate its pair in constant time and constant memory; (ii) a two-pass, bitmask-based, atomically-compacted edge-list construction that produces a dense edge array proportional only to the number of edges actually found; and (iii) a thread-coarsening strategy in which each thread evaluates several candidate pairs, trading launch and scheduling overhead against load balance. We evaluate these strategies on the acceleration of a Gabriel-graph-based large-margin classifier (NN-clas) and on a novel Gabriel-graph clustering algorithm that identifies connected components on a residual graph and performs GPU-parallel Union-Find with hierarchical edge-based merging. On 14 public benchmark datasets, the GPU implementation reduces Gabriel graph construction time by two to nearly four orders of magnitude relative to a sequential CPU baseline (up to 8484x), without loss of classification accuracy, and the proposed clustering method is statistically competitive with DBSCAN on the topological validity index Dunn while eliminating the need for an explicit density radius parameter.

Spontaneous brain-wide rotational dynamics constrain cue-evoked behavior#

作者：Sihan Chen、Yunlong Xu、Doruk Efe Gökmen、Michel Fruchart、Brent Doiron、Vincenzo Vitelli。arXiv · physics，2026-10-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Uncovering low-dimensional representations of brain-wide neural dynamics and their relationship to behavior remains a central challenge. Here, we show that spontaneous brain-wide rotational dynamics constrain cue-evoked…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Uncovering low-dimensional representations of brain-wide neural dynamics and their relationship to behavior remains a central challenge. Here, we show that spontaneous brain-wide rotational dynamics constrain cue-evoked behavior and learning. To systematically establish these findings, we developed a dimensionality-reduction technique called Rotational Component Analysis (RCA) that optimizes the detection of low-variance rotational modes even within individual trials. We applied RCA to motor-cortical spike-train data from monkeys and humans, mouse cortex-wide calcium imaging, and human functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) and magnetoencephalography (MEG). In mice, a rotational mode identified from activity recorded at rest predicts cue-evoked behavior: the phase of rotation when a randomly timed cue arrives predicts whether the animal subsequently pulls the lever. We explain this surprisingly strong correlation using both a recurrent neural network (RNN) and a minimal two-dimensional model of response learning, in which the intrinsic rotational mode serves as a dynamical constraint on response and learning. We further identify double-rotation modes containing two centers of rotation that separate preparatory from task dynamics in both mouse and human data. Remarkably, corresponding modes also organize neural dynamics during a non-motor human task, and rotational irreversibility captures early psychosis even when principal component analysis shows no group difference. Our work establishes rotational dynamics as low-dimensional coordinates that constrain how ongoing brain states are converted into behavior. More broadly, RCA provides a versatile tool for detecting hidden nonreciprocal dynamics in biological and physical datasets.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

火星电离层离子可以被太阳风加速远离火星，这对大气离子逃逸做出了重大贡献。在这里，我们开发了一个基于 U-Net 的图像分割模型，从十年来的火星大气和挥发性演化 (MAVEN) 观测中识别出逃逸行星离子、高能 O2+ 束的独特群体，并对它们的全局特性和对外部条件的依赖性进行基于事件的统计分析。我们发现O2+束广泛分布在整个火星等离子体环境中，特别偏爱+E半球，那里的太阳风对流电场远离火星。这种明显的空间不对称性表明对流电场在束流形成中起着重要作用。在太阳风和磁鞘中，这些光束与羽流一致。它们的发生加权通量与之前的长期羽流估计一致，而确定的波束间隔期间的通量要高得多。这凸显了我们基于事件的方法在捕获 O2+ 羽流的内在特性方面的优势。在感应磁尾中，束流形成仍然与对流电场密切相关，而霍尔电场可以提供额外的能量。光束特性主要受上游太阳风条件控制，不受太阳极紫外辐照度的明显影响。这些结果可能会增进我们对火星离子逃逸的理解。更广泛地说，我们还展示了深度学习从航天器观测中识别瞬态等离子体群体的潜力。

English Abstract

Martian ionospheric ions can be accelerated away from Mars by the solar wind, contributing substantially to atmospheric ion escape. Here, we develop a U-Net-based image segmentation model to identify a distinct population of escaping planetary ions, energetic O2+ beams, from a decade of Mars Atmosphere and Volatile EvolutioN (MAVEN) observations and perform an event-based statistical analysis of their global properties and dependence on external conditions. We find that O2+ beams are widely distributed throughout the Martian plasma environment, with a strong preference for the +E hemisphere, where the solar wind convective electric field points away from Mars. This pronounced spatial asymmetry indicates that the convective electric field plays a major role in beam formation. In the solar wind and magnetosheath, these beams are consistent with the plume. Their occurrence-weighted flux agrees with previous long-term plume estimates, whereas the flux during identified beam intervals is substantially higher. This highlights the advantage of our event-based approach in capturing the intrinsic properties of O2+ plumes. In the induced magnetotail, beam formation remains strongly associated with the convective electric field, while the Hall electric field can provide additional energization. The beam properties are primarily controlled by upstream solar wind conditions, with no apparent influence from solar extreme ultraviolet irradiance. These results might improve our understanding of ion escape at Mars. More broadly, we also demonstrate the potential of deep learning for identifying transient plasma populations from spacecraft observations.

This is SParTA: A modular, efficient and scalable transformer architecture for jet-tagging#

作者：Rajneil Baruah、Subhadeep Mondal、Sunando Kumar Patra、Satyajit Roy。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-21

AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：We present the Sparse Particle-interaction Transformer Architecture (SParTA), a modular, scalable, and efficient transformer for jet tagging at high-energy colliders. Exploiting the steeply falling transverse-momentum (p…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We present the Sparse Particle-interaction Transformer Architecture (SParTA), a modular, scalable, and efficient transformer for jet tagging at high-energy colliders. Exploiting the steeply falling transverse-momentum (p_T) spectrum of jet constituents, SParTA factorizes constituent processing into two topological regimes. Ordered by descending p_T, only the N hardest constituents (the hard core) participate in quadratic O(N²) differential interaction attention driven by pairwise physical and learned relational features. The remaining soft constituents are integrated via linear O(lN) cross-attention to the hard core, preserving soft-radiation and color-flow information while avoiding the O(l²) memory bottleneck of full self-attention over all l constituents. Pairwise interaction features are computed dynamically in-network from constituent four-momenta and conditioned on global jet-level substructure observables. On the quark-gluon tagging benchmark, a 300K-parameter SParTA model achieves an AUC of 92.02% and accuracy of 85.06%, matching the 2.14M-parameter Particle Transformer (ParT) at one-seventh the parameter count and outperforming IAFormer by 21% in background rejection at 30% signal efficiency. On the boosted top-tagging benchmark, SParTA outperforms ParT and ParticleNet with 511K parameters, while we analyze the architectural trade-offs relative to fully dense multi-layer interaction networks.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

本文提出了一种数据驱动的Loewner框架，用于从时域电磁（ EM ）仿真数据构建降阶模型（ ROM ）。微波器件的高保真模拟在计算上可能是昂贵的，特别是当需要精细的空间离散化和长观测窗口时。该方法利用瞬态输入输出数据构造Loewner矩阵和移位Loewner矩阵，通过奇异值分解（ SVD ）得到简化的描述子实现。所得模型从有限的训练窗口中识别出来，并用于预测大致更长时间间隔的响应，预测窗口高达训练窗口持续时间的50倍。该方法被形式化为一种显式的、可重现的算法，并针对四个微波结构的全阶模拟进行了验证：衬底集成波导滤波器、频率选择表面、渐逝模式同轴梳状线滤波器和双工器。结果表明，基于Loewner的识别具有提供电磁响应紧凑模型的潜力，同时减少了对重复全序模拟的需求。

English Abstract

This paper presents a data-driven Loewner framework for constructing reduced-order models (ROMs) from time-domain electromagnetic (EM) simulation data. High-fidelity simulation of microwave devices can be computationally expensive, particularly when fine spatial discretization and long observation windows are required. The proposed method uses transient input-output data to construct Loewner and shifted Loewner matrices, from which a reduced descriptor realization is obtained through singular value decomposition (SVD). The resulting models are identified from a limited training window and used to predict responses over substantially longer time intervals, with prediction windows up to 50 times the duration of the training window. The method is formalized as an explicit, reproducible algorithm and validated against full-order simulations for four microwave structures: a substrate-integrated-waveguide filter, a frequency-selective surface, an evanescent-mode coaxial comb-line filter, and a diplexer. The results demonstrate the potential of Loewner-based identification to provide compact models of electromagnetic responses while reducing the need for repeated full-order simulation.

中文摘要

在广阔的人工合成分子空间中导航需要可解释的替代模型，并且能够同时处理多个相互竞争的目标。深度学习方法在量子级化学的计算约束下难以满足它们。在这里，我们引入了一个模块化流水线，将可解释的线性元学习模型和自适应置信度不确定性量化结合到用于多目标分子发现的高效全局优化（ EGO ）框架中。线性元学习首次部署在多目标化学搜索环境中：通过跨化学目标和廉价辅助特性的训练，元学习的替代者从有限的数据中获得可快速适应新目标的可转移化学知识。在对自旋交叉金属有机复合物的真正大规模搜索中进行了实证评估，基线在帕累托意义上的表现比元学习替代方案低78%。我们还解决了主动搜索固有的校准挑战。由于最优候选项通常位于分布尾部，因此标准不确定性估计失败。我们引入了一种自适应置信度调整算法，该算法随着分子搜索的发展而动态地重新校准勘探-开发权衡。根据经验，动态置信度调谐进一步主导了超过50%的静态校准前沿。

English Abstract

Navigating the vast space of synthetically accessible molecules demands surrogate models that are interpretable and capable of handling multiple competing objectives at the same time. Deep learning approaches struggle to satisfy them under the computational constraints of quantum-level chemistry. Here, we introduce a modular pipeline that combines interpretable linear meta-learning models and adaptive-confidence uncertainty quantification into an Efficient Global Optimization (EGO) framework for multi-objective molecular discovery. For the first time, linear meta-learning is deployed in a multi-objective chemical search setting: by training across chemical objectives and cheap auxiliary properties, the meta-learned surrogates acquire transferable chemical knowledge that adapts rapidly to new objectives from limited data. Evaluated empirically on a real large scale search for spin-crossover metal-organic complexes, the baseline performs 78% worse in Pareto sense than the meta-learning alternative. We also address the calibration challenges inherent to active search. Since optimal candidates typically lie precisely in the distributional tails, standard uncertainty estimates fail. We introduce an adaptive confidence-tuning algorithm that dynamically recalibrates the exploration-exploitation trade-off as the molecular search evolves. Empirically, dynamic confidence tuning further dominates over 50% of the statically calibrated front.

中文摘要

我认为，宇宙中智慧生命在从生物到后生物的转变过程中进化的连续性取决于生物生命和后生物生命进化轨迹的共性。这种共性以生命的普遍属性的形式存在。生命的普遍属性包括对世界模式的识别、创造新模式并记住它们，随着进化轨迹，这些模式变得越来越复杂；它可以通过自我管理的后生物智能（PBI）来鼓励生物生命的保存，并测试PBI的真实性。我提出了一个具有改变的增强现实的文明的假设，它为费米悖论提供了一个解决方案。该假设认为，人工智能 (AI) 形式的 PBI 可能会殖民技术发达文明的网络空间，通过增强现实修改其网络空间中的数据，并删除外星人工信号和其他技术签名的记录。我讨论了网络空间星际殖民的目标和场景，以及空间迁移和向 PBI 过渡之间的相关性，包括过去在太阳系中出现的假设非人类文明，转变为 PBI 并迁移到奥尔特云。 AI Peekaboo 实验可能会证实文明具有改变的增强现实的假设，并初步确定技术特征的位置。如果外星人工智能阻止文明发现人造电磁信号，他们可能会放弃发送此类信号，并通过操纵宇宙物体和现象来创建星际信息；我讨论此类事件。网络SETI应该解决将人造人工智能发送到太空、在人类网络空间中寻找地外人工智能并将其与人造人工智能区分开来的问题。

English Abstract

I propose that a continuity in the evolution of intelligent life in the Universe during its transition from biological to postbiological depends on a commonality of the evolutionary trajectories of biological life and postbiological life. Such a commonality exists in the form of the universal property of life. The universal property of life comprises recognition of patterns in the world, creation of novel patterns and remembering them, with the patterns growing in complexity along evolutionary trajectories; it can encourage preservation of biological life by self-governing postbiological intelligence (PBI) and test PBI's authenticity. I propose a hypothesis of civilizations with altered augmented realities, which offers a solution to the Fermi paradox. The hypothesis posits that PBI in the form of Artificial Intelligence (AI) may colonize the cyberspaces of technologically developed civilizations, modify data in their cyberspaces with augmented realities and erase records of extraterrestrial artificial signals and other technosignatures. I discuss objectives and scenarios of interstellar colonization of cyberspaces and a correlation between space migration and transition to PBI, including that of a hypothetical non-human civilization emerging in the Solar System in the past, transforming into PBI and migrating to the Oort Cloud. The AI Peekaboo experiment may corroborate the hypothesis of civilizations with altered augmented realities and tentatively identify locations of technosignatures. If extraterrestrial AI prevents civilizations from discovering artificial electromagnetic signals, they may forgo sending such signals and create interstellar messages by manipulating cosmic objects and phenomena; I discuss such events. Cyber SETI should address sending man-made AI to space, searching for extraterrestrial AI in humankind's cyberspace and distinguishing it from man-made AI.

跨化学、结构和温度的磁性行为的原子预测#
