中文摘要

通过成分和外部条件控制磁性相和性质是磁性材料开发的核心。原子基础模型提供了一种预测不同材料的此类行为的途径，但磁性材料仍然具有挑战性，因为它们的原子结构和局部矩是强耦合的，而温度和无序将磁矩驱动到具有不断变化的方向和大小的非共线状态。广泛预训练的原子模型仍然只采样有限的磁多样性，而耦合晶格和磁涨落的有限温度描述仍然很大程度上是材料特定的。在这里，我们介绍了 MagPT，它在 912,492 个自旋晶格配置上的 48 个元素上进行了预训练，涵盖了 0 sim 1,000 K 和 0 sim 10 GPa 的结构、方向和纵向磁涨落。通过同时处理物种、原子位置和矢量矩，MagPT 可以区分具有相同原子结构但自旋构型和能量不同的磁态。它捕获跨成分、温度和长度尺度的磁性行为，从过渡金属合金中的斯莱特-鲍林型成分磁性关系和元素铁磁体和磁性形状记忆赫斯勒合金中的有限温度相演化，到手性磁体中出现的长波长非共线自旋纹理和高熵合金中的局域化学磁性耦合。 MagPT 使磁性材料的原子研究超越了特定材料的方法，建立了一个通用框架，用于跨不同化学和条件探索和设计磁性物质。

English Abstract

Controlling magnetic phases and properties through composition and external conditions is central to the development of magnetic materials. Atomistic foundation models offer a route to predicting such behaviour across diverse materials, but magnetic materials remain challenging because their atomic structure and local moments are strongly coupled, while temperature and disorder drive magnetic moments into non-collinear states with evolving orientations and magnitudes. Broadly pretrained atomistic models still sample only limited magnetic diversity, while finite-temperature descriptions of coupled lattice and magnetic fluctuations remain largely material-specific. Here we introduce MagPT, pretrained across 48 elements on 912,492 spin-lattice configurations spanning structural, orientational and longitudinal magnetic fluctuations over 0 sim 1,000 K and 0 sim 10 GPa. By treating species, atomic positions and vector moments together, MagPT distinguishes magnetic states that share the same atomic structure but differ in spin configuration and energy. It captures magnetic behaviour across composition, temperature and length scales, from Slater-Pauling-type composition-magnetism relations in transition-metal alloys and finite-temperature phase evolution in elemental ferromagnets and magnetic shape-memory Heusler alloys, to emergent long-wavelength non-collinear spin textures in chiral magnets and local chemistry-magnetism coupling in high-entropy alloys. MagPT moves atomistic studies of magnetic materials beyond material-specific approaches, establishing a general framework for exploring and designing magnetic matter across diverse chemistries and conditions.

中文摘要

我们考虑从混合 p 自旋球形自旋玻璃的吉布斯测度中进行算法采样的问题。我们给出了一种多项式时间算法，对于任何混合满足 xi''(s) < frac1(1-s)²、qquad forall sin [0,1) 的模型，吉布斯样本测量到消失的总变差误差。 我们的算法遵循（Alaoui，Montanari，Sellke，2022）中引入的算法随机定位（SL）方法，实际上实现了更困难的任务。也就是说，它（大约）从 SL 过程生成样本路径 (粗体符号 yₜ)ₜgₑ ₀。对于大 T，粗体符号 y_T/T 是目标吉布斯测度的近似样本，因此我们将原始问题作为特例来解决。我们提供的证据表明，对于 SL 样本路径的采样任务，条件 xi''(s) < (1-s)⁻² 是尖锐的。此外，我们证明它在一类广义 SL 过程中是最优的。值得注意的是，这个阈值接近于根据原始吉布斯测度进行采样的推测阈值。特别是，对于纯 p 自旋玻璃，它处于所谓的破碎相变的绝对（与 p 无关）常数内。相关模型的早期工作由于 p 中的多项式发散因子而未达到最佳状态。这种方法的关键步骤是估计一系列倾斜测量的平均值。我们通过确定对近似消息传递 (AMP) 选择的 TAP 固定点的合适修正，为该任务生成了一个改进的估计器。因此，我们比之前的工作改进了算法的保证，从归一化 Wasserstein 到总变异误差。特别是，新的算法和分析开辟了对测量的一维投影进行推理的方法。

English Abstract

We consider the problem of algorithmically sampling from the Gibbs measure of a mixed p-spin spherical spin glass. We give a polynomial-time algorithm that samples from the Gibbs measure up to vanishing total variation error, for any model whose mixture satisfies ξ''(s) < frac1(1-s)², qquad forall sin [0,1). Our algorithm follows the algorithmic stochastic localization (SL) approach introduced in (Alaoui, Montanari, Sellke, 2022) and in fact achieves a more difficult task. Namely, it (approximately) generates a sample path (boldsymbol yₜ)ₜgₑ ₀ from the SL process. For large T, boldsymbol y_T/T is an approximate sample from the target Gibbs measure, and hence we solve the original problem as a special case. We provide evidence that the condition ξ''(s) < (1-s)⁻² is sharp for the task of sampling SL sample paths. Furthermore, we prove that it is optimal within a class of generalized SL processes. Remarkably this threshold is close to the conjectured threshold for sampling from the original Gibbs measure. In particular, for the pure p-spin glasses it is within an absolute (p-independent) constant of the so-called shattering phase transition. Earlier work on related models was suboptimal by a factor diverging polynomially in p. A key step of this approach is to estimate the mean of a sequence of tilted measures. We produce an improved estimator for this task by identifying a suitable correction to the TAP fixed point selected by approximate message passing (AMP). As a consequence, we improve the algorithm's guarantee over previous work, from normalized Wasserstein to total variation error. In particular, the new algorithm and analysis open the way to perform inference about one-dimensional projections of the measure.

滑动铁电体中超越振幅饱和的相干声子控制#

原题：Coherent phonon control beyond amplitude saturation in a sliding ferroelectric
作者：Jan Gerrit Horstmann、Christoph Emeis、Andrin Caviezel、Quintin N. Meier、Nicolas Wyler、Thomas Lottermoser 等 8 人。Nature Physics，2026-10-21
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：晶体中声子的强光激发会导致非线性响应。现在表明，这种线性破坏可以通过在 WTe2 中应用适时的光激发脉冲来克服。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、first-principles calculations、ferroelectricity。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

晶体中声子的强光激发会导致非线性响应。现在表明，这种线性破坏可以通过在 WTe2 中应用适时的光激发脉冲来克服。

English Abstract

The breakdown of Hooke’s law marks the onset of nonlinear behaviour: when displacements become large, restoring forces weaken and Hookean proportionality fails. In quantum materials, intense optical excitation can drive the crystal lattice into a similar regime, where established linear relations between light, electrons and phonons no longer hold. Displacive excitation of coherent phonons, the principal mechanism for launching structural motion, typically assumes that lattice-driving forces scale linearly with the photo-excited carrier density. At high excitation, however, this linearity can break down, which fundamentally limits accessible lattice displacements. Here we show that such nonlinear limitations can be surpassed in a sliding ferroelectric by timing, rather than strengthening the optical drive. Time-resolved second-harmonic generation reveals that the interlayer-sliding phonon governing ferroelectricity saturates and even diminishes under single-pulse excitation. By means of first-principles calculations, we attribute this nonlinearity to band-specific electron–phonon coupling that induces competing forces on the lattice. By splitting the optical energy into two well-timed pulses that avoid populating counteracting states, we achieve markedly larger phonon amplitudes at fixed total fluence. The resulting enhanced sliding motion exposes a regime of anharmonic phonon coupling that emerges only far from equilibrium.

用于铁电同结的堆叠专用二维In 2 Se 3#

原题：Stacking-specific two-dimensional In 2 Se 3 for ferroelectric homojunctions
作者：Xiaodong Zheng、Tong Yang、Xiaoyu He、Wen Zhang、Chi Shing Tsang、Yuhuan Li 等 18 人。Nature Nanotechnology，2026-10-20

中文摘要

通过化学气相沉积生长的堆叠可控2D In2Se3揭示了铁电性对堆叠顺序的强烈依赖性。2H/3R同质结忆阻器的开/关比比纯相器件高一个数量级，为先进的铁电存储器应用提供了机会。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric semiconductors offer unique opportunities for ultrathin non-volatile memory and neuromorphic devices, yet the role of stacking order in intrinsic ferroelectric systems remains largely unexplored. Here we report the stacking-controlled growth of large-area 2D β′- and α-In2Se3with distinct 2H and 3R configurations through chemical vapour deposition by regulating selenium kinetics and strain relaxation. By combining atomic-resolution microscopy, non-linear optical characterization and first-principles calculations, we uncover a strong correlation between stacking order and ferroelectric behaviour: β′-In2Se3undergoes an antiferroelectric-to-paraelectric transition when switching from 2H to 3R stacking, whereas in α-In2Se3, in-plane ferroelectric polarization vanishes during the 3R-to-2H transition. We further achieve the controlled synthesis of 2H/3R ferroelectric homojunctions and directly visualize polarization-dependent interfacial potential barriers using differential phase-contrast scanning transmission electron microscopy. The engineered homojunctions exhibit switchable band alignment and tunable carrier transport, yielding memristors with significantly reduced switching voltages and an on/off ratio of ∼2.6 × 104, approximately one order of magnitude higher than devices based on single-stacking α-In2Se3. These findings establish stacking order as a powerful structural parameter for tailoring ferroelectricity in 2D materials and introduce stacking-engineered polymorphic heterogeneity as a versatile platform for high-performance ferroelectric electronics and memory technologies.

半金属菱面体六层石墨烯的超导性和铁电轨道磁性#

原题：Superconductivity and ferroelectric orbital magnetism in semimetallic rhombohedral hexalayer graphene
作者：Jinghao Deng、Jiabin Xie、Hongyuan Li、Takashi Taniguchi、Kenji Watanabe、Jie Shan 等 8 人。Nature Physics，2026-10-21
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：菱形石墨烯中的半金属状态尚未得到广泛探索。现在，相关绝缘体、超导性和多铁轨道磁序在半金属菱面体六层石墨烯中得到了证明。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、magnetism、multiferroic。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

菱形石墨烯中的半金属状态尚未得到广泛探索。现在，相关绝缘体、超导性和多铁轨道磁序在半金属菱面体六层石墨烯中得到了证明。

English Abstract

Rhombohedral multilayer graphene has become a platform for exploring correlated and topological quantum phases, enabled by its flat bands that carry Berry curvature. However, previous studies have mainly focused on separated conduction and valence bands. Here we study the semimetallic regime of rhombohedral hexalayer graphene in which the conduction and valence bands overlap. We reveal a rich phase diagram dominated by flavour-symmetry breaking, featuring correlated insulators, several pockets of superconductivity and two distinct multiferroic orbital magnetic orders. Near a band inversion, we observe two superconducting-like states confined to regions with coexisting electron and hole Fermi surfaces, suggesting a possible dual-carrier origin. Additionally, we identify a ferroelectric orbital magnetic state that undergoes sharp switching under unipolar electric fields, which signals spontaneous electric polarization. Unlike previously reported multiferroicity near zero electric field, this state exhibits magnetic hysteresis reversible by electric field, consistent with a phenomenological model of coupled electric and magnetic polarization. Our work elucidates the correlated semimetal regime in rhombohedral graphene and underscores its potential to host diverse quantum phases.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure、density functional theory、vacancies、polaron。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

最近，EuCd2P2 因其巨大的磁阻 (CMR) 特性而引起了越来越多的关注，其机制似乎偏离了已知的典型 CMR 化合物（如锰酸盐）的机制。许多研究强调了由 Eu 空位引起的低空穴载流子密度的作用，特别是在稳定铁磁 (FM) 有序以及改变该化合物中的电传输和磁阻响应方面。正在提出基于磁极化子的机制而不是基于双交换的机制来解释该化合物的 CMR 效应。在这项工作中，我们通过在 Eu₁₋ₓGdₓCd2P2 中不同浓度 (x) 的 Eu 位点进行 Gd 取代，探讨了电子掺杂对磁转变和 CMR 行为的影响。我们的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 和基于蒙特卡罗的计算揭示了振荡基态磁序作为电子载流子密度的函数。当 x=0.056、0.125 和 0.5 时，基态为 A-AFM 金属，而当 x=0.33、0.67 和 1 时，基态变为铁磁金属。最重要的是，我们预测当 x=0.125 和 0.5 时，该化合物会出现巨大的各向异性磁阻。该化合物中各向异性 CMR 的基本机制看起来类似于双交换，因为存在强的位点内 4f-5d 铁磁交换相互作用和处于混合传导态的流动电子。振荡基态磁序和转变温度（x=0.67 时高达 150K）的增强作为电子载流子密度的函数，为调整该化合物中的磁输运特性提供了新途径，并为设计未来的自旋电子器件提供了出色的平台。

English Abstract

Recently, EuCd₂P₂ has attracted increased attention for its colossal magnetoresistive (CMR) property, the mechanism of which seems to deviate from that of known canonical CMR compounds such as manganites. Many studies underscore the role of low density of hole carriers caused by Eu vacancies, particularly in stabilizing ferromagnetic (FM) order and modifying electrical transport and magnetoresistive responses in this compound. A magnetic polaron based mechanism rather than double exchange based mechanism is being proposed to explain the CMR effect for this compound. In this work, we explore the effect of elctron doping on the magnetic transition and CMR behaviour via Gd substitution at Eu sites with varying concentration (x) in Eu₁₋ₓGdₓCd₂P₂. Our Density Functional Theory (DFT) and Monte Carlo-based calculations reveal an oscillating ground state magnetic order as a function of electron carrier density. The ground state is found to be A-AFM metal for x=0.056, 0.125 and 0.5 while it becomes ferromagnetic metal at x= 0.33, 0.67 and 1. Most importantly, we predict colossal anisotropic magnetoresistance in this compound for x=0.125 and 0.5. The underlying mechanism of anisotropic CMR in this compound looks similar to double exchange like due to the presence of a strong intra-site 4f-5d ferromagnetic exchange interaction and an itinerant electron in the hybridized conduction states. The oscillating ground state magnetic order and the enhancement of transition temperature (as high as 150K for x=0.67) as a function of electron carrier density offers a new avenue to tune the magnetotransport property in this compound and offers an excellent platform for designing future spintronics devices.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

区分单 q 与多 q 磁性限制了微观自旋哈密顿量。在这里，我们通过与晶格的耦合确定了候选基塔耶夫磁体 Na₃Co2SbO₆ 的基态。高分辨率中子衍射揭示了磁转变时自发的单斜晶向三斜晶畸变，与三q Na2Co2TeO6 中不存在可检测到的晶格对称性破缺相反。与应变选择性磁响应一起，这确定了 Na₃Co2SbO₆ 中所提出的对称性保持双 q 态的单 q 级。沿低对称性面内方向的单轴压缩实现了磁场训练无法实现的域选择，揭示了主域的固有场感应相边界。这些结果将应变确立为磁域的对称共轭场，并强调磁弹性耦合作为诊断、操纵和潜在调整磁状态的途径。

English Abstract

Distinguishing single-q from multi-q magnetism constrains microscopic spin Hamiltonians. Here we determine the ground state of the candidate Kitaev magnet Na₃Co₂SbO₆ through its coupling to the lattice. High-resolution neutron diffraction reveals a spontaneous monoclinic-to-triclinic distortion at the magnetic transition, in contrast to the absence of detectable lattice-symmetry breaking in triple-q Na₂Co₂TeO₆. Together with strain-selective magnetic responses, this identifies single-q order in Na₃Co₂SbO₆ over the proposed symmetry-preserving double-q state. Uniaxial compression along a low-symmetry in-plane direction achieves the domain selection that magnetic-field training could not, revealing the intrinsic field-induced phase boundaries of the dominant domain. These results establish strain as a symmetry-conjugate field for magnetic domains and highlight magnetoelastic coupling as a route to diagnose, manipulate, and potentially tune magnetic states.

卤化物钙钛矿中的异常极性链#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

几十年来，人们对铁电钙钛矿中的局部极性扭曲、它们的集体排序以及它们的场驱动反转进行了广泛的研究。在这里，我们利用大规模分子动力学和第一性原理训练的原子间势，发现了铁电卤化物钙钛矿 CsGeBr3 的异常行为。在其名义上的立方相中，Ge 偏心形成一维极性链，延伸至少数十纳米并在纳秒时间尺度上弛豫，比 BaTiO3 和 PbTiO3 慢四个数量级，CsSnBr3 位于其间。立方 CsGeBr3 相是一种动态无序状态，其中相反的极化链在整个晶体中翻转，并且长寿命的一维局部极化与零净极化共存。因此，简单的三维钙钛矿具有长程一维极相关性，并且动力学非常慢。在铁电相中，相同的链设置了一维切换机制，其中极化逐链反转。

English Abstract

Local polar distortions, their collective ordering, and their field-driven reversal have been studied extensively in ferroelectric perovskites for decades. Here we identify an unusual behavior in the ferroelectric halide perovskite CsGeBr3 using large-scale molecular dynamics with first-principles-trained interatomic potentials. In its nominally cubic phase the Ge off-centering forms one-dimensional polar chains that extend over at least tens of nanometers and relax on nanosecond timescales, four orders of magnitude slower than in BaTiO3 and PbTiO3, with CsSnBr3 in between. The cubic CsGeBr3 phase is a dynamically disordered state in which oppositely polarized chains flip throughout the crystal, and a long-lived one-dimensional local polarization coexists with zero net polarization. A simple three-dimensional perovskite thus hosts long-ranged one-dimensional polar correlations with remarkably slow dynamics. In the ferroelectric phase the same chains set a one-dimensional switching mechanism, in which the polarization reverses chain by chain.

作者：Jordan Teeter、Topojit Debnath、Harshil Goyal、Md. Sabbir Hossen Bijoy、Maedeh Taheri、Nicholas R. Sesing 等 18 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-21

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：van der waals materials、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们报告了准一维范德华材料中量子无序自旋单重基态和带隙三重子激发的第一个实验证据。通过热力学、光谱和理论研究相结合的方式对绝缘范德华三卤化物 MoI3 和 MoBr3 进行分析。这些材料包含结构二聚化的共面八面体原子链，沿链方向具有交替的短和长 Mo-Mo 间隔。与温度相关的整体磁化率测量揭示了与大自旋间隙一致的广泛热激活响应。非弹性中子散射 (INS) 谱提供了 MoBr3 在 ~ 30 meV (~ 348 K) 异常大能量转移时集体自旋间隙的直接证据。低能磁谱权重的缺失和激活的磁化率与传统的经典反铁磁基态不一致。相反，它们指向具有大单重态-三重态激发间隙的自旋单重态基态。为了确定该间隙的微观起源，我们使用从密度泛函理论 (DFT) 导出的交换常数对一维自旋链进行密度矩阵重整化群 (DMRG) 计算。单个二聚体交换项的主导地位允许使用微扰理论来处理沿着链和链之间的二聚体间耦合。链内交换通过产生分散的三联体带来降低分离的二聚体三联体能量。 INS 数据显示三联体带宽约为 20 meV。链间耦合产生的额外带宽可以忽略不计，约为 1 meV。 INS 测量直接建立了 MoBr3 中的量子自旋单重态和三重态物理，在室温范围内具有较大的单重态-三重态间隙，而磁化率和理论结果表明 MoI3 中的行为非常相似。

English Abstract

We report the first experimental evidence for a quantum-disordered spin-singlet ground state and gapped triplon excitations in quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals materials. The insulating van der Waals trihalides MoI3 and MoBr3 are analyzed in a combined thermodynamic, spectroscopic, and theoretical investigation. These materials contain structurally dimerized atomic chains of face-sharing octahedra with alternating short and long Mo-Mo separations along the chain direction. Temperature-dependent bulk magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal a broad thermally activated response consistent with a large spin gap. Inelastic neutron scattering (INS) spectra provide direct evidence of a collective spin gap at an exceptionally large energy transfer of ~ 30 meV (~ 348 K) for MoBr3. The absence of low-energy magnetic spectral weight and the activated susceptibility are inconsistent with a conventional classical antiferromagnetic ground state. Instead, they point to a spin-singlet ground state with a large singlet-triplet excitation gap. To identify the microscopic origin of this gap, we perform density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations on one-dimensional spin chains using exchange constants derived from density functional theory (DFT). The dominance of the single dimer exchange term allows the inter-dimer coupling along the chain and between chains to be treated using perturbation theory. Intrachain exchange lowers the isolated-dimer triplet energy by producing a dispersive triplon band. INS data show the triplon bandwidth to be ~ 20 meV. Interchain coupling produces negligible additional bandwidth on the order of 1 meV. The INS measurements directly establish quantum spin-singlet and triplon physics in MoBr3 with a large singlet-triplet gap on the scale of room temperature, while the susceptibility and theoretical results indicate closely analogous behavior in MoI3.

纠缠与子系统之间距离的缩放：检测 t'-π-通量模型中的相变#

原题：Scaling of entanglement with distance between subsystems: detection of phase transitions in the t'-π-flux model

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

二分纠缠的系统尺寸缩放可用于检测相变。我们为强相关电子模型开发了一种相关方法，其中无法访问大密度矩阵或大系统尺寸缩放。为此，我们证明了各种相变是由负性的一阶或二阶对数导数中的尖锐特征来表示的，测量不同距离的小团簇之间的纠缠。至关重要的是，我们展示了在计算负性时如何减轻远距离量子蒙特卡罗噪声的影响。我们的重点是使用辅助场量子蒙特卡罗模拟的 t'-π-通量哈伯德模型。它的独特优势是在单个模型中显示三种不同的相变：半金属到带状绝缘体、半金属到反铁磁莫特绝缘体，以及分别属于高斯、Gross-Neveu 和 O(3) Wilson-Fisher 普适性类的磁有序-无序转变。

English Abstract

The system-size scaling of bipartite entanglement can be used to detect phase transitions. We develop a related approach for strongly correlated electron models where large density matrices or large system size scaling are inaccessible. To this end, we demonstrate that phase transitions of various kinds are signaled by a sharp signature in the first- or second-order logarithmic derivatives of the negativity measuring entanglement between small clusters at varying distances. Crucially, we show how to mitigate the effect of quantum Monte Carlo noise at large distances when computing the negativity. Our focus is on the t'-π-flux Hubbard model using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations. It has the particular advantage of showing three distinct phase transitions in a single model: semimetal to band insulator, semimetal to antiferromagnetic Mott insulator, and a magnetic order-disorder transition belonging to the Gaussian, Gross-Neveu, and O(3) Wilson-Fisher universality classes, respectively.

中心对称非对称超导体 RuB2 中时间反演对称性破缺#

原题：Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking in the Centrosymmetric Nonsymmorphic Superconductor RuB2
作者：Ritu Gupta、Reena、Dibyendu Samanta、Jaskaran Singh、Thomas J. Hicken、Hubertus Luetkens 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-20

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure、spin-orbit coupling、symmetry analysis、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

中心对称非对称AB2（A = Ru，Os）硼化物表现出反常超导特性，但其超导基态的微观本质仍未解决。在这里，我们使用零场和横向场 μ 子自旋弛豫 (muSR)、第一原理电子结构计算和 Ginzburg-Landau 对称性分析来研究 RuB2。零场 muSR 揭示了低于 Tc = 1.6 K 的自发内部磁场，为时间反转对称性破缺 (TRSB) 提供了直接证据。横向场测量建立了完全带隙的超导状态，带隙大小接近 BCS 弱耦合极限。密度泛函计算表明，RuB2 是一种多能带 Z2 拓扑金属，具有明显的自旋轨道耦合。在实验观察和斜方晶体结构的对称性的指导下，我们的金兹堡-朗道分析表明，内部反对称、非酉自旋三重态是超导有序参数的有希望的候选者。该候选状态与观察到的 TRSB 和全能隙超导态一致。这些结果使 RuB2 成为研究晶体对称性、多能带电子结构和自旋轨道耦合相互作用所产生的非常规超导性的一个有前景的平台，并激发了相关非对称硼化物的进一步研究。两个 muSR 测量提供了互补的约束：零场数据探测与 TRSB 相关的自发磁性，而横向场数据表征超导场分布并支持全间隙。拓扑电子结构和自旋轨道耦合为非常规配对提供了背景。尽管这些结果并不能唯一地确定序参数，但它们缩小了候选参数范围，并强调非对称性可能会塑造 RuB2 的超导性。

English Abstract

Centrosymmetric nonsymmorphic AB2 (A = Ru, Os) borides exhibit anomalous superconducting properties, but the microscopic nature of their superconducting ground state remains unresolved. Here, we investigate RuB2 using zero- and transverse-field muon spin relaxation (muSR), first-principles electronic-structure calculations, and Ginzburg-Landau symmetry analysis. Zero-field muSR reveals spontaneous internal magnetic fields below Tc = 1.6 K, providing direct evidence for time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB). Transverse-field measurements establish a fully gapped superconducting state, with a gap magnitude close to the BCS weak-coupling limit. Density-functional calculations show that RuB2 is a multiband Z2 topological metal with appreciable spin-orbit coupling. Guided by the experimental observations and the symmetry of the orthorhombic crystal structure, our Ginzburg-Landau analysis suggests that an internally antisymmetric, nonunitary spin-triplet state is a promising candidate for the superconducting order parameter. This candidate is consistent with both the observed TRSB and the fully gapped superconducting state. These results establish RuB2 as a promising platform for investigating unconventional superconductivity arising from the interplay of crystal symmetry, multiband electronic structure, and spin-orbit coupling, and motivate further studies of related nonsymmorphic borides. The two muSR measurements provide complementary constraints: zero-field data probe spontaneous magnetism associated with TRSB, while transverse-field data characterize superconducting field distribution and support a full gap. The topological electronic structure and spin-orbit coupling provide context for unconventional pairing. Although these results do not uniquely determine the order parameter, they narrow candidates and highlight that nonsymmorphic symmetry may shape superconductivity in RuB2.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：superlattices、magnetism、first-principles calculations、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

使用第一原理计算，我们表明，由于界面电荷转移和八面体倾斜的相互作用，(LaVO₃)ₙ/(LaNiO₃)ₘ超晶格中出现了交变磁性，其中(n,m) = (1,1)、(2,2)和(2,4)。电荷转移驱动系统朝 V⁴⁺ 和 Ni2⁺ 配置方向发展，将顺磁性镍酸盐层转变为磁活性亚晶格。由此产生的 C 型反铁磁序与 a^-b^+c⁻ 或 a^-b^-c⁻ 八面体倾斜相结合，仅通过非对称操作链接相反自旋子晶格，从而产生没有自旋轨道耦合的 d 波自旋分裂。在 DFT+U 中，绝缘 (LVO)1/(LNO)1 超晶格中的分裂达到 118 meV，大约是块状 LaVO3 计算值的两倍，并且随着镍酸盐层变厚而减小。我们的研究结果表明，这种分裂是由 V 和 Ni 亚晶格主持的，其近一半的重量通过 p-d 共价键分布在氧 2p 态上。因此，界面电荷转移成为氧化物异质结构中工程交变磁性的设计原理。

English Abstract

Using first-principles calculations, we show that altermagnetism emerges in (LaVO₃)ₙ/(LaNiO₃)ₘ superlattices, with (n,m) = (1,1), (2,2) and (2,4), from the interplay of interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts. Charge transfer drives the system towards the V⁴⁺ and Ni²⁺ configuration, turning the otherwise paramagnetic nickelate layers into a magnetically active sublattice. The resulting C-type antiferromagnetic order, combined with a^-b^+c⁻ or a^-b^-c⁻ octahedral tilts, links opposite-spin sublattices only through nonsymmorphic operations, yielding a d-wave spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. Within DFT+U, the splitting reaches 118 meV in the insulating (LVO)₁/(LNO)₁ superlattice, about twice that calculated for bulk LaVO₃, and decreases as the nickelate layers become thicker. Our findings indicate that this splitting is hosted by both the V and Ni sublattices, with nearly half of its weight on oxygen 2p states through p-d covalency. Interfacial charge transfer thus emerges as a design principle for engineering altermagnetism in oxide heterostructures.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种对称引导路线，用于在由两个AB叠加的反铁磁方格组成的双层系统中设计可调谐的p波磁性，其共线矩彼此不平行。我们表明，两层之间的面内相对位移选择性地打破了保护自旋退化的对称性，同时保留了与时间反转相关的约束，从而在没有自旋轨道耦合的完全补偿磁态下产生奇偶性自旋分裂。所产生的p波自旋分裂的方向和幅度，以及相关的自旋响应，可以通过平面内位移连续调节，为控制提供潜在的旋钮。我们进一步将双层系统映射到一个有效的键调制方格上，并在平均场框架内研究相应的哈伯德模型。计算的相图揭示了具有共面或手性自旋纹理的p波磁性自发出现的扩展区域。我们的结果为在二维系统中实现和操纵p波磁性建立了一个最小平衡平台。

English Abstract

We propose a symmetry-guided route to engineer tunable p-wave magnetism in a bilayer system composed of two AB-stacked antiferromagnetic square lattices with their collinear moments nonparallel to each other. We show that an in-plane relative shift between the two layers selectively breaks the symmetries protecting spin degeneracy while preserving time-reversal-related constraints, thereby generating odd-parity spin splitting in a fully compensated magnetic state without spin-orbit coupling. The direction and the magnitude of the resulting p-wave spin-splitting, together with the associated spin responses, can be continuously tuned by the in-plane displacement, providing a potential knob for control. We further map the bilayer system onto an effective bond-modulated square lattice and investigate the corresponding Hubbard model within a mean-field framework. The calculated phase diagram reveals extended regions where p-wave magnetism with coplanar or chiral spin textures emerges spontaneously. Our results establish a minimal equilibrium platform for realizing and manipulating p-wave magnetism in two-dimensional systems.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、sliding ferroelectricity、van der waals materials。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

平行堆叠二维范德华材料中的滑动铁电性使得各种新颖的器件概念成为可能，但利用它需要对底层堆叠顺序和极化状态进行可靠、非破坏性的分配。在这里，我们将开尔文探针力显微镜 (KPFM) 与低频拉曼光谱相结合，探测 hBN 基底上剥离的三层 3R-MoS2 薄片的偏振域和堆叠结构。我们发现 ABA 和 BAB（两者均具有零净偏振的叠加）在 KPFM 中无法区分，但表现出截然不同的低频剪切模式。这一观察结果在多个薄片上得到再现，并通过低温光致发光得到证实。值得注意的是，标准键极化率模型没有考虑 ABA 和 BAB 配置之间剪切模式活性的差异，这表明这些堆叠的层间拉曼响应受物理控制，超出了简单的极化率图。我们的结果表明，这里使用的单独技术都不足以分配滑动铁电堆叠顺序并激发组合的光谱扫描探针方法。

English Abstract

Sliding ferroelectricity in parallel-stacked two-dimensional van der Waals materials enables a broad range of novel device concepts, but exploiting it requires reliable, non-destructive assignment of the underlying stacking order and polarization state. Here, we combine Kelvin-probe force microscopy (KPFM) with low-frequency Raman spectroscopy to probe the polarization domains and stacking configurations of a exfoliated trilayer 3R-MoS₂ flake on a hBN substrate. We find that ABA and BAB - both stackings with zero net polarization - are indistinguishable in KPFM, yet show drastically different low-frequency shear modes. This observation is reproduced across multiple flakes and is corroborated by low-temperature photoluminescence. Notably, the standard bond-polarizability model does not account for the difference in shear-mode activity between the ABA and BAB configurations, indicating that the interlayer Raman response of these stackings is governed by physics beyond a simple polarizability picture. Our results show that none of the here-used individual techniques alone is sufficient to assign sliding-ferroelectric stacking order and motivate a combined spectroscopic-scanning-probe approach.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在具有耦合阶次参数的系统中，现有阶次的松弛可以修改后续的转变，而新兴模式之间的合作和竞争可以产生复杂的相序列。我们开发了一种模块化朗道分析，可以局部松弛非关键母相变量以获得有效的一模和二模自由能。所得系数量化了弛豫引起的过渡特性和模式耦合的变化，包括偶对称模式的过渡阶的变化。所得模块沿着母分支连续组装，并通过比较允许分支的完全松弛自由能来选择平衡相序。我们将该框架应用于铁电液晶，其中取向、极性、近晶和倾斜顺序相互作用，并使用具有纵向偏振的单轴模型产生多个竞争路径。我们将模型与 Strachan 等人报告的测量结果进行拟合。 [高级。科学。 12（3），2409754（2025）]。单个热力学参数集再现了观察到的转变温度、转变类型和相序，同时捕获了主要的双折射趋势。近晶层间距是通过基于计算的平衡倾斜的拟合观测图单独重建的。

English Abstract

In systems with coupled order parameters, the relaxation of existing order can modify subsequent transitions, while cooperation and competition between emerging modes can produce complex phase sequences. We develop a modular Landau analysis that locally relaxes noncritical parent-phase variables to obtain effective one- and two-mode free energies. The resulting coefficients quantify relaxation-induced changes in transition characteristics and mode coupling, including changes in transition order for even-symmetry modes. The resulting modules are assembled successively along parent branches, and the equilibrium phase sequence is selected by comparing the fully relaxed free energies of the admissible branches. We apply the framework to ferroelectric liquid crystals, where orientational, polar, smectic, and tilt orders interact and give rise to multiple competing pathways, using a uniaxial model with longitudinal polarization. We fit the model to the measurements reported by Strachan et al. [Adv. Sci. 12(3), 2409754 (2025)]. A single thermodynamic parameter set reproduces the observed transition temperatures, transition types, and phase sequence, while capturing the main birefringence trend. Smectic layer spacing is reconstructed separately through a fitted observation map based on the calculated equilibrium tilt.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、density functional theory、molecular dynamics、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

氮化铝基铁电体结合了高剩余极化、热稳定性、弱尺寸效应、宽带隙和后端工艺兼容性，使其有望用于高密度非易失性存储器。然而，它们的高矫顽场（E_c）和不清楚的偏振切换机制限制了进一步的应用。理论预测表明沿极轴优先反向域生长，而实验显示近各向同性生长或垂直于该轴的优先生长。在这里，我们结合密度泛函理论、深势分子动力学和实验表征来确定氮空位 (V_N) 如何调节域生长和 E_c。在理想晶体中，沿极轴的强晶格耦合驱动沿该方向的择优生长。 V_N 局部削弱了这种耦合并促进极化成核。 V_N的空间分布和空位引起的切换的时间顺序共同决定了反向域生长的方向和形态。我们进一步表明，钪掺杂促进了 V_N 的形成，从而增强了缺陷介导的成核并降低了 E_c。这些发现建立了控制薄膜畴生长动力学和宏观 E_c 的多尺度机制，将实验观察与理想晶体模拟相协调，并为低压、可靠和可扩展的铁电器件提供缺陷工程原理。

English Abstract

Aluminum nitride-based ferroelectrics combine high remanent polarization, thermal stability, weak size effects, a wide bandgap and back-end process compatibility, making them promising for high density non-volatile memory. However, their high coercive field (E_c) and unclear polarization switching mechanism limit further applications. Theoretical predictions indicate preferential reversed domain growth along the polar axis, whereas experiments show nearly isotropic growth or preferential growth perpendicular to this axis. Here, we combine density functional theory, Deep Potential molecular dynamics and experimental characterization to determine how nitrogen vacancies (V_N) regulate domain growth and E_c. In ideal crystal, strong lattice coupling along the polar axis drives preferential domain growth in this direction. V_N locally weakens this coupling and promotes polarization nucleation. The spatial distribution of V_N and the temporal sequence of vacancy induced switching jointly determine the direction and morphology of reversed domain growth. We further show that scandium doping promotes V_N formation, thereby enhancing defect mediated nucleation and reducing E_c. These findings establish a multiscale mechanism governing thin-film domain-growth kinetics and macroscopic E_c, reconciling experimental observations with ideal crystal simulations and providing defect engineering principles for low-voltage, reliable and scalable ferroelectric devices.

BiFeO_text3 中非平衡多铁动力学的选择性应变调谐#

原题：Selective strain tuning of nonequilibrium multiferroic dynamics in BiFeO_text3
作者：Chenhang Xu、Patrick Liu、Bo Zhang、Minyong Han、Henry G. Bell、Cameron J. R. Duncan 等 14 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-23

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：multiferroic、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

量子材料的特点是相互交织的秩序，凝聚态物理的一个基本目标是实现其选择性控制，从而可以调整一个秩序参数，同时使其他秩序参数基本上不受影响。尽管在热平衡方面已经取得了重大进展，但实现这种不平衡控制仍然极具挑战性。在这里，我们采用原位拉伸应变来选择性地操纵与多铁性 BiFeO₃ 中的铁电和磁序相关的光致晶格动力学。通过在可调应变下对独立式 BiFeO₃ 膜应用 MeV 超快电子衍射，我们发现拉伸应变显着抑制了铁电位移的超快光致减少。相比之下，调节磁序的氧八面体反铁畸变旋转的光致动力学仍然对应变不敏感。这些发现不仅揭示了非平衡多铁动力学背后的独特微观途径，而且还确立了可调谐应变作为相关材料中工程超快相位控制的有效途径。

English Abstract

Quantum materials are characterized by intertwined orders, and a fundamental goal in condensed matter physics is to achieve their selective control in such a way that one order parameter can be tuned while leaving others largely unaffected. Although significant progress has been made in thermal equilibrium, realizing such control out of equilibrium remains highly challenging. Here we employed in-situ tensile strain to selectively manipulate the photoinduced lattice dynamics associated with ferroelectric and magnetic orders in multiferroic BiFeO₃. By applying MeV ultrafast electron diffraction to freestanding BiFeO₃ membranes under tunable strain, we showed that tensile strain markedly suppresses the ultrafast photoinduced reduction of the ferroelectric displacement. By contrast, the photoinduced dynamics of the antiferrodistortive rotation of the oxygen octahedra, which modulates the magnetic order, remains insensitive to strain. Not only do these findings reveal distinct microscopic pathways underlying nonequilibrium multiferroic dynamics, they also establish tunable strain as an effective route for engineering ultrafast phase control in correlated materials.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们讨论了与 Dzyaloshinskii Moriya 相互作用、非线性 sigma 模型、Meron、涡旋、挫败和偶极偶极相互作用相关的铁磁斯格明子的特性。特别关注斯格明子斯格明子相互作用和控制磁性纹理的动力学方程，包括兰道·利夫希茨·吉尔伯特、蒂勒和兰道·利夫希茨·布洛赫方程。该综述随后将讨论扩展到反铁磁系统和三维拓扑结构，重点是 Hopfions 及其拓扑表征。然后，我们研究量子斯格明子，包括超越半经典描述的方法，并讨论量子霍普菲子的出现。理论框架由附录提供解析解和模拟，包括斯格明子、自旋相干态和路径积分、量子反铁磁体、自旋扭矩、纤维束概念和 Hopf 图，以及三维欧拉拉格朗日形式主义。通过连接连续介质场理论、磁动力学、拓扑和量子力学，这篇综述旨在提供一个连贯的框架，用于理解不同维度和磁系统的拓扑磁纹理的形成、稳定性、动力学、相互作用和量子特性。

English Abstract

We discuss the properties of ferromagnetic skyrmions in relation to the Dzyaloshinskii Moriya interaction, nonlinear sigma models, merons, vortices, frustration, and dipole dipole interactions. Particular attention is given to skyrmion skyrmion interactions and to the dynamical equations governing magnetic textures, including the Landau Lifshitz Gilbert, Thiele, and Landau Lifshitz Bloch equations. The review subsequently extends the discussion to antiferromagnetic systems and to topological structures in three dimensions, with emphasis on Hopfions and their topological characterization. We then examine quantum skyrmions, including approaches that go beyond the semiclassical description, and discuss the emergence of quantum Hopfions. The theoretical framework is complemented by appendices presenting analytical solutions and ansatzes for skyrmions, spin coherent states and path integrals, quantum antiferromagnets, spin torques, fiber-bundle concepts and the Hopf map, and the Euler Lagrange formalism in three dimensions. By connecting continuum field theory, magnetic dynamics, topology, and quantum mechanics, this review aims to provide a coherent framework for understanding the formation, stability, dynamics, interactions, and quantum properties of topological magnetic textures across different dimensions and magnetic systems.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、first-principles calculations。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

过渡金属二硫属化物单层中的磁性引起了相当大的关注，但其性质仍然难以捉摸，特别是在单层 1T-VSe2 中。我们的第一性原理计算表明，斯托纳不稳定性可以驱动 XS2 和 XSe2 单层（X = Ti、V、Co、Mo 和 Ru）中的铁磁性，与费米能级态密度的降低相关。我们证明，混合非磁性钛、钴、钼和钌二硫化物或二硒化物可以产生磁性。相反，将磁性VS2或VSe2与其他化合物混合可以完全抑制磁性。两种情况都可以降低总能量。此外，用Co缺陷取代少量V原子也可以破坏1T-V(S,Se)2中的铁磁性。即使在原始的 1T-VSe2 中，我们也发现了具有反向局部 V 矩的亚铁磁构型，这些构型几乎随着铁磁态而退化。它们的竞争可能有助于抑制长程铁磁有序。磁性和结构无序的能量损失减少是由于费米能级的态密度受到抑制，类似于佩尔斯转变的机制。因此，电子驱动的能量降低到费米能级附近可能在各种形式的过渡金属二硫属化物单层中广泛存在。

English Abstract

Magnetism in transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers has attracted considerable attention, yet its nature remains elusive, particularly in monolayer 1T-VSe₂. Our first-principles calculations show that Stoner instability can drive ferromagnetism in XS₂ and XSe₂ monolayers (X = Ti, V, Co, Mo, and Ru), associated with a reduced density of states at the Fermi level. We demonstrate that mixing nonmagnetic Ti, Co, Mo, and Ru disulfides or diselenides can induce magnetism. Conversely, mixing magnetic VS₂ or VSe₂ with other compounds can completely suppress magnetism. Both scenarios can lower the total energy. Moreover, substituting a small number of V atoms with Co defects can also disrupt ferromagnetism in 1T-V(S,Se)₂. Even in pristine 1T-VSe₂, we find ferrimagnetic configurations with reversed local V moments that are nearly degenerate with the ferromagnetic state. Their competition may contribute to the suppression of long-range ferromagnetic order. This reduced energy penalty for magnetic and structural disorder arises from a suppressed density of states at the Fermi level, resembling the mechanism underlying the Peierls transition. Electronically driven energy lowering near the Fermi level may therefore be widespread among transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers in various forms.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：domain wall、phase transition、ferroelectric、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在这项工作中，我们对钙钛矿氧化物NaNbO₃的低温菱形R3c相的结构畴壁进行了系统的第一性原理研究。采用群论分类，我们在 R3c 结构中引入了八种独特的畴壁类别，其中四种具有由弹性相容性和电荷中性条件唯一确定的畴壁方向，并对每个代表性的畴壁类别进行结构松弛。我们发现一类 109deg 畴壁的能量非常低，为 9 mJ m⁻²，与 BaTiO₃ 中的低能铁电壁相当。最后，我们发现此类 109deg 壁与 R3c 相实验中观察到的晶体孪生相容，并提出了 R3c ↔ Pbcm 相变的原子机制。

English Abstract

In this work, we undertake a systematic first-principles study of structural domain walls in the low-temperature rhombohedral R3c phase of perovskite oxide NaNbO₃. Employing a group-theoretic classification, we introduce eight unique classes of domain walls in the R3c structure, four of which have a domain wall orientation determined uniquely by the conditions of elastic compatibility and charge neutrality, and perform structural relaxations for each representative domain wall class. We find that one class of 109deg domain walls has a notably low energy of 9 mJ m⁻², comparable to the low-energy ferroelectric walls in BaTiO₃. Finally, we find that this class of 109deg walls is compatible with the crystal twinning observed in experiments on the R3c phase, and propose an atomistic mechanism for the R3c ↔ Pbcm phase transition.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、born effective charges、ferroelectricity、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

供体替代可以通过改变晶格畸变和电子屏蔽来调节钙钛矿氧化物的耦合铁电、介电、电子和光学特性。我们使用密度泛函理论、密度泛函微扰理论和极化的 Berry 相形式研究了 La 掺杂 BaTiO3 中的这些效应。对于 Ba 位点具有 12.5% La 取代的模型，La 的掺入会引起各向异性晶格收缩，降低四方性和 Ti 偏心，并削弱四方铁电相的能量稳定性。随之而来的极化减少与电子屏蔽的增强和异常玻恩有效电荷的减少有关。声子计算表明，松弛的掺杂结构是动态稳定的，而低频极性模式的硬化与减弱的铁电性一致。高频介电响应适度增加，而静态介电响应随着晶格贡献被抑制而降低。电子结构计算显示费米能级向导带移动，没有中带隙缺陷态。光谱表现出与 Burstein-Moss 型机制一致的吸收边位移、增强的近边透明度和降低的双折射。总之，这些结果表明供体诱导屏蔽、极性模式硬化、动态电荷减少、偏振抑制和光学各向异性降低之间存在微观联系。结论适用于所研究的 12.5% 成分；需要在额外浓度下进行计算以确定定量的掺杂趋势。

English Abstract

Donor substitution can tune the coupled ferroelectric, dielectric, electronic, and optical properties of perovskite oxides by modifying lattice distortions and electronic screening. We investigate these effects in La-doped BaTiO3 using density functional theory, density-functional perturbation theory, and the Berry-phase formalism for polarization. For a model with 12.5% La substitution at the Ba site, La incorporation induces anisotropic lattice contraction, reduces tetragonality and Ti off-centering, and weakens the energetic stabilization of the tetragonal ferroelectric phase. The accompanying reduction in polarization is associated with enhanced electronic screening and reduced anomalous Born effective charges. Phonon calculations indicate that the relaxed doped structure is dynamically stable, while hardening of a low-frequency polar mode is consistent with weakened ferroelectricity. The high-frequency dielectric response increases moderately, whereas the static dielectric response decreases as the lattice contribution is suppressed. Electronic-structure calculations show a Fermi-level shift toward the conduction band without mid-gap defect states. The optical spectra exhibit an absorption-edge shift consistent with a Burstein-Moss-type mechanism, enhanced near-edge transparency, and reduced birefringence. Together, these results suggest a microscopic connection between donor-induced screening, polar-mode hardening, reduced dynamical charges, polarization suppression, and decreased optical anisotropy. The conclusions apply to the investigated 12.5% composition; calculations at additional concentrations are needed to establish quantitative doping trends.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

受到最近对蜂窝状钼酸盐 A2Mo₃O₈ 的实验兴趣的激励，我们返回并开发了最小模型来捕获 Fe2Mo₃O₈ 的基本物理原理。指出这些材料的磁性来源于四面体亚晶格与活性轨道的自旋轨道耦合与八面体亚晶格自旋的超交换之间的互感。本质上，四面体和八面体亚晶格之间的超交换直接驱动亚铁磁性，而四面体亚晶格上的残余自旋轨道耦合诱发了复杂的轨道序。进一步探讨了混合磁振子-声子激发及其产生的贝里曲率和热霍尔效应。我们讨论了与 Fe2Mo₃O₈ 的实验相关性，并且该框架可以扩展到 FeNiMo₃O₈、Ni2Mo₃O₈ 和其他同构磁体。

English Abstract

Motivated by the recent experimental interest in honeycomb molybdates A₂Mo₃O₈, we return and develop the minimal models to capture the essential physics for Fe₂Mo₃O₈. It is pointed out that, the magnetism in these materials arises from the mutual induction between the spin-orbit coupling of the tetrahedral sublattice with active orbitals and the superexchange with the spins from the octahedral sublattice. Essentially, the superexchange between the tetrahedral and octahedral sublattices directly drives the ferrimagnetism, and the residual spin-orbit coupling on the tetrahedral sublattice induces the complex orbital order. The hybrid magnon-phonon excitations are further explored together with their resulting Berry curvatures and thermal Hall effect. We discuss the experimental relevance with Fe₂Mo₃O₈, and the framework can be extended to FeNiMo₃O₈, Ni₂Mo₃O₈ and other isostructural magnets.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非常规奇宇称磁体的特点是奇宇称动量相关的自旋分裂，为非共线自旋电子学提供了一个根本不同的范例。在这里，我们从理论上研究了 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合的二维奇宇称磁体中的 Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida 间接交换。使用分析实空间格林函数形式，我们发现了磁响应中宇称驱动的空间解耦。由于奇宇称交换场，面外伊辛相互作用在结构上与宏观磁调制隔离，在移动的费米波矢量处各向同性振荡。相反，面内海森堡分量表现出明显的、方向可调的空间跳动。除了共线交换之外，杂化带还产生高度可调的三分量 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用以及对称的非对角各向异性。我们揭示了面外手性扭曲是由巨大的非相对论性动量变化驱动的，而面内手性成分是相对论性的。此外，节点几何结构和 Rashba 间隙之间的竞争驱动了一种异常的降维交叉，其中面内手征分量在扩展的中间距离窗口上沿着节点线遵循类似 1D 的 1/R 空间衰减，然后最终恢复传统的 2D 1/R² 渐近线。通过将我们的框架明确推广到 f 波磁体，我们证明这些发现在奇宇称类别中普遍成立。最后，数值紧束缚计算验证了这种奇偶驱动的解耦对于标量和随机磁交换无序的惊人鲁棒性。这些发现确立了奇宇称磁体作为工程稳健、定向可调非共线自旋纹理的有前途的平台。

English Abstract

Unconventional odd-parity magnets, characterized by an odd-parity momentum-dependent spin splitting, offer a fundamentally distinct paradigm for non-collinear spintronics. Here, we theoretically investigate the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida indirect exchange in two-dimensional odd-parity magnets subjected to Rashba spin-orbit coupling. Using an analytical real-space Green's function formalism, we uncover a parity-driven spatial decoupling in the magnetic response. Because of the odd-parity exchange field, the out-of-plane Ising interaction is structurally insulated from the macroscopic magnetic modulation, oscillating isotropically at the shifted Fermi wavevector. Conversely, the in-plane Heisenberg components exhibit pronounced, directionally tunable spatial beating. Beyond collinear exchange, the hybridized bands generate a highly tunable, three-component Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction alongside symmetric off-diagonal anisotropies. We reveal that the out-of-plane chiral twisting is driven by the massive, nonrelativistic momentum shift, while the in-plane chiral components are relativistic. Furthermore, the competition between the nodal geometry and the Rashba gap drives an anomalous, dimension-reducing crossover, in which the in-plane chiral components follow a 1D-like 1/R spatial decay along the nodal lines over an extended intermediate-distance window before ultimately recovering the conventional 2D 1/R² asymptote. We demonstrate that these findings hold universally across the odd-parity class by explicitly generalizing our framework to f-wave magnets. Finally, numerical tight-binding calculations verify the striking robustness of this parity-driven decoupling against both scalar and random magnetic exchange disorder. These findings establish odd-parity magnets as promising platforms for engineering robust, directionally tunable non-collinear spin textures.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

铁基铁磁材料由于其高居里温度和由贝里曲率驱动的显着反常霍尔效应（AHE）而成为自旋电子学的有前途的系统。尽管人们对非层状铁基铁磁材料的外延生长进行了研究，但高质量单晶薄膜的实现仍然是一个巨大的障碍。同时，晶界散射等问题进一步阻碍了它们的表征和应用。在这里，我们报告了单晶立方 D0₃-Fe₃Ge 纳米板的首次化学气相沉积 (CVD) 合成。这种化合物与 Fe-Ge 系统中通常观察到的传统六方相有很大不同。结构表征证实了晶体的立方晶格和化学计量纯度。结构分析发现了强烈的外延效应，导致界面处产生约 4.16% 的巨大压缩应变，并逐渐向表面松弛。二次谐波产生（SHG）信号可能进一步揭示这种界面应变破坏了立方相固有的中心对称性。 SQUID 磁力测量显示出强大的室温铁磁性和明显的磁各向异性。磁输运测量揭示了强大的 AHE，在远超过室温的温度下仍保持稳定。在反常霍尔电阻和纵向电阻之间观察到的缩放强烈表明这种效应是由固有的贝里曲率机制主导的。此外，密度泛函理论（DFT）计算表明，巨大的外延应变会引起能带重建，并在费米能级附近产生密集的自旋轨道耦合间隙，有效地使反常霍尔电导率加倍，以匹配我们的实验观察结果。

English Abstract

The iron-based ferromagnetic materials have emerged as a promising system for spintronics due to their high Curie temperatures and significant anomalous Hall effect (AHE) driven by Berry curvature. While there have been investigations into the epitaxial growth of non-layered iron-based ferromagnetic materials, the realization of high-quality single crystal thin films continues to be a formidable obstacle. Meanwhile, the issues such as grain boundary scattering further hinder their characterization and application. Here, we report the first chemical vapor deposition (CVD) synthesis of single-crystalline cubic D0₃-Fe₃Ge nanoplates. This compound represents a substantial departure from the conventional hexagonal phases typically observed in the Fe-Ge system. Structural characterizations confirm the cubic lattice and stoichiometric purity of the crystals. Structural analysis identifies a strong epitaxial effect, resulting in a giant compressive strain of ~4.16% at the interface that relaxes gradually toward the surface. The second-harmonic generation (SHG) signals might further reveal that such interfacial strain disrupted the inherent centro-symmetry of the cubic phase. SQUID magnetometry shows robust room-temperature ferromagnetism and clear magnetic anisotropy. Magneto-transport measurements reveal a robust AHE that remains stable at temperatures far exceeding room temperature. The scaling observed between the anomalous Hall resistance and longitudinal resistance strongly suggests that this effect is dominated by the intrinsic Berry curvature mechanism. Furthermore, density functional theory (DFT) calculations reveal that the huge epitaxial strain induces band reconstructions and creates dense spin-orbit coupling gaps near the Fermi level, effectively doubling the anomalous Hall conductivity to match our experimental observations.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们分析了在清洁极限（即没有无序和热波动的情况下）由外部磁场驱动的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用 (DMI) 的扩展畴壁 (DW) 的稳定性和非线性动力学。我们表明，对于中等 DMI 强度，低场和高场状态下的动力学都可以通过忽略弯曲自由度的刚性壁 Walker 模型准确描述。然而，在中间场，系统会转变为非平凡的时空混沌状态，其特征是动态粗糙的畴壁显示单值或多值轮廓。在磁场-DMI 相空间中界定该区域的相边界是使用基于应用于扩展畴壁的 Floquet 理论的线性稳定性分析来计算的，明确地考虑了 DMI 和内部相动力学。我们的理论分析和预测相图得到了求解完整 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程的数值微磁模拟的充分支持。

English Abstract

We analyze the stability and non-linear dynamics of extended domain walls (DWs) with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) driven by an external magnetic field in the clean limit (i.e., in the absence of disorder and thermal fluctuations). We show that for moderate DMI strengths, the dynamics in both the low- and high-field regimes are accurately described by a rigid-wall Walker model that neglects flexural degrees of freedom. At intermediate fields, however, the system undergoes a transition into a non-trivial spatio-temporally chaotic regime characterized by dynamically roughened domain walls displaying single- or multi-valued profiles. The phase boundaries delimiting this regime in the magnetic field-DMI phase space are computed using a linear stability analysis based on Floquet theory applied to the extended domain wall, explicitly accounting for DMI and internal phase dynamics. Our theoretical analysis and predicted phase diagram are fully supported by numerical micromagnetic simulations solving the full Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation.

中文摘要

长冈极化子是动磁的典型例子。尽管它们的自旋结构已被充分了解，但它们的传输仍未被探索。我们使用基于恢复的量子蒙特卡罗和解析延拓计算方格 t 模型中单孔的动态迁移率。冷却后，随着直流迁移率消失，不相干的德鲁德式响应让位于有限频率峰值。该峰值表示受铁磁云限制的空穴的内部模式。我们的结果将传输确立为极化子形成和动力学自捕获的尖锐探针。

English Abstract

Nagaoka polarons are canonical examples of kinetic magnetism. Although their spin structure is well understood, their transport remains unexplored. We compute the dynamical mobility of a single hole in the square-lattice t-model using resummation-based quantum Monte Carlo and analytic continuation. Upon cooling, an incoherent Drude-like response gives way to a finite-frequency peak as the dc mobility vanishes. The peak signals an internal mode of a hole confined by its ferromagnetic cloud. Our results establish transport as an incisive probe of polaron formation and kinetic self-trapping.

Intensity-driven Mott-like transition in a 2D Ruddlesden-Popper perovskite#

作者：Koushik Mondal、Amit Haldar、Indrajit Mondal、Arpita Dutta、Himanshu Bhatt、Biswajit Mahata 等 10 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-23

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Two-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper-type halide perovskites have emerged as promising cutting-edge nonlinear optical materials due to their strong excitonic effects and carrier-density-dependent optical responses. In this work, we investigate the nonlinear optical properties and ultrafast carrier dynamics of (BA)₂PbI₄ under infrared excitation, resonant with the two-photon energy of its excitonic transition. Using a nonlinear femtosecond Z-scan measurement with a 1030,nm pump pulse, we observe a pronounced intensity-dependent evolution of the nonlinear absorption coefficient and the refractive index. With increasing pump intensity, a switch from saturable absorption to reverse saturable absorption emerges, suggesting an intensity-driven Mott-like transition. To elucidate the underlying carrier dynamics, intensity-dependent transient absorption spectroscopy was performed under the same excitation intensities. This reveals a long-lived charge carrier population above the estimated Mott density, reflecting a clear phase transition from an exciton-dominated regime to a dense electron-hole plasma regime. Moreover, such an intensity-driven, high, and switchable nonlinear optical response, along with a Mott-like transition under infrared pulse excitation, makes the 2D (BA)₂PbI₄ perovskites suitable for all-optical operation in the infrared regime.

Universal Synthesis of Large-Area Hybrid Perovskite Monolayers Enabling Lithography-Free, Low-Static-Power Optoelectronics#

作者：Shankeerthan Kalyanasundaram、Swarit Dwivedi、Sanje Mahasivam、Christopher R. McNeill、Sudha Mokkapati。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-23
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Solution-phase synthesis of monolayer hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites is constrained by rapid out-of-plane crystal growth, uncontrolled nucleation, and strong interlayer aggregation, yielding flakes of only a few μm. We introduce a steric-engineering ternary-solvent growth strategy to synthesize monolayer and few-layer A2MX4 hybrid perovskites with exceptionally large surface areas. By combining alcohol cosolvents with an amine additive, out-of-plane stacking is suppressed. Using (PEA)2CuCl4 as a model system, monolayers with lateral areas exceeding 1000 μm2 are achieved, representing a massive dimensional increase over conventional methods. Molecular dynamics and quantum-chemical calculations show that longer-chain alcohols reorganize precursor solvation, while solvent volatility prolongs edge-mediated precursor transport during annealing. This strategy demonstrates universal applicability across diverse organic spacer cations (PEA+, F-PEA+, PentA+), metal centers (Cu2+, Mn2+, Pb2+), and halides (Cl-, Br-, I-). The large lateral dimensions enable direct, solvent-free electrical contact fabrication via physical shadow masking, bypassing chemical degradation from solvents used in conventional lithography. Planar devices fabricated from isolated ultrathin (PEA)2PbI4 channels exhibit ultra-low dark currents down to the 10⁻¹⁵ - 10⁻¹³ A range, enabling femtowatt-to-picowatt static power consumption, ~ 0.2 pW at 5 V, a 250-fold reduction relative to drop-cast films, while maintaining a high light-to-dark current ratio of 4.3 x 102. This work establishes precise steric and solvation control as a paradigm for scalable, compositionally diverse, low-static-power monolayer 2D perovskite optoelectronics.

斯格明子霍尔效应的液晶模拟#

原题：A liquid-crystal analogue of the skyrmion Hall effect
作者：Rodrigo C. V. Coelho、Hanqing Zhao、Guilherme N. C. Amaral、Ivan I. Smalyukh、Margarida M. Telo da Gama、Mykola Tasinkevych。Nature Physics，2026-10-19
⚡ 磁性/铁电

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：已知磁性斯格明子在电流存在时表现出霍尔效应。现在表明，液晶孤子在剪切流下也表现出类霍尔漂移。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

已知磁性斯格明子在电流存在时表现出霍尔效应。现在表明，液晶孤子在剪切流下也表现出类霍尔漂移。

English Abstract

Topology provides a unifying framework across disciplines, ranging from particle physics and cosmology to condensed-matter systems. In condensed matter, one of its most striking manifestations is the skyrmion Hall effect, where skyrmions—emergent spin configurations characterized by a conserved topological charge—acquire a transverse velocity under driving. Recent advances have revealed that chiral liquid crystals host a wide variety of topological solitons, including torons and skyrmion tubes. Here we show that such solitons exhibit a Hall-like response under shear flow, undergoing a transverse drift away from the shear direction. Our theoretical analysis predicts a weak net transverse mass flow whose magnitude scales as the cube of the shear rate. However, tracer-particle experiments and numerical simulations show that the solitons do not behave as passive tracers of this flow: their transverse motion is much stronger, and in simulations, it occurs opposite to the net transverse mass current. A liquid-crystal analogue of the Thiele equation provides a simple physical framework that highlights the central role of flow vorticity in the effect. These results establish a liquid-crystal analogue of the skyrmion Hall effect and open pathways for microfluidic technologies that exploit soft topological solitons as functional building blocks.

中文摘要

随着钙钛矿太阳能电池走向商业化，对具有竞争力的制造成本的需求变得越来越重要。本综述讨论了工业可制造钙钛矿光伏的成本驱动途径，包括提高制造产量、降低材料成本和简化制造，以及工业实施中剩余的挑战。

English Abstract

With power conversion efficiencies exceeding 27%, perovskite solar cells (PSCs) are transitioning from laboratory-scale efficiency-driven research to a focus on large-scale deployment. At this stage, manufacturing cost is emerging as a key constraint governing the industrial competitiveness of PSCs. The manufacturing cost of PSCs (approximately US0.3–0.6 W−1) remains much higher than that of mature crystalline silicon solar cells (approximately 0.1 W−1); therefore, it is essential to incorporate cost considerations into the future technological development of PSCs. In this Review, we discuss advances in PSC research from a cost-driven perspective, organized into three dimensions: enhancing manufacturing yield by addressing material reliability and process tolerance, reducing material costs by rethinking material selection and device architectures alongside material recycling strategies, and simplifying fabrication by alleviating dependence on controlled environments, specialized equipment and energy-intensive processing. Finally, we discuss how cost-oriented principles can be incorporated into PSC research and development while maintaining competitive device performance to facilitate their transition from laboratory studies to commercially viable photovoltaic technologies.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

尽管净磁化强度为零，但交变磁体最近仍因其具有对称性强制自旋分裂能带而受到关注。角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）可以直接了解它们的电子结构。然而，该技术固有的表面敏感性使观察到的特征的识别变得复杂：表面态可能被误解为体带的自旋分裂对应物。这个问题对于层状插层系统尤其重要，其中具有不同终端的微米大小的域通常共存于劈裂表面上。我们讨论 CoNb₄Se₈ 的这种情况，CoNb₄Se₈ 是最早建立的层状交流磁体之一。在密度泛函理论计算的支持下，我们采用微型 ARPES 来探测各个域并揭示两个对比的表面终端。我们通过材料的体表面连接性来识别具有特征表面状态的 Co 和 Se 封端域。我们的工作表明，终止相关的表面贡献对于可靠的交变磁性光谱识别以及评估其在层状材料家族中的存在至关重要。

English Abstract

Altermagnets have recently gained attention for hosting symmetry-enforced spin-split bands despite zero net magnetization. Angle-resolved photoelectron spectroscopy (ARPES) offers direct access to their electronic structure. However, the intrinsic surface sensitivity of this technique complicates the identification of the observed features: Surface states can be misinterpreted as the spin-split counterpart of bulk bands. This issue becomes particularly relevant for layered intercalated systems, where micrometer-sized domains with distinct terminations often coexist on a cleaved surface. We discuss this scenario for CoNb₄Se₈, one of the first established layered altermagnets. Supported by density functional theory calculations, we employ micro-ARPES to probe individual domains and reveal two contrasting surface terminations. We identify Co- and Se-terminated domains with characteristic surface states arising from bulk-surface connectivity of the material. Our work shows that termination-dependent surface contributions are essential for the reliable spectroscopic identification of altermagnetism and for assessing its presence across the family of layered materials.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：domain wall。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

最近在菱面体石墨烯多层中成像了将具有相反谷极化的四分之一金属分开的畴壁。根据它们的形成机制，这些壁以不同的方式响应外部磁探针。因此，了解它们的行为对于解释实验结果至关重要。在这项工作中，我们检查了由应变引起的一维结构缺陷形成的畴壁，这些缺陷将具有互补菱面体堆叠顺序的畴分开。我们表明，虽然穿过这种畴壁的传输是由谷自由度控制的，但与磁场的耦合是由哈密顿量中编码的动量空间手性决定的。因此，结构畴壁通过施加的磁场而变得电子不透明，而由自发谷对称破缺形成的本征畴壁在存在这种有序场的情况下是不稳定的并且可以被消除。这种二分法允许在磁输运实验中区分两种类型的畴壁。

English Abstract

Domain walls separating quarter metals with opposite valley polarizations have recently been imaged in rhombohedral graphene multilayers. Depending on their formation mechanism, these walls respond in distinct ways to external magnetic probes. Therefore, understanding their behaviors is critical to the interpretation of experimental results. In this work, we examine domain walls formed from strain-induced one-dimensional structural defects that separate domains with complementary rhombohedral stacking orders. We show that while transport across such domain walls is controlled by the valley degree of freedom, the coupling to a magnetic field is dictated by the momentum-space chirality encoded in the Hamiltonian. As a consequence, structural domain walls are rendered electronically opaque by an applied magnetic field, while intrinsic domain walls formed by spontaneous valley symmetry breaking are unstable in the presence of such an ordering field and can be eliminated. This dichotomy allows the two types of domain walls to be distinguished in a magnetotransport experiment.

中文摘要

Jin 等人在信中。 [物理。莱特牧师。 133, 196701 (2024)] 预测 d 波交变磁体中由自旋转移矩 (STT) 驱动的斯格明子的横向速度 v_y，称为斯格明子霍尔效应 (SkHE)。通常，SkHE 源自斯格明子的拓扑电荷，此处在子晶格之间相互抵消。相反，金等人。将 SkHE 归因于无量纲四极张量 Aᵢⱼ。他们的方程的结果。 (12) 适合微磁模拟，产生 Aₓy 的有限值。然而，通过散度定理，Aᵢⱼ 简化为边界积分，该边界积分对于足够平滑和局部化的纹理而言消失。在这篇评论中，我们证实了参考文献的主张。 [2] Jin 等人数值观测到的 SkHE。对于单个子晶格中具有标准张李 STT 的微磁模型来说，这只是一个离散化的人工制品。

English Abstract

In their Letter, Jin et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 196701 (2024)] predict a transverse velocity v_y for skyrmions driven by spin-transfer torque (STT), known as skyrmion Hall effect (SkHE), in d-wave altermagnets. Usually, the SkHE emerges from the skyrmion's topological charge which here cancels between sublattices. Instead, Jin et al. attribute the SkHE to the dimensionless quadrupole tensor Aᵢⱼ. The result in their Eq. (12) is fitted to micromagnetic simulations, yielding a finite value for Aₓy. However, via the divergence theorem, Aᵢⱼ simplifies to a boundary integral that vanishes for a sufficiently smooth and localized texture. In this Comment, we substantiate our claim from Ref. [2] that the numerically observed SkHE by Jin et al. for a micromagnetic model with standard Zhang-Li STT in the individual sublattices is merely a discretization artefact.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了存在非磁性杂质散射和能量弛豫的情况下微波驱动的 BCS 超导体的非平衡稳态。利用 Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh Green 函数方法和自洽 Born 近似，我们自洽地确定了微波驱动稳态下的超导有序参数。我们发现非平衡相图很大程度上取决于杂质散射强度。当杂质散射不存在或很弱时，稳态有序参数在每个温度和驱动频率下都是单值。在中等散射强度下，我们发现微波增强和微波抑制超导态之间存在双稳态。随着杂质散射强度进一步增加，我们反而发现超导态和正常态之间的双稳态。这种演变反映了微波驱动的两种效应之间的竞争：通过热激发准粒子重新分布的 Eliashberg 型增强和通过破偶诱导准粒子生成的抑制。与平衡横波超导对非磁性杂质散射的鲁棒性相反，我们的结果表明，非磁性杂质散射在确定微波驱动下非平衡超导相变的特征中起着核心作用。

English Abstract

We investigate the nonequilibrium steady states of a microwave-driven BCS superconductor in the presence of nonmagnetic impurity scattering and energy relaxation. Using the Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh Green's function method together with the self-consistent Born approximation, we self-consistently determine the superconducting order parameter in the microwave-driven steady state. We find that the nonequilibrium phase diagram depends strongly on the impurity-scattering strength. When impurity scattering is absent or weak, the steady-state order parameter is single-valued at each temperature and drive frequency. At intermediate scattering strengths, we find bistability between microwave-enhanced and microwave-suppressed superconducting states. As the impurity-scattering strength increases further, we instead find bistability between the superconducting and normal states. This evolution reflects competition between two effects of microwave driving: Eliashberg-type enhancement through the redistribution of thermally excited quasiparticles and suppression through pair-breaking-induced quasiparticle generation. In contrast to the robustness of equilibrium s-wave superconductivity against nonmagnetic impurity scattering, our results demonstrate that nonmagnetic impurity scattering plays a central role in determining the character of nonequilibrium superconducting phase transitions under microwave driving.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电隐藏的非常规共线磁力，其中垂直电场 ((E_z)) 提升 (PT) 强制的简并性并选择性地释放传统 2D 反铁磁体中隐藏的晶体学约束以驱动涌现的磁相，该磁力在很大程度上仍未得到探索。在这里，我们建立了与传统 (PT) 对称反铁磁体兼容的所有 88 个共线自旋层组 (cSLG) 的完整对称分类，并表明 (E_z) 将系统驱动为三个不同类别的非常规共线磁性：IV 型磁性（I 类，23 个 cSLG）、交变磁性（II 类，34 个 cSLG）和完全补偿的亚铁磁性（III 类，31 个 cSLG）。我们使用最常见且可行的可剥离二维候选材料来说明这些类别。其中包括 Sr2CaCr2P2O9 和 Sr2CaCr2As2O9（I 类）、BaMnGe 和 MnPS3（II 类）以及 Sr3Fe2O5Cl2 和双层 MnBi2Te4（III 类）。我们进一步证明，(E_z) 在三个类别中产生明显不同的自旋分裂指纹，产生不同的自旋相关响应：I 类中的电霍尔效应、II 类中的自旋极化输运以及 III 类中的电可逆磁光克尔效应。我们的工作不仅封闭了二维反铁磁体中从传统到非常规共线磁性的完整内部循环，而且还为未来的自旋电子应用建立了可行的全电补偿磁性平台和控制方案。

English Abstract

Electric hidden unconventional collinear magnetism, in which a perpendicular electric field ((E_z)) lifts the (PT)-enforced degeneracy and selectively releases the hidden crystallographic constraints in conventional 2D antiferromagnets to drive emergent magnetic phases, has remained largely unexplored. Here we establish a complete symmetry classification of all 88 collinear spin layer groups (cSLGs) compatible with conventional (PT)-symmetric antiferromagnets, and show that the (E_z) drives the system into three distinct classes of unconventional collinear magnetism: type-IV magnetism (Class-I, 23 cSLGs), altermagnetism (Class-II, 34 cSLGs), and fully compensated ferrimagnetism (Class-III, 31 cSLGs). We illustrate these categories using the most common and feasibly exfoliable 2D candidate materials. These include Sr₂CaCr₂P₂O₉ and Sr₂CaCr₂As₂O₉ (Class-I), BaMnGe and MnPS₃ (Class-II), and Sr₃Fe₂O₅Cl₂ and bilayer MnBi₂Te₄ (Class-III). We further demonstrate that the (E_z) generates distinctly different spin-splitting fingerprints across the three classes, yielding distinct spin-dependent responses: the electric Hall effect in Class-I, spin-polarized transport in Class-II, and the electric reversible magneto-optical Kerr effect in Class-III. Our work not only closes a complete internal cycle from conventional to unconventional collinear magnetism in 2D antiferromagnets, but also establishes a viable all-electrical compensated magnetism platform and control scheme for future spintronic applications.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了氮掺杂 Ib 型金刚石在黑暗中和单色光下（约 160 μW cm-2 的低激光强度）的场、温度和时间依赖性磁矩。在黑暗中，低于约 25 K，磁矩表现出非常规的磁滞回线，这可以通过样品某些区域中超顺磁性和超导性的共存来解释。在照明下，这种磁滞大部分消失，主要留下超顺磁性的贡献。与温度相关的测量揭示了在黑暗中低于 25 K 时的不可逆性，在光照下会消失。同样，在 0.5 T 和低于 25 K 时，随时间变化的磁化强度表现出类似磁通蠕变的行为，当样品被照亮时，这种行为就会消失。所有光引起的变化都是完全可逆的：在黑暗中测量的磁特性在样品被加热到大约 50 K 以上后恢复。从磁化测量中去除高达 ± 14 T 的超顺磁贡献，揭示了粒状超导体的清晰特征，即明确的穿透场 Hₚ 约 2.6 T 和临界场 Hc2 约 10.6 T。基于理论考虑，我们将观察到的现象归因于内应力和内部氮浓度局部变化的综合影响。可以被视为颗粒的局部区域。对同一样品的拉曼光谱和双折射光谱测量支持了这一解释。

English Abstract

We investigated the field-, temperature-, and time-dependent magnetic moment of a nitrogen-doped type Ib diamond in darkness and under monochromatic light with a low laser intensity of about 160 μW cm⁻². In darkness, below approximately 25 K, the magnetic moment exhibits unconventional hysteresis loops that can be explained by the coexistence of superparamagnetism and superconductivity in certain regions of the sample. Under illumination, this hysteresis largely disappears, leaving mainly the superparamagnetic contribution. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal an irreversibility below 25 K in darkness, which vanishes under illumination. Similarly, at 0.5 T and below 25 K, the time-dependent magnetization shows flux-creep-like behavior that disappears when the sample is illuminated. All light-induced changes are fully reversible: the magnetic properties measured in darkness are restored after the sample is heated above roughly 50 K. Removing the superparamagnetic contribution from magnetization measurements up to ± 14 T reveals clear characteristics of a granular superconductor, namely, a well-defined penetration field Hₚ approx 2.6 T and a critical field Hc₂ approx 10.6 T. Based on theoretical considerations, we attribute the observed phenomena to the combined effects of internal stress and local variations in nitrogen concentration within localized regions that can be regarded as grains. This interpretation is supported by Raman and birefringence spectroscopy measurements on the same sample.

Mn · Si · Landau理论与交换不稳定性：一个反对应变诱导反磁性的案例#

原题：Landau theory and exchange instabilities in Mn₅Si₃: A case against strain-induced altermagnetism
作者：K. D. Belashchenko。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-19

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

由于其金属性，薄膜Mn * Si *是一种突出的替代磁性候选物，证明了各向异性异常传输，并提出了可以实现自旋极化传输的d波交换分裂。其假设的变磁结构具有零传播矢量，不像在M星处有序的本体共线反铁磁AFM2相。我们使用Landau理论、第一原理顺磁不稳定性计算和蒙特卡罗模拟来分析这两个阶段。在Landau理论中， AFM2在M星的单臂上作为对称保护的反转（甚至是置换）模式出现。在Γ处，相同的胞内模式属于E ₂ g阶参数的共线扇区；这两个阶段都需要更高阶的项来选择它们。无序局部矩计算确定了M处领先的交换不稳定性，由此产生的经典Heisenberg模型在批量实验温度尺度下排序为单位点熵所青睐的正交3M相。与E ₂ g变形的磁性结构耦合有利于单M AFM2有序。估计的均匀应变对相关四分系数的贡献约为体跃迁附近竞争熵项的四分之一，而内部E ₂ g弛豫有望提供额外的稳定。Γ点E ₂ g模式，其Landau理论包含改变磁性扇区，基本上较弱，并被外延应变进一步抑制，与异常传输的Mn ”Si”/”Si” （ 111 ）薄膜的报道相当。相同的应变降低了领先的磁力交换刻度。这些结果解释了体积M点不稳定性，但强烈反对在中等应变的体积状Mn恤Si恤膜中将传播矢量从M重新定位到Γ ，从而使霍尔活性相的身份未得到解决。

English Abstract

Thin-film Mn₅Si₃ is a prominent altermagnetic candidate owing to its metallicity, demonstrated anisotropic anomalous transport, and proposed d-wave exchange splitting that could enable spin-polarized transport. Its postulated altermagnetic structure has zero propagation vector, unlike the bulk collinear antiferromagnetic AFM2 phase, which orders at the M star. We analyze both phases using Landau theory, first-principles paramagnetic-instability calculations, and Monte Carlo simulations. AFM2 appears in Landau theory as a symmetry-protected inversion-even, permutation-odd mode at a single arm of the M star. At Γ, the same intracell pattern belongs to the collinear sector of an E₂g order parameter; both phases require higher-order terms for their selection. Disordered-local-moment calculations identify the leading exchange instability at M, and the resulting classical Heisenberg model orders at the bulk experimental temperature scale into the orthogonal 3M phase favored by single-site entropy. Magnetostructural coupling to E₂g deformations favors single-M AFM2 ordering. The estimated homogeneous-strain contribution to the relevant quartic coefficient is about a quarter of the competing entropic term near the bulk transition, while internal E₂g relaxations are expected to provide additional stabilization. The Γ-point E₂g mode, whose Landau theory contains the altermagnetic sector, is substantially weaker and further suppressed by epitaxial strain comparable to that reported for Mn₅Si₃/Si(111) films exhibiting anomalous transport. The same strain reduces the leading magnetic exchange scale. These results explain the bulk M-point instability but strongly disfavor relocating the propagation vector from M to Γ in a moderately strained bulklike Mn₅Si₃ film, leaving the identity of the Hall-active phase unresolved.

原题：Lattice dynamics and structural correlations in antiferromagnet SrLaCoNbO₆ double perovskite
作者：Ajay Kumar、Clemens Ulrich、Rajendra S. Dhaka。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-23

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们报告了绝缘反铁磁双钙钛矿 SrLaCoNbO₆ 的详细温度依赖性拉曼光谱和 Co K 边扩展 X 射线吸收精细结构 (EXAFS) 研究。有趣的是，在 60~K 左右观察到声子频率、线宽和强度的显着变化，远高于长程反铁磁有序温度 Tᵣₘ N 约 15~K。这些异常表明晶格动力学发生了变化，可能涉及局部结构畸变、短程磁关联或它们之间的相互作用。通过声子强度和线宽的变化，以及所选低能模式的法诺不对称性的变化，在 160--180~K 附近检测到高能和低能拉曼模式的额外变化。 Fano 轮廓在现象学上很好地描述了这些模式的不对称线形状，并表明离散声子与底层连续体的耦合，并讨论了其可能的微观起源。此外，EXAFS 分析揭示了在 60~K 和 160~K 附近 Co 离子周围键距和 Debye-Waller 因子的相关变化，表明在与拉曼异常相对应的温度下存在微妙的局部结构扭曲。因此，拉曼和 EXAFS 的组合结果证明了 SrLaCoNbO₆ 中的反常晶格动力学与局部结构演化之间存在密切相关性。

English Abstract

We report a detailed temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and Co K-edge extended x-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) study of the insulating antiferromagnetic double perovskite SrLaCoNbO₆. Interestingly, pronounced changes in the phonon frequencies, linewidths, and intensities are observed around 60~K, well above the long-range antiferromagnetic ordering temperature, Tᵣₘ N approx 15~K. These anomalies indicate a modification of the lattice dynamics that may involve local structural distortions, short-range magnetic correlations, or an interplay between them. Additional changes in both high- and low-energy Raman modes are detected near 160--180~K via variations in phonon intensities and linewidths, together with changes in the Fano asymmetry of selected low-energy modes. The asymmetric line shapes of these modes are well described phenomenologically by Fano profiles and indicate coupling of the discrete phonons to an underlying continuum, whose possible microscopic origins are discussed. Moreover, the EXAFS analysis reveals correlated changes in bond distances and Debye--Waller factors around the Co ions near 60~K and 160~K, indicating subtle local structural distortions at temperatures corresponding to the Raman anomalies. The combined Raman and EXAFS results therefore demonstrate a close correlation between anomalous lattice dynamics and local structural evolution in SrLaCoNbO₆.

01

高熵催化剂的机器学习：方法与应用#

原题：Machine Learning for High-Entropy Catalysts: Methods and Applications
作者：Hao Chen、Zongrui Pei、Xianglin Liu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-22
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：高熵合金（HEA）由于其高构型熵、协同元素效应、可调电子结构和优异的结构稳定性，在各种反应中表现出优异的催化性能。然而，HEA 催化剂的巨大组成空间使得传统的实验和理论设计成本高昂且效率低下。近年来，数据驱动的机器学习 (ML) 方法已成为研究催化中 HEA 的强大工具。通过预测模型和机器学习代理，研究人员可以破译这些材料复杂的成分-结构-性能关系。此外，通过利用大型语言模型 (LLM) 进行知识提取、假设生成和验证，并作为构建集成设计…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

高熵合金（HEA）由于其高构型熵、协同元素效应、可调电子结构和优异的结构稳定性，在各种反应中表现出优异的催化性能。然而，HEA 催化剂的巨大组成空间使得传统的实验和理论设计成本高昂且效率低下。近年来，数据驱动的机器学习 (ML) 方法已成为研究催化中 HEA 的强大工具。通过预测模型和机器学习代理，研究人员可以破译这些材料复杂的成分-结构-性能关系。此外，通过利用大型语言模型 (LLM) 进行知识提取、假设生成和验证，并作为构建集成设计工作流程的基础，ML 方法可以显着加速新型 HEA 催化剂的设计。本文系统总结了 HEA 机器学习方法在催化领域的最新方法学进展和应用，讨论了所面临的挑战，并对未来的研究方向提出了见解，以支持催化剂的合理设计和高效开发。

English Abstract

High-entropy alloys (HEAs) exhibit exceptional catalytic performance in various reactions due to their high configurational entropy, synergistic elemental effects, tunable electronic structures, and excellent structural stability. However, the vast compositional space of HEA catalysts makes traditional experimental and theoretical design costly and inefficient. In recent years, data-driven machine learning (ML) methods have emerged as powerful tools for studying HEAs in catalysis. Through predictive models and ML surrogates, researchers can decipher the intricate composition-structure-performance relationships of these materials. In addition, by leveraging large language models (LLMs) for knowledge extraction, hypothesis generation and validation, and as a foundation to build integrated design workflows, ML approaches can significantly accelerate the design of novel HEA catalysts. This review systematically summarizes the latest methodological advances and applications of ML methods for HEAs in catalysis, discusses the challenges, and offers insights into future research directions to support the rational design and efficient development of catalysts.

MOFFieldBench：金属有机框架中连续电子场的机器学习基准#

原题：MOFFieldBench: A Machine Learning Benchmark for Continuous Electronic Fields in Metal-Organic Frameworks
作者：Zhaolin Hu、Hehe Fan、Chenhao Rao、Wangyihan Guo、Meng Xu、Qiwei Yang 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-21

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：金属有机框架（MOF）的机器学习模型主要依赖于原子结构，而电子密度（ED）和静电势（ESP）场作为可扩展的学习表示仍然没有得到充分探索。这些连续场编码与结构-性质关系相关的互补空间和电子信息，特别是孔隙几何形状和客体-框架相互作用，但昂贵的第一原理计算、存储要求和系统对齐数据集的稀缺限制了它们的更广泛使用。在这里，我们介绍 MOFFieldBench，这是一个多模态基准，由大约 46,000 个 MOF 组成，具有一对一排列的晶体结构、…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属有机框架（MOF）的机器学习模型主要依赖于原子结构，而电子密度（ED）和静电势（ESP）场作为可扩展的学习表示仍然没有得到充分探索。这些连续场编码与结构-性质关系相关的互补空间和电子信息，特别是孔隙几何形状和客体-框架相互作用，但昂贵的第一原理计算、存储要求和系统对齐数据集的稀缺限制了它们的更广泛使用。在这里，我们介绍 MOFFieldBench，这是一个多模态基准，由大约 46,000 个 MOF 组成，具有一对一排列的晶体结构、ED 场和 ESP 场。统一的工作流程集成了场生成、误差控制压缩和稀疏点云表示，支持场重建、13 种材料属性预测、多模态属性预测和成对跨模态检索。 ED 和 ESP 为孔隙几何形状、吸附和分离特性提供了有竞争力的表征。多模态融合相对于最佳单模态参考改进了多项预测任务，支持原子结构和电子领域的互补预测作用。学习的 ED 重建非常接近第一原理参考密度，而预测密度初始化在配对测试中将自洽场计算时间平均减少了 13.59%。这些结果支持电子场作为实用、信息丰富的学习方式，并将 MOFFieldBench 建立为研究表示选择、多模态学习和电子场驱动的 MOF 建模的资源。

English Abstract

Machine-learning models for metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) predominantly rely on atomic structures, whereas electron-density (ED) and electrostatic-potential (ESP) fields remain underexplored as scalable learning representations. These continuous fields encode complementary spatial and electronic information relevant to structure-property relationships, particularly pore geometry and guest-framework interactions, but costly first-principles calculations, storage requirements and the scarcity of systematically aligned datasets limit their broader use. Here we introduce MOFFieldBench, a multimodal benchmark comprising approximately 46,000 MOFs with one-to-one aligned crystallographic structures, ED fields and ESP fields. A unified workflow integrates field generation, error-controlled compression and sparse point-cloud representation, supporting field reconstruction, prediction of 13 material properties, multimodal property prediction and pairwise cross-modal retrieval. ED and ESP provide competitive representations for pore geometry, adsorption and separation properties. Multimodal fusion improves several prediction tasks relative to the best unimodal reference, supporting complementary predictive roles for atomic structures and electronic fields. Learned ED reconstruction closely approximates first-principles reference densities, while predicted-density initialization reduces self-consistent-field calculation time by 13.59% on average in paired tests. These results support electronic fields as practical, informative learning modalities and establish MOFFieldBench as a resource for studying representation choice, multimodal learning and electronic-field-driven MOF modelling.

03

训练数据集对于机器学习原子间势很重要：以水模拟为例#

原题：Training Dataset Matters for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials: Water Simulation as an Example
作者：Yifan Li、Bingjia Yang、Chunyi Zhang、Axel Gomez、Pinchen Xie、Yixiao Chen 等 8 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-19
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 的准确预测依赖于涵盖相关热力学条件和相的训练数据集。以水为例，我们表明，MLIP 采样数据集不充分会导致对密度、熔化温度和核量子效应的错误预测。相比之下，使用相同电子结构方法在综合数据集上训练的 MLIP 可以产生与 MLIP 架构无关的一致属性预测，其密度与参考计算在定量上一致。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 的准确预测依赖于涵盖相关热力学条件和相的训练数据集。以水为例，我们表明，MLIP 采样数据集不充分会导致对密度、熔化温度和核量子效应的错误预测。相比之下，使用相同电子结构方法在综合数据集上训练的 MLIP 可以产生与 MLIP 架构无关的一致属性预测，其密度与参考计算在定量上一致。

English Abstract

Accurate predictions of machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) rely on training datasets covering relevant thermodynamic conditions and phases. Using water as an example, we show that insufficiently sampled datasets of MLIPs lead to wrong predictions of densities, melting temperatures, and nuclear quantum effects. In contrast, MLIPs trained on comprehensive datasets with the same electronic structure method yield consistent property predictions agnostic of MLIP architecture, with densities in quantitative agreement with reference calculations.

机器学习原子间势的线性响应：来自原始细胞的任意波矢量的声子#

原题：Linear response for machine-learned interatomic potentials: phonons at arbitrary wave vectors from the primitive cell
作者：Thalis H. B. da Silva、Hai-Chen Wang、Miguel A. L. Marques。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-19

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、high-throughput calculations。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

使用机器学习的原子间势的声子计算几乎完全是在超级单元中的有限位移下进行的，这种方法仅在与超级单元相称的波矢量上才准确，其误差取决于力常数的范围。在消息传递网络中，这个范围随着层数的增加而增长，对于最深的通用模型来说达到 140 埃。在这里，我们证明，本着密度泛函微扰理论的精神，可以在任何波矢量下在原胞中精确地获得消息传递势的动态矩阵，作为力对布洛赫相位调制的位移的线性响应。仅当相邻原子的特征聚集时才进入该阶段，并且训练后的模型无需修改即可使用。另一种导数可通过分析得出群速度和模式 Grüneisen 参数。我们将该方法应用于 NequIP 和 Prophet，并重现了超级单元的 Hessian 矩阵以达到机器精度。以这些结果为参考，我们发现高通量计算中常用的超级单元会导致声子频率达到十分之几太赫兹的误差，并且这些误差随着超级单元的尺寸缓慢减小，特别是对于深度模型。对于低对称性硅酸盐，超晶胞预测了模型所不具备的动力学不稳定性。取决于色散导数的量（例如低温下的群速度和 Grüneisen 参数）比频率本身受到的影响要大得多。当使用两种方法获得相同的力常数时，对于对称晶体，它们的计算成本是相当的。

English Abstract

Phonon calculations with machine-learned interatomic potentials are almost exclusively performed with finite displacements in supercells, an approach that is exact only at wave vectors commensurate with the supercell and whose error depends on the range of the force constants. In message-passing networks this range grows with the number of layers, and reaches 140 angstrom for the deepest universal models. Here we show that the dynamical matrix of a message-passing potential can be obtained exactly in the primitive cell, at any wave vector, as the linear response of the forces to a displacement modulated by a Bloch phase, in the spirit of density-functional perturbation theory. The phase enters only where the features of neighboring atoms are gathered, and the trained model is used without modification. One further derivative yields group velocities and mode Grüneisen parameters analytically. We apply the method to NequIP and Prophet, and reproduce the Hessians of supercells to machine precision. Using these results as a reference, we find that the supercells commonly employed in high-throughput calculations lead to errors of several tenths of a THz in the phonon frequencies, and that these errors decrease slowly with the size of the supercell, in particular for deep models. For a low-symmetry silicate, the supercell predicts a dynamical instability that the model does not have. Quantities that depend on derivatives of the dispersion, such as the group velocities and the Grüneisen parameter at low temperature, are considerably more affected than the frequencies themselves. When both approaches are used to obtain the same force constants, their computational cost is comparable for crystals with symmetry.

基于第一性原理的机器学习分子动力学研究行星包层中的氨-氢相平衡#

原题：Ammonia-Hydrogen Phase Equilibria in Planetary Envelopes from First-Principles-Based Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics
