关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

氨和氢是太阳系内外行星的主要成分，但它们在行星包层中如何相互作用却鲜为人知。我们在经过密度泛函理论计算训练的机器学习原子间势的驱动下，对 500 至 4000 K 和 0.1 至 100 GPa 的氨-氢系统进行大规模两相共存模拟。根据这些，我们绘制了共存的富氢相和富氨相的组成，以及区分完全混溶条件和相分离条件的临界曲线。临界温度随着压力的增加而增加，从 0.1 GPa 时的 500 K 到接近 30 GPa 的 2000 K，并且我们发现在 3000 和 4000 K 直至 100 GPa 时没有相分离。临界曲线的斜率在 0.3 至 30 GPa 之间大幅下降，并且在约 30 GPa 和 2000 K 以上，曲线几乎变平，此时分子开始让位于原子物种。共存曲线与富含氨的临界成分不对称。对于预期的包络成分，相界位于天王星和海王星已发表的热剖面附近并与之相交，这意味着今天这些行星上可能会下雨。相比之下，在TOI-270 d等温暖的亚海王星中，氨和氢可能以任何比例混溶。结合之前的研究，我们的结果表明，当行星冷却时，硅酸盐、水和氨会依次与氢分离，这对其成分、热和结构演化产生影响。

English Abstract

Ammonia and hydrogen are major constituents of planets within and beyond the solar system, yet how they interact in planetary envelopes is poorly known. We perform large-scale two-phase coexistence simulations of the ammonia-hydrogen system from 500 to 4000 K and 0.1 to 100 GPa, driven by machine-learning interatomic potentials trained on density functional theory calculations. From these we map the compositions of coexisting hydrogen-rich and ammonia-rich phases, and the critical curve that separates conditions of complete miscibility from those of phase separation. The critical temperature increases with pressure, from 500 K at 0.1 GPa to 2000 K near 30 GPa, and we find no phase separation at 3000 and 4000 K up to 100 GPa. The slope of the critical curve decreases substantially between 0.3 and 30 GPa, and the curve nearly flattens above about 30 GPa and 2000 K, where molecules begin to give way to atomic species. The coexistence curves are asymmetric with an ammonia-rich critical composition. For the expected envelope compositions, the phase boundary lies close to, and intersects, published thermal profiles of Uranus and Neptune, implying ammonia may be raining out in these planets today. In contrast, in warm sub-Neptunes such as TOI-270 d, ammonia and hydrogen are likely miscible in all proportions. Together with previous studies, our results suggest that silicates, water and ammonia separate from hydrogen in sequence as planets cool, with implications for their compositional, thermal and structural evolution.

SoLiD26：用于机器学习原子间势的第一原理固液界面数据集#

原题：SoLiD26: A First Principles Solid-Liquid Interface Dataset for Machine-learned Interatomic Potentials
作者：Jonas Busk、Emil J. P. Frost、Yogeshwaran Krishnan、Henrik H. Kristoffersen、August E. G. Mikkelsen、Xueping Qin 等 10 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-22

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：与传统的从头计算方法相比，机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 能够在更长的时间尺度上模拟更大的原子系统，但需要大量、多样化的训练数据。现有数据集缺乏对固液界面的全面采样，这些界面表现出复杂的现象，例如对于能源和催化材料至关重要的溶剂分层和表面重建。在这里，我们展示了 SoLiD26，这是一个精心策划的固液界面数据集，包含 1540 万个第一性原理结构，最多包含 576 个原子和 15 种化学元素，用于训练和评估 MLIP。这些结构是根据密度…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

与传统的从头计算方法相比，机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 能够在更长的时间尺度上模拟更大的原子系统，但需要大量、多样化的训练数据。现有数据集缺乏对固液界面的全面采样，这些界面表现出复杂的现象，例如对于能源和催化材料至关重要的溶剂分层和表面重建。在这里，我们展示了 SoLiD26，这是一个精心策划的固液界面数据集，包含 1540 万个第一性原理结构，最多包含 576 个原子和 15 种化学元素，用于训练和评估 MLIP。这些结构是根据密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算得出的，大多数配置源自从头算分子动力学 (AIMD) 模拟。 SoLiD26 包括水性造币金属界面、电极-电解质系统和选定的块体参考结构，使用 PBE 函数和 D3 色散校正通过 VASP 进行计算。 SoLiD26 在训练和评估 MLIP 方面的应用通过一套 MACE 模型在简单的训练、验证和测试拆分上进行了演示。该数据集为推进 MLIP 开发提供了宝贵的资源，该开发旨在对电化学和催化系统中的固液界面进行建模。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable simulations of larger atomistic systems over longer timescales than traditional ab initio methods but require large, diverse training data. Existing datasets lack comprehensive sampling of solid-liquid interfaces, which exhibit complex phenomena such as solvent layering and surface reconstruction critical for energy and catalytic materials. Here, we present SoLiD26, a curated solid-liquid interface dataset of 15.4 million first-principles structures with up to 576 atoms and 15 chemical elements for training and evaluating MLIPs. The structures were compiled from density functional theory (DFT) calculations, with most configurations originating from ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations. SoLiD26 includes aqueous coinage metal interfaces, electrode-electrolyte systems, and selected bulk reference structures, calculated with VASP using the PBE functional and D3 dispersion corrections. The application of SoLiD26 for training and evaluating MLIPs is demonstrated with a suite of MACE models on a simple training, validation and test split. This dataset provides a valuable resource for advancing MLIP development targeted at modelling solid-liquid interfaces in electrochemical and catalytic systems.

ZnCl2 电解质溶液中的浓度依赖性形态：机器学习原子间势的可转移性和 EXAFS 的形态敏感性#

原题：Concentration-Dependent Speciation in ZnCl2 Electrolyte Solutions: Transferability of Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials and Speciation Sensitivity of EXAFS

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

预测浓电解质中的形态需要准确的离子相互作用和对缓慢相互转换的配位环境进行充分采样。我们使用机器学习原子间势将 Zn K 边扩展 X 射线吸收精细结构 (EXAFS) 测量与长时标分子动力学 (MD) 相结合，研究从稀释条件到 30 m 的 ZnCl2 水溶液。我们训练基于 revPBE 的 DeepMD 模型，其中包含或排除含 Zn 对的 D3 校正，并将它们与 DPMD-SCAN、经典 MD (CMD) 和 MACE-MATPES-r2SCAN 进行比较。 EXAFS 计算以水合和氯化物配位 Zn 参考络合物为基准，然后应用于 MD 系综以及 Zn-Cl 配位自由能分析。增加浓度会减少八面体配位，用氯化物代替水，并促进氯化物桥联。所有三个 DeepMD 模型在 3 m 处均产生类似的 EXAFS，但 DPMD-SCAN 显示出更大的跨浓度可转移性，再现了测量的光谱趋势、混合 ZnCl2/LiCl 光谱的关键特征以及与其他研究一致的八面体分数。 DFT 处理强烈影响配位自由能和聚集；排除与 Zn 相关的 D3 修正并不能系统地改善 EXAFS 一致性。 CMD 显示出较大的光谱差异和不太一致的协调趋势。 MACE-MATPES-r2SCAN 在 CMD 的基础上进行了改进，产生的光谱可与 DPMD-SCAN 相媲美，从而提供更一致的一致性和更高的计算效率。邻域解析分析表明，距离无序和散射路径干扰可以为不同的配位系综产生类似的 EXAFS 特征，这突出表明需要严格采样和系综平均光谱来区分分离的和氯桥 Zn 物种。

English Abstract

Predicting speciation in concentrated electrolytes requires accurate ion interactions and adequate sampling of slowly interconverting coordination environments. We combine Zn K-edge extended X-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) measurements with long-timescale molecular dynamics (MD) using machine-learning interatomic potentials to study aqueous ZnCl2 from dilute conditions to 30 m. We train revPBE-based DeepMD models with D3 corrections included or excluded for Zn-containing pairs and compare them with DPMD-SCAN, classical MD (CMD), and MACE-MATPES-r2SCAN. EXAFS calculations are benchmarked against hydrated and chloride-coordinated Zn reference complexes, then applied to MD ensembles alongside Zn-Cl coordination free-energy analysis. Increasing concentration reduces octahedral coordination, replaces water with chloride, and promotes chloride-bridged association. All three DeepMD models yield similar EXAFS at 3 m, but DPMD-SCAN shows greater transferability across concentrations, reproducing measured spectral trends, key features of mixed ZnCl2/LiCl spectra, and octahedral fractions consistent with other studies. DFT treatment strongly affects coordination free energies and aggregation; excluding Zn-related D3 corrections does not systematically improve EXAFS agreement. CMD shows larger spectral discrepancies and less consistent coordination trends. MACE-MATPES-r2SCAN improves upon CMD and yields spectra comparable to DPMD-SCAN, which provides more consistent agreement and greater computational efficiency. Neighbor-resolved analysis shows that distance disorder and scattering-path interference can produce similar EXAFS signatures for distinct coordination ensembles, highlighting the need for rigorous sampling and ensemble-averaged spectra to distinguish isolated from chloride-bridged Zn species.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在恒定电势下用显式溶剂模拟带电金属-水界面对于理解电化学过程至关重要，但从头计算方法仍然非常昂贵。我们推出了 TRECI，这是一种数据高效的工作流程，用于构建机器学习力场 (ML-FF)，可在电子大规范分子动力学中实现从头开始的精度。通过利用通用和特定领域模型的迁移学习，TRECI 使用更少的参考配置在广泛的潜在范围内实现稳定和准确的模拟。这种效率允许使用高级元 GGA 函数和严格的表面电气化方案。应用于 Cu(111)-水时，仅经过 1000 种配置训练的模型就可以产生准确的分子动力学模拟，捕获先前未报道的依赖于偏差的溶剂重组效应。 TRECI 提供了表征不同材料和界面化学的通用策略，显着降低了实际恒电位模拟的成本，并扩大了定量电化学建模的范围。

English Abstract

Simulating electrified metal-water interfaces with explicit solvent under constant potential is essential for understanding electrochemical processes, yet remains prohibitively expensive with ab initio methods. We present TRECI, a data-efficient workflow for constructing machine learning force-fields (ML-FFs) that achieve ab initio-level accuracy in electronically grand-canonical molecular dynamics. By leveraging transfer learning from general-purpose and domain-specific models, TRECI enables stable and accurate simulations across a wide potential range using a reduced number of reference configurations. This efficiency allows the use of high-level meta-GGA functionals and rigorous surface-electrification schemes. Applied to Cu(111)-water, models trained on just one thousand configurations yield accurate molecular dynamics simulations, capturing bias-dependent solvent restructuring effects not previously reported. TRECI offers a general strategy for characterising diverse materials and interfacial chemistries, significantly lowering the cost of realistic constant-potential simulations and expanding access to quantitative electrochemical modelling.

中文摘要

多孔硅（pSi）是一种非常有前途的纳米级热管理材料，但由于复杂的结构-性能依赖性，预测其导热率仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们将实验气体麦克风光声 (PA) 测量与原子分子动力学 (MD) 模拟和连续体级物理信息神经网络 (PINN) 相结合，对 pSi 中的热传输进行了全面的多尺度研究。通过高掺杂p+型硅的电化学蚀刻制备了孔隙率在48%至75%之间的介孔pSi层，并通过实验确定了它们的有效导热率。我们的研究结果表明，热性能从根本上是由初始衬底掺杂决定的，这决定了各向同性海绵状或各向异性柱状形态的形成。我们证明，MD 模拟通过明确考虑声子限制来准确捕获重掺杂样品的热行为，而 PINN 方法可以出色地模拟轻掺杂和中掺杂硅典型的随机网络中的渗流和宏观热通量。通过将这些计算和实验数据集与文献值相结合，我们利用符号回归推导出 pSi 有效导热系数作为孔隙率和温度函数的通用分析表达式。这种混合多尺度框架将纳米级声子力学与宏观热物理性质联系起来，为多孔硅基热电和隔热装置的设计提供了预测工具。

English Abstract

Porous silicon (pSi) is a highly promising material for nanoscale thermal management, but predicting its thermal conductivity remains challenging due to complex structure-property dependencies. In this work, we present a comprehensive multiscale investigation of heat transport in pSi, combining experimental gas-microphone photoacoustic (PA) measurements with atomistic Molecular Dynamics (MD) simulations and continuum-level Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs). Mesoporous pSi layers with porosities ranging from 48% to 75% were fabricated via electrochemical etching of highly doped p+-type silicon, and their effective thermal conductivity was experimentally determined. Our findings reveal that the thermal properties are fundamentally governed by the initial substrate doping, which dictates the formation of either isotropic sponge-like or anisotropic columnar morphologies. We demonstrate that MD simulations accurately capture the thermal behavior of heavily doped samples by explicitly accounting for phonon confinement, whereas the PINN approach excellently models percolation and macroscopic heat flux in stochastic networks typical of lightly and moderately doped silicon. By integrating these computational and experimental datasets with literature values, we utilized symbolic regression to derive a generalized analytical expression for the effective thermal conductivity of pSi as a function of porosity and temperature. This hybrid multiscale framework connects nanoscale phonon mechanics to macroscopic thermophysical properties, providing a predictive tool for the design of porous silicon-based thermoelectric and thermal-insulation devices.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

聚合物参考相互作用位点模型（PRISM）理论是 Ornstein-Zernike 液态理论的后代，是预测平衡聚合物体系的结构和热力学的有力工具，但其准确性和适用性在某些重要情况下可能受到限制。通常，这些缺点可以追溯到用于求解积分方程的解析闭包关系。在这里，我们提出了一种基于机器学习（ML）的闭合关系，在均聚物熔体和溶液的粗粒度分子动力学模拟数据集上进行训练。在预测典型粗粒度模型系统的结构方面，具有 ML 闭包的 PRISM 理论优于传统的原子闭包（例如 Percus-Yevick）。我们还使用 ML 闭包对小角度中子散射实验的结果进行精确建模。因此，这种机器学习增强的 PRISM 理论可以实现快速软材料发现和设计工作。

English Abstract

Polymer reference interaction site model (PRISM) theory, a descendant of Ornstein–Zernike liquid state theory, is a powerful tool to predict the structure and thermodynamics of equilibrium polymer systems, but its accuracy and applicability can be limited in some important cases. Typically, these shortcomings are traced to the analytical closure relationships used to solve the integral equations. Here, we propose a machine learning (ML)-based closure relation trained on a dataset of coarse-grained molecular dynamics simulations of homopolymer melts and solutions. PRISM theory with the ML closure outperforms traditional atomic closures (e.g., Percus–Yevick) in predicting the structure of typical coarse-grained model systems. We also use the ML closure to accurately model the results of small-angle neutron scattering experiments. This ML-enhanced PRISM theory can, therefore, enable rapid soft materials discovery and design efforts.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

预测晶体结构如何影响高温稳定性仍然是材料建模和设计中的一个关键挑战。碳化硅 (SiC) 是已知的热稳定性和化学稳定性最高的材料之一，为研究这一问题提供了理想的系统，因为它的许多多型体保留了相似的局部四面体键合，但长程堆叠几何形状不同。为了确定多型结构对 SiC 高温稳定性的影响，我们将相共存机器学习分子动力学与有限温度声子分析相结合，通过微调的 MACE 原子间势来实现，该势能准确描述多种 SiC 多型的晶体、高温和无序构型。我们确定了一个清晰的稳定性排序，3C > 2H > 9R，这在结构无序、层间滑动和有限温度声子谱中得到了一致反映。在所有多型体中，熔化是通过形成短 C-C 接触和富含碳的局部区域开始的，随后四面体 Si-C 连接性逐渐丧失。长周期9R多型体的稳定性降低与其堆积周期性和相关区域折叠产生的更密集的低频横向声学模式有关，这些区域折叠在高温下进一步展宽和软化。这些模式可以为热激活的横向双层运动提供额外的低能量通道，与 9R 中观察到的较大滑动幅度及其与局部结构紊乱的正相关性一致。

English Abstract

Predicting how crystal structure influences high-temperature stability remains a key challenge in materials modeling and design. Silicon carbide (SiC), one of the most thermally and chemically stable materials known, provides an ideal system for studying this problem because its many polytypes preserve similar local tetrahedral bonding while differing in long-range stacking geometry. To determine the impact of polytype structure on the high-temperature stability of SiC, we combine phase-coexistence machine-learning molecular dynamics with finite-temperature phonon analysis, enabled by a fine-tuned MACE interatomic potential that accurately describes crystalline, high-temperature, and disordered configurations across multiple SiC polytypes. We identify a clear stability ordering, 3C > 2H > 9R, reflected consistently in structural disordering, interlayer sliding, and finite-temperature phonon spectra. Across all polytypes, melting initiates through the formation of short C–C contacts and carbon-rich local regions, followed by a progressive loss of tetrahedral Si–C connectivity. The reduced stability of the long-period 9R polytype is associated with a denser manifold of low-frequency transverse-acoustic modes arising from its stacking periodicity and associated zone folding, which broaden and soften further at high temperature. These modes can provide additional low-energy channels for thermally activated lateral bilayer motion, consistent with the larger sliding amplitudes observed in 9R and their positive correlation with local structural disorder.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于图神经网络（ GNN ）的机器学习原子间势已成为势能面（ PES ）的第一原理精度例程。它们的主干，通常是O (3) -在设计上等价的，越来越多地用于学习其他原子特性。其中许多具有高角度顺序或假张量特征，定义为每个原子，或在大原子中心的基准集上扩展。这种靶标会使等变量GNN产生应变，其张量积随着角度顺序的变化而急剧增长，如果被截断，则可能无法代表靶标，从而迫使超参数之间难以权衡。像点边变换器（ PET ）这样的无约束模型用边对边注意力代替显式O （ 3 ）代数，并从数据中学习等方差，以便相同的骨干原则上可以学习任何等变特性。我们演示了如何使用PET来学习分子偶极子和极化率、核磁共振化学屏蔽张量以及电子密度的辅助基础膨胀。系统地增加主干尺寸可以提高精度和等方差误差，有针对性的架构和训练选择可以进一步减少对称性突破。PET以较低的推理成本匹配或改进已发布的参考模型，并且与等变量模型相比，内存占用更小。预测给出了准确的红外和拉曼光谱和各向同性屏蔽；预测的密度加速了自洽场计算并给出了一次性基态特性，并且通过对少量数据进行微调，预训练的密度模型转移到更高的理论水平。对于大多数可观测对象来说，近似等方差的影响很小，这进一步验证了这种方法可以为PES以外的属性训练快速、准确和有用的替代模型。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials based on graph neural networks (GNNs) have made first-principles accuracy routine for the potential energy surface (PES). Their backbones, often O(3)-equivariant by design, are increasingly used to learn other atomistic properties. Many of these are of high angular order or pseudotensorial character, defined per atom, or expanded on large atom-centered basis sets. Such targets strain equivariant GNNs, whose tensor products grow steeply in cost with angular order and can fail to represent the target if truncated, forcing difficult trade-offs between hyperparameters. Unconstrained models like the Point-Edge Transformer (PET) instead replace explicit O(3) algebra with edge-to-edge attention and learn equivariance from data, so that the same backbone can in principle learn any equivariant property. We demonstrate how to use PET to learn molecular dipoles and polarizabilities, NMR chemical shielding tensors, and auxiliary-basis expansions of the electron density. Increasing the backbone size systematically improves both accuracy and equivariance error, and targeted architectural and training choices reduce the symmetry breaking further. PET matches or improves on the released reference models at lower inference cost and, compared with equivariant models, a smaller memory footprint. The predictions give accurate IR and Raman spectra and isotropic shieldings; the predicted densities accelerate self-consistent field calculations and give one-shot ground-state properties, and pretrained density models transfer to higher levels of theory by fine-tuning on little data. The impact of approximate equivariance is small for most observables, further validating this approach to train fast, accurate, and useful surrogate models for properties beyond the PES.

具有挑战性的无序碳原子基础模型#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基础机器学习原子间势（ MLIP ）具有广泛的可转移性，但其域外可靠性很难确定。在这里，我们使用跨越多个阶段的纯含O/H无序碳、竞争性杂交和多孔框架来对六种流行的基础MLIP进行压力测试。我们首先筛选所有六个关于密度泛函理论（ DFT ）的能量和力误差和结合曲线，然后对四个进行熔融淬火-退火分子动力学，并评估所得到的结构可观测值。绑定曲线和包含O/H的轨迹揭示了低DFT能量和力误差未能揭示的非物理外推和动态失效。在测试的模型中， MACE-MH-1是最稳定的，具有低DFT误差和稳定的MD。尽管架构不同，但基于OMAT的模型共享结构偏差，突出了培训覆盖率的重要性。所有四个模型都会产生纳米多孔碳的结构，再现主要的实验和专门的碳电位特征。结合O/H使短期秩序基本保持不变，但将孔径分布转向更大的孔隙。这些结果强调了对特定应用验证的需求，并将纳米多孔碳的分子动力学建模扩展到实验上逼真的结构。

English Abstract

Foundation machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) promise broad transferability, yet their out-of-domain reliability is hard to establish. Here, we use pure and O/H-containing disordered carbon spanning multiple phases, competing hybridisations and porous frameworks to stress-test six popular foundation MLIPs. We first screen all six on density functional theory (DFT) energy and force errors and binding curves, then subject four to melt-quench-anneal molecular dynamics and evaluate the resulting structural observables. Binding curves and O/H-containing trajectories reveal unphysical extrapolation and dynamical failures that low DFT energy and force errors fail to reveal. Of the models tested, MACE-MH-1 is the most consistently robust, with low DFT errors and stable MD. Despite differing architectures, OMAT-based models share structural biases, highlighting the importance of training coverage. All four models generate structures of nanoporous carbon that reproduce the principal experimental and specialised carbon potential signatures. Incorporating O/H leaves short-range order largely unchanged but shifts pore-size distributions towards larger pores. These results emphasise the need for application-specific validation and extend molecular dynamics modelling of nanoporous carbon towards experimentally realistic structures.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了 NEP-kappa 2.0，这是一个统一的框架，集成了密度泛函理论、特定材料的机器学习原子间势以及用于高级声子计算的预训练力模型。通用工作流程可实现谐波晶格动力学、准谐波和随机自洽声子处理、三声子和四声子输运、维格纳布居相干输运以及时间相关声子动力学。在 3C-SiC、BA 和 Cs2AgInCl6 上的应用展示了一致的力模型比较、有限温度重整化、高阶散射和模间相干性。除了稳态传输之外，选择性激发 3C-SiC 的模式解析模拟表明，注入的光学声子能量的一半在大约 3.75 ps 内达到声学模式，主要是通过一光学 - 二声学散射。声学分支的响应时间相差五倍，竞争性散射通道会产生瞬态能量超调。通道分辨的熵产生和持续的非热占据将分支间能量重新分配与分支内热化区分开来。该框架将平衡声子输运与微观非平衡能量动力学联系起来。

English Abstract

We present NEP-kappa 2.0, a unified framework that integrates density-functional theory, material-specific machine-learning interatomic potentials, and pretrained force models for advanced phonon calculations. A common workflow enables harmonic lattice dynamics, quasi-harmonic and stochastic self-consistent phonon treatments, three- and four-phonon transport, Wigner population--coherence transport, and time-dependent phonon dynamics. Applications to 3C-SiC, BAs, and Cs₂AgInCl₆ demonstrate consistent force-model comparisons, finite-temperature renormalization, higher-order scattering, and intermode coherence. Beyond steady-state transport, mode-resolved simulations of selectively excited 3C-SiC reveal that half of the injected optical-phonon energy reaches acoustic modes within approximately 3.75 ps, primarily through one-optical--two-acoustic scattering. Acoustic branches exhibit response times differing by a factor of five, with competing scattering channels producing a transient energy overshoot. Channel-resolved entropy production and persistent nonthermal occupations distinguish interbranch energy redistribution from intrabranch thermalization. The framework connects equilibrium phonon transport with microscopic nonequilibrium energy dynamics.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Rb2SO4、Rb2Ca2(SO4)3 和 Ba3Lu(BO3)3 是结构复杂的材料，其热膨胀已在高温 X 射线衍射的基础上根据特定的多面体重排进行了解释。我们使用分子动力学研究它们的晶格动力学及其对热和电子性质的影响，并使用密度泛函理论数据训练的主动学习矩张量势。对于 β-Rb2SO4、Rb2Ca2(SO4)3 和 Ba₃Lu(BO3)3，非谐性测度 σ^A（将原子间作用力的非谐性贡献与作用在原子上的总力进行比较）达到约 0.3-0.5，对于取向无序的 α-Rb2SO4，非谐性测度约为 0.75，虚部谐波模式为Rb2Ca2(SO4)3和α-Rb2SO4在有限温度下稳定。模拟再现了测得的热膨胀并支持衍射提出的机制：β-Rb2SO4中沿c的膨胀是由波纹Rb链的伸长和拉直引起的，BO3重新取向伴随着Ba3Lu(BO3)3的各向异性膨胀，多面体连接的弯曲限制了沿弱膨胀方向和立方体Rb2Ca2(SO4)3的膨胀。 Green-Kubo 晶格热导率在具有可比 σ^A 的化合物之间相差超过一个数量级，这表明非谐性测量本身并不能决定热传输。声子引起的带隙重整化（包括晶格热膨胀）在 Rb2Ca2(SO4)3 和 Ba3Lu(BO3)3 中在 1250 K 下达到 2.7 和 1.7 eV，在 β-Rb2SO4 中在 650 K 下达到 1.4 eV，零点重整化分别为 0.64、0.70 和β-Rb2SO4、Rb2Ca2(SO4)3 和 Ba3Lu(BO3)3 分别为 0.31 eV。

English Abstract

Rb₂SO₄, Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃, and Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ are structurally complex materials whose thermal expansion has been interpreted, on the basis of high-temperature x-ray diffraction, in terms of specific polyhedral rearrangements. We study their lattice dynamics and its consequences for thermal and electronic properties using molecular dynamics with actively learned moment tensor potentials trained on density-functional theory data. The anharmonicity measure σ^A (compares the anharmonic contribution to the interatomic forces with the total forces acting on atoms) reaches approx0.3-0.5 for β-Rb₂SO₄, Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃, and Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ and approx0.75 for orientationally disordered α-Rb₂SO₄, and the imaginary harmonic modes of Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃ and α-Rb₂SO₄ are stabilized at finite temperature. The simulations reproduce the measured thermal expansion and support the mechanisms proposed from diffraction: expansion along c in β-Rb₂SO₄ arises from both elongation and straightening of corrugated Rb chains, BO₃ reorientation accompanies the anisotropic expansion of Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃, and bending of polyhedral linkages limits expansion along the weakly expanding directions and in cubic Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃. Green-Kubo lattice thermal conductivities differ by more than an order of magnitude between compounds with comparable σ^A, showing that the anharmonicity measure alone does not determine heat transport. Phonon-induced band-gap renormalization, including lattice thermal expansion, reaches 2.7 and 1.7 eV at 1250 K in Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃ and Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ and 1.4 eV at 650 K in β-Rb₂SO₄, with zero-point renormalizations of 0.64, 0.70, and 0.31 eV for β-Rb₂SO₄, Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃, and Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃, respectively.

理解通用机器学习原子间势中的压缩-扩展误差不对称性#

原题：Understanding compression-extension error asymmetry in universal machine-learning interatomic potentials

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通用机器学习原子间势越来越多地应用于各种材料，但其在定向变形下的可靠性仍不清楚。我们比较了十一个预训练势沿来自相同父结构的成对变形路径的压缩和延伸。该基准包括 1,963 个材料项目轨迹 (MPtrj) 母体和 1,286 个实验来源的 MinX-Eq 母体，其应变大小从 1% 到 10% 有 6 个量级。密度泛函理论为 MPtrj 中的各向同性、单轴和双轴载荷以及 MinX-Eq 中的各向同性载荷提供了参考。在 ± 10% 时，每个模型在压缩下都具有较大的中值力误差，MPtrj 和 MinX-Eq 的合并各向同性压缩与拉伸比率分别为 2.12 和 3.30。将误差增长分解为 DFT 力尺度增长和相对误差演化表明，两个分支上的相对误差均减少，在 MPtrj 中压缩时速度更快，但在 MinX-Eq 中压缩时速度更慢。尽管 MinX-Eq 保留了跨模型的压缩侧过剩，但力尺度匹配使误差率趋于统一。径向和 SOAP 分析将压缩结构置于较短的间隔和远离公共参考池的位置，而与读出相关的模型特征显示更大的压缩侧位移。这些结果将更强的物理响应与相对预测准确性的变化区分开来，并为评估变形模拟中的普遍潜力提供了基础。

English Abstract

Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials are increasingly used across diverse materials, yet their reliability under directional deformation remains unclear. We compare compression and extension along paired deformation paths from the same parent structures for eleven pretrained potentials. The benchmark comprises 1,963 Materials Project trajectory (MPtrj) parents and 1,286 experimental-source MinX-Eq parents at six strain magnitudes from 1% to 10%. Density-functional theory provides references for isotropic, uniaxial and biaxial loading in MPtrj and isotropic loading in MinX-Eq. At ± 10%, every model has a larger median force error under compression, with pooled isotropic compression-to-extension ratios of 2.12 and 3.30 for MPtrj and MinX-Eq. Decomposing error growth into DFT force-scale growth and relative-error evolution shows that relative errors decrease on both branches, faster under compression in MPtrj but slower under compression in MinX-Eq. Force-scale matching moves the error ratios toward unity, although MinX-Eq retains a compression-side excess across models. Radial and SOAP analyses place compressed structures at shorter separations and farther from public reference pools, while readout-associated model features show larger compression-side displacements. These results distinguish stronger physical responses from changes in relative predictive accuracy and provide a basis for assessing universal potentials in deformation simulations.

使用机器学习和大型语言模型自动提取材料数据和知识#

原题：Automated Materials Data and Knowledge Extraction using Machine Learning and Large Language Models

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在过去的十年中，人们致力于开发机器学习 (ML) 方法，以直接从科学文献中提取材料数据（如果可能）和材料知识。本综述追溯了这些方法的演变，从启发式、基于规则的管道，到自然语言处理 (NLP) 模型和基于转换器的编码器，最后到大型语言模型 (LLM) 和能够跨多种模式（包括文本、表格和图形）提取材料信息的代理系统。除了构建支持下游材料发现的大型可查询材料数据库和知识图之外，我们还讨论了这些进步如何实现新功能，例如跨材料科学的通用问答、用于研究协助的人工智能联合科学家和聊天机器人，以及与自主实验室集成时的闭环优化和发现。我们严格评估使用这些方法生成的数据集、它们的提取精度和计算成本，以及不同方法各自的优点和局限性，甚至为开发更可靠和可扩展的材料数据提取管道提供实用指南。最后，我们确定了关键的开放挑战，包括在样本水平上提取完整的材料结构-性能-加工-性能四面体、缺乏用于系统评估的可靠基准数据集，以及跨不同文档模式的非标准材料术语的解决。

English Abstract

Over the past decade, substantial efforts have been devoted to developing machine learning (ML) methods for extracting materials data and, where possible, materials knowledge directly from the scientific literature. This review traces the evolution of these methods from heuristic, rule-based pipelines through natural language processing (NLP) models and transformer-based encoders to, lastly, large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems capable of extracting materials information across multiple modalities, including text, tables, and figures. Beside construction of large, queryable materials databases and knowledge graphs that support downstream materials discovery, we discuss how these advances have enabled new capabilities, such as, generic question answering across materials science, AI co-scientists and chatbots for research assistance, and closed-loop optimization and discovery when integrated with autonomous laboratories. We critically assess the datasets generated using these approaches, their extraction accuracy and computational costs, and the respective strengths and limitations of different methodologies, and even provide practical guidelines for developing more reliable and scalable materials data extraction pipelines. Finally, we identify key open challenges, including the extraction of full materials structure-property-processing-performance tetrahedron at the sample level, the scarcity of reliable benchmark datasets for systematic evaluation, and the resolution of non-standard materials terminology across different document modalities.

中文摘要

机器学习已经渗透到材料优化中，提供了高价值。然而，它对大量样本的依赖限制了它对相对探索的材料族的适用性。在本文中，我们通过 PYTHIA（概率可转移分层交互架构）框架将机器学习的适用性扩展到新颖和待研究的材料。 Pythia 利用两个最强大的机器学习概念：基础模型和迁移学习。我们采用一个复杂的基础模型，在化学相似分子的大型数据集上进行训练，其目标在物理上接近我们的预测目标。然后，我们冻结权重并使用变换器层执行子空间对齐，并使用高斯过程添加不确定性量化。这两层可以在有限的数据集上进行训练，但允许我们利用所获得的知识以及所研究的材料和复杂的架构。所得模型用作贝叶斯优化方案的替代模型。为了展示该框架的稳健性，我们将其应用于两个不同系统的吸附和缺陷优化的研究。我们不仅能够正确有效地识别甚至复杂的a的最佳吸附位点

English Abstract

Machine learning has permeated material optimization, providing high value. Nevertheless, its re- liance on a high number of samples limits its applicability to relatively explored material families. In this paper we extend the applicability of machine learning to novel and understudied materials through the PYTHIA (Probabilistic Yet Transferable Hierarchical Interaction Architecture) framework. Pythia utilizes two of the most powerful machine learning concepts, foundational models and transfer learning. We employ a complex foundational model trained on a large dataset of chemically similar molecules, on a target that is physically close to our prediction target. Then, we freeze the weights and perform subspace alignment with the use of a transformer layer and add uncertainty quantification with gaussian processes. These two layers can be trained on a limited dataset yet allow us to leverage the gained knowl- edge and the studied materials and complex architectures. The resulting model is used as a surrogate for a Bayesian Optimization scheme. To showcase the robustness of the framework, we applied it to the investigation of adsorption and defect optimization of two distinct systems. 页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

评估 X 射线束穿过聚合物复合材料的能量透射比是辐射屏蔽研究中的一项具有挑战性的任务。蒙特卡罗输运模拟的参考解决方案非常准确，但计算成本昂贵：我们的每个参考模拟的上限为 4×10⁹ 光子历史和 20,000 秒（超过 5 小时）的时间限制，而封闭式分析方法无法代表如此复杂的场景。我们提出了一种人工神经网络模型，可在 1-3 毫秒内将无序多组分子成分、它们的质量分数和四个体物理描述符直接映射到传输比 I(t)/I₀。该模型是一个 Set Transformer，它通过构造使其排列不变，并接受固定大小的填充集中最多 11 个成分的不同大小的混合。每个成分都嵌入了化学预训练的 Transformer 语言模型 ChemBERTa。在我们提出的方法中，训练数据是由自动 PENELOPE/penEasy 管道使用 30、80、100 和 120 kV 管电压的辐射源在厚度跨度为 0.15-2.5 mm 的聚乙烯基屏蔽复合板上生成的。预处理后，数据集包含 n = 4697 个模拟配置，在其上执行无泄漏、联合分层 10 倍交叉验证，获得均方误差 MSE = (1.12 ± 0.56)×10⁻⁴、归一化 MSE 百分比 NMSE% = 0.0294% ± 0.0147% 和 R² = 0.9988。相应的每个样本百分比误差的 alpha 修正 (α = 0.10) 绝对百分比误差 (APE) 为 1.66%，中位 APE 为 1.15%。我们模型的推理速度比底层蒙特卡罗模拟快几个数量级（每个查询的时间为亚毫秒，而参考运行的时间为几个小时），并且可以有效准确地预测 I(t)/I₀。

English Abstract

Evaluating the energy transmission ratio of an X-ray beam through a polymer composite is a challenging task in radiation shielding studies. The reference solution with Monte Carlo transport simulation is highly accurate but computationally expensive: each of our reference simulations is capped at 4×10⁹ photon histories and a 20,000 s (over 5 hours) time limit, while closed-form analytical methods fail to represent such complex scenarios. We present an artificial neural network model that maps the unordered multi-set of molecular constituents, their mass fractions, and four bulk physical descriptors directly to the transmission ratio I(t)/I₀ in 1-3 milliseconds. The model is a Set Transformer, which makes it permutation invariant by construction and accepts mixtures of different sizes up to 11 constituents within a fixed-size padded set. Each constituent is embedded with a chemistry-pretrained transformer language model, ChemBERTa. In our proposed method, training data are produced by an automated PENELOPE/penEasy pipeline using the radiation source at 30, 80, 100, and 120 kV tube voltages over polyethylene-based shielding composite slabs of thicknesses spanning 0.15-2.5 mm. After preprocessing, the dataset contains n = 4697 simulated configurations on which a leakage-free, jointly stratified 10-fold cross-validation is performed, attaining mean squared error MSE = (1.12 ± 0.56)×10⁻⁴, normalized-MSE percentage NMSE% = 0.0294% ± 0.0147%, and R² = 0.9988. The corresponding per-sample percentage errors give an alpha-trimmed (α = 0.10) absolute percentage error (APE) of 1.66% and a median APE of 1.15%. Our model's inference is several orders of magnitude faster than the underlying Monte Carlo simulation (sub-millisecond per query vs. several hours for the reference run) with effective accuracy in predicting I(t)/I₀.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在人工智能和机器人技术在实验科学中日益融合的推动下，我们通过开发无需现场工作人员即可操作的高度自动化终端站，重新定义了同步加速器软 X 射线束线的用户实验的执行。该系统安装在 NanoTerasu 的光束线 BL08U 上，由四个室单元和两个机器人组成，可自动将样品从环境空气转移到超高真空测量台进行光谱评估，然后在测量后将样品返回到原始位置。一旦通过在指定样品区域上扫描微聚焦光束进行编程，就会自动获取软 X 射线吸收光谱，然后使用机器学习技术（例如主成分分析、多维缩放、t 分布随机邻域嵌入以及均匀流形逼近和投影）进行自动评估。高通量数据系统地积累在数据库中，供远程用户检查。这种无人值守的终端站为用户提供高效的射束时间，并允许射束线科学家/技术人员追求更多的人性化和创造性的努力。

English Abstract

Driven by the growing integration of artificial intelligence and robotics in experimental science, we redefine the execution of user experiments at synchrotron soft X-ray beamlines by developing a highly automated end-station that can be operated without on-site staff. Installed at beamline BL08U of NanoTerasu, the system comprises four chamber units and two robots that automatically transfer samples from ambient air to an ultrahigh vacuum measurement stage for spectroscopic evaluations, before returning them to their original locations post-measurement. Soft X-ray absorption spectra are automatically acquired once programmed by scanning the micro-focused beam over the designated sample area, followed by automated evaluation using machine learning techniques such as principal-component analysis, multidimensional scaling, t-distributed stochastic neighbour embedding, and uniform manifold approximation and projection. The high-throughput data are systematically accumulated in the database for remote user examination. This unstaffed end-station provides efficient beamtime for users and allows beamline scientists/technicians to pursue more human and creative endeavors.

从表面反应到抑制剂覆盖：区域选择性原子层沉积的机器学习辅助计算研究#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

区域选择性原子层沉积 (AS-ALD) 能够实现选择性材料生长，用于制造纳米级半导体器件。预测抑制剂覆盖率对于理解和优化 AS-ALD 中的表面封闭至关重要。在这里，我们提出了一种计算工作流程，将预训练的通用机器学习原子间势与晶格外正则蒙特卡罗模拟相结合，并将其应用于非晶 SiO2 上的抑制剂吸附和前体阻断。真实的表面模型揭示了反应能量和活化势垒的显着位点依赖性变化，其中硅烷醇基团充当氨基硅烷和相关抑制剂和金属前体的主要吸附位点。覆盖模拟表明，产生相同吸附片段的抑制剂可以产生不同的覆盖范围，因为完整的分子施加不同的空间限制，导致不同的可接近位点密度和阻断能力。除了覆盖范围之外，我们还强调了吸附的抑制剂片段对前体的反应性在确定抑制剂稳定性和阻断性能方面的重要性。总之，这些发现表明需要考虑现实非晶表面上的空间效应和化学反应性。我们的计算将局部反应化学与抑制剂覆盖率联系起来，并为理解抑制机制和为 AS-ALD 选择合适的抑制剂-前体组合提供计算基础。

English Abstract

Area-selective atomic layer deposition (AS-ALD) enables selective material growth for the fabrication of nanoscale semiconductor devices. Predicting inhibitor coverage is essential for understanding and optimizing surface blocking in AS-ALD. Here, we present a computational workflow combining a pretrained universal machine-learning interatomic potential with off-lattice grand canonical Monte Carlo simulations and apply it to inhibitor adsorption and precursor blocking on amorphous SiO2. Realistic surface models reveal substantial site-dependent variations in reaction energies and activation barriers, with silanol groups serving as the primary adsorption sites for aminosilane and related inhibitors and metal precursors. Coverage simulations show that inhibitors producing identical adsorbed fragments can yield different coverages because the intact molecules impose distinct steric constraints, leading to different accessible site densities and blocking capabilities. Beyond coverage, we highlight the importance of the reactivity of adsorbed inhibitor fragments toward precursors in determining inhibitor stability and blocking performance. Together, these findings demonstrate the need to consider both steric effects and chemical reactivity on realistic amorphous surfaces. Our calculations connect local reaction chemistry with inhibitor coverage and provide a computational basis for understanding inhibition mechanisms and selecting suitable inhibitor-precursor combinations for AS-ALD.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

X 射线和中子散射对于凝聚态物理和材料科学的结构表征是不可或缺的。然而，由于散射实验通常记录没有相位信息的强度，不同的结构可以产生相似的散射图案，从而使得相应的反演问题不唯一。通过组合互补测量可以减少这种模糊性，但将它们集成到机器学习框架中仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍一个基于先验摊销流量匹配的概率多测量分析的模块化框架。单观测后验估计器针对各个观测类型或物理探针单独进行训练，并仅在推理时通过组合采样器进行组合。这种后期融合方法允许重复使用预训练的单观察模型，而无需专门的联合再训练。作为一个恰当的例子，我们考虑反射测量法，它提供有关薄膜和界面的深度分辨纳米级信息。我们的单次观测演示涵盖 X 射线、飞行时间中子和偏振中子反射计。在单独的协同细化研究中，任务匹配的估计器可以实现跨多个中子对比的均匀成分以及 X 射线和中子测量的异构多模态成分。总之，这些结果将成分流匹配建立为不确定性感知反演和协同细化的模块化方法，并具有对其他散射问题的潜在适用性，在其他散射问题中，多个观测值约束共同的物理结构，同时保留特定于测量的参数。

English Abstract

X-ray and neutron scattering are indispensable for structural characterization across condensed-matter physics and materials science. Yet, because scattering experiments generally record intensities without phase information, distinct structures can yield similar scattering patterns, rendering the corresponding inverse problems non-unique. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining complementary measurements, but their integration into machine-learning frameworks remains challenging. Here we introduce a modular framework for probabilistic multi-measurement analysis based on prior-amortized flow matching. Single-observation posterior estimators are trained separately for individual observation types or physical probes and combined only at inference time through a compositional sampler. This late fusion approach allows pretrained single-observation models to be reused without dedicated joint retraining. As a case in point, we consider reflectometry, which provides depth-resolved, nanometer-scale information about thin films and interfaces. Our single-observation demonstrations span X-ray, time-of-flight neutron and polarized neutron reflectometry. In separate co-refinement studies, task-matched estimators enable homogeneous composition across multiple neutron contrasts and heterogeneous multimodal composition of X-ray and neutron measurements. Together, these results establish compositional flow matching as a modular approach to uncertainty-aware inversion and co-refinement, with potential applicability to other scattering problems in which multiple observations constrain a common physical structure while retaining measurement-specific parameters.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

材料科学数据收集可能非常昂贵，因此数据集的重用和长期效用对于未来的发现非常重要。在实践中，数据收集通常会优先考虑当前感兴趣的属性，而其他属性则被排除在获取过程之外。这可以生成非常适合当前目标的数据集，但会丢失未来感兴趣的属性所需的信息。在这里，我们介绍如何构建为两者提供信息的数据集。我们开发和评估采购框架，将预测分歧与材料特征、预测结果或两者的多样性结合起来。我们在四个 DFT 数据集和一个实验热电数据集上测试了这些方法。数据集构建中的多样性可以通过在采集过程中保留更广泛的材料和属性行为来减少当前和未来目标的权衡。在所有数据集中，本工作中开发的具有分歧和特征多样性（NSGA-II Dis.+多样性）框架的非支配排序遗传算法 II 提供了当前和未来目标之间最稳健的平衡。它保持当前目标的预测性能高于随机采样，同时减轻排除属性的偏差，并且在许多情况下，提高其性能超越随机采样。这种具有多样性意识的采集框架提供了一条实用途径，可以通过公正的质量条目构建材料数据集，并减轻后续建模和发现活动中的冷启动限制。

English Abstract

Materials science data collection can be expensive, making dataset reuse and long-term utility important for future discovery. In practice, data collection often prioritizes properties of current interest while other properties are excluded from the acquisition process. This can produce datasets that are well suited for current objectives but lose information needed for properties of future interest. Here, we present how to construct datasets that remain informative for both. We develop and evaluate acquisition frameworks that combine predictive disagreement with diversity in material features, predicted outcomes, or both. We test these approaches across four DFT datasets and an experimental thermoelectric dataset. Diversity inclusion in dataset construction can reduce present and future objectives trade-off by preserving a broader range of materials and property behavior during acquisition. Across all datasets, the Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II with disagreements and feature diversity (NSGA-II Dis. + diversity) framework developed in this work provides the most robust balance between current and future objectives. It maintains prediction performance above random sampling for current objectives while mitigating bias on excluded properties and, in many cases, improving their performance beyond random sampling. This diversity-aware acquisition framework provides a practical route to building materials datasets with unbiased quality entries and mitigating cold-start limitations in subsequent modeling and discovery campaigns.

适用于合金的通用 MLIP 效果如何？基准仅与其控制一样值得信赖#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）根据 DFT 数据库的形成能量误差进行排名，就好像该数据库是真实的，但独立的 DFT 数据库不同意。我们将 145 万个有序金属间化合物的 14 个 uMLIP 与 AFLOW 进行了比较（没有经过任何训练），并将每个能量和稳定性误差与该分歧进行比较。对于一半的结构，AFLOW、Alexandria 和 OQMD 的差异小于 6-8 meV/原子，但少数结构的差异要大得多，将平均值提高到 16-30。这种分歧为任何模型的可测量误差设定了底限。重新参考，最好的 uMLIP 与 AFLOW 的一致性与 Alexandria 的一致，接下来的四个位于地板上方 2-7 meV/atom。一旦筛选出损坏的元素参考，能量和稳定性排名几乎在所有地方都一致。在正确的控制下，三个明显的模型故障会缩小或消失：相关性较差的剪切模量、数千个“不良”DFT 参考以及领先者的膨胀领先优势。

English Abstract

Universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) are ranked by their formation-energy error against a DFT database, as if that database were ground truth, but independent DFT databases disagree. We benchmark fourteen uMLIPs on 1.45 million ordered intermetallics against AFLOW, which none trained on, and compare every energy and stability error with that disagreement. For half the structures, AFLOW, Alexandria, and OQMD differ by less than 6-8 meV/atom, but a minority differ by far more, raising the average to 16-30. That disagreement sets a floor on any model's measurable error. Re-referenced, the best uMLIP agrees with AFLOW as closely as Alexandria does, and the next four sit 2-7 meV/atom above the floor. Once broken elemental references are screened, the energy and stability rankings agree almost everywhere. Three apparent model failures shrink or vanish under the right control: poorly correlated shear moduli, thousands of "bad" DFT references, and the leader's inflated lead.

CME-SpectrumBench：法学硕士可以分析凝聚态光谱数据吗？#

原题：CME-SpectrumBench: Can LLMs Analyze Condensed Matter Spectral Data?
作者：Jin Gene Wong、Anjney Midha、Joseph Mathew Tennyson、Wei-Lin Chiang、Ion Stoica、Zhi-Xun Shen。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-20

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

目前，大型语言模型 (LLM) 通过专家级数学和物理基准进行了很好的评估，这些基准侧重于理论和符号推理。然而，我们缺乏评估前沿研究问题中的数据分析的实验基准，而这是未来特定领域基础模型必须具备的能力。为此，我们推出了 CME-SpectrumBench，这是一个专家设计的基准，由 190 个问题组成，用于衡量前沿法学硕士在凝聚态实验 (CME) 中分析研究级光谱科学数据的可靠性。使用模拟的 CME 光谱数据，我们提示模型在面对噪声和有限的仪器分辨率时从 2D 强度阵列中识别结构，这是对密集上下文推理的测试。模型的评分标准是其数值答案接近真实情况的准确程度。在直接查询下，由于明显的故障模式，与手写代码相比，分数通常显得苍白：模型可以找到简单的特征，但很难跟踪模式或计算派生量，并且性能通常会随着数据分辨率和噪声/卷积级别的增加而下降。通过允许在有/无 ReAct 提示的情况下使用工具（访问 Python 解释器），LLM 的性能可与手写代码相媲美，实际上将问题映射到代理代码生成问题。我们的研究结果表明，前沿模型可以通过代码与数据交互来充当研究助手，但仍然面临直接处理来自大型数据集的信息的困难 - 这是未来基础模型必须克服的关键障碍。代码和数据可在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/jingene/CMESpectrumBench 上公开获取。

English Abstract

Large Language Models (LLMs) are presently well assessed by expert-level mathematics and physics benchmarks which focus on theoretical, symbolic reasoning. However, we lack experimental benchmarks that evaluate data analysis in frontier research problems, a capability that future domain-specific foundation models must have. To this end, we present CME-SpectrumBench, an expert-designed benchmark consisting of 190 questions that measures how reliably frontier LLMs analyze research-level spectral scientific data in condensed matter experiments (CME). Using simulated CME spectral data, we prompt models to identify structures from 2D intensity arrays in the face of noise and limited instrumental resolution, a test of dense context reasoning. Models are scored by how accurately their numerical answers approach the ground truth. Under direct query, scores generally pale in comparison to handwritten code due to clear failure modes: models can find simple features but struggle to trace patterns or compute derived quantities, and performance typically degrades with increased data resolution and noise/convolution levels. LLM performance becomes comparable to handwritten code by allowing tool use (access to a Python interpreter) with/without ReAct prompting, in effect mapping questions onto agentic code generation problems. Our findings show that frontier models can serve as research assistants by interfacing with data through code, but still face difficulties processing information directly from large datasets - a key barrier future foundation models must overcome. The code and data are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jingene/CMESpectrumBench.

从天气到增强风化：运营规模二氧化碳去除的天气基础模型#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

增强岩石风化 (ERW) 是一种碳去除解决方案，每年有可能去除数十亿吨二氧化碳，但如今量化这种去除量需要密集的土壤采样和实验室分析。我们根据留一字段输出协议对来自巴西和美国 264 个字段的 5,192 个时间序列评估了五种天气基础模型 (FM)，作为为轻量级解码器提供数据的冻结编码器。据我们所知，这是对战争遗留爆炸物气象FM的首次全面评估，也是对战争遗留爆炸物业务数据的最大规模评估。我们发现，跨天气 FM，表示转移到 ERW：我们最好的模型 TerraNova-ERW，基于 Aurora 1.5 构建，在它从未见过的领域内积极地进行测量。对新场均值去除的零样本预测尚不可行，我们将其归因于数据规模：相同的架构在 sim10⁵ 自然风化序列上表现出色，锚定了我们期望 ERW 数据必须达到的规模。现在支持少样本预测，将模型与每个字段的几个样本混合：TerraNova-ERW 通过每个字段两个样本恢复超过 60% 的最高移除字段，将估计字段平均移除所需的样本大致减半，并且可以将最密集字段的采样减少 28-98%。

English Abstract

Enhanced Rock Weathering (ERW) is a carbon removal solution with the potential to remove gigatons of CO₂ per year, but quantifying that removal today requires dense soil sampling and laboratory analysis. We evaluate five weather Foundation Models (FMs) as frozen encoders feeding lightweight decoders, under a leave-one-field-out protocol on 5,192 chronosequences from 264 fields across Brazil and the US. To our knowledge, this is the first holistic evaluation of weather FMs for ERW and the largest evaluation on operational ERW data. We find that across weather FMs, representations transfer to ERW: our best model, TerraNova-ERW, built on Aurora 1.5, positively orders measurements within fields it has never seen. Zero-shot prediction of a new field's mean removal is not yet feasible, and we attribute this to data scale: the same architecture excels on sim10⁵ natural-weathering sequences, anchoring the scale we expect ERW data must reach. Few-shot prediction, blending the model with a few samples per field, is supported today: TerraNova-ERW recovers over 60% of the highest-removal fields with two samples per field, roughly halves the samples needed to estimate a field's mean removal, and can cut sampling on our densest fields by 28-98%.

GBClaw：用于原子晶界研究和机制发现的代理人工智能框架#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

代理人工智能（AI）系统在材料发现和设计中复杂工作流程自动化方面具有巨大潜力，但将其扩展到领域密集型机械研究仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出了 GBClaw，一种用于研究一般晶界 (GB) 结构和变形的代理人工智能框架。尽管一般晶界构成了多晶材料中绝大多数现实世界的界面，但由于其结构复杂性和界面失配使实验和建模研究变得复杂，因此它们仍然未被充分研究。 GBClaw 将大型语言模型 (LLM) 集成到模块化架构中，其中包括协调器、专用代理、特定领域工具、模拟接口和分层内存。通过动态重新规划和迭代工作流程细化，该框架有效地将系统模拟与有针对性的机制分析联系起来。 GBClaw 应用于镍中的 14 个一般不对称倾斜晶界，描述了界面缺陷结构，并识别了这些不相称边界上的不同变形模式。比较有和没有二次晶界错配位错的边界滑动揭示了剪切强度的显着差异，强调了缺陷层次在滑动阻力中的关键作用。此外，研究人员引导的后续计算发现亚稳态边界结构具有异常高的抗剪力。这项工作表明，将代理人工智能与领域专业知识相结合，可以将高通量计算筛选与对复杂材料界面的深入物理理解联系起来。

English Abstract

Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems hold immense potential for automating complex workflows in materials discovery and design, but extending them to domain-intensive mechanistic investigations remains challenging. Here, we present GBClaw, an agentic AI framework for investigating the structure and deformation of general grain boundaries (GBs). Although general GBs constitute the vast majority of real-world interfaces in polycrystalline materials, they remain understudied because their structural complexity and interfacial mismatch complicate experimental and modeling research. GBClaw integrates large language models (LLMs) into a modular architecture comprising an orchestrator, specialized agents, domain-specific tools, simulation interfaces, and hierarchical memory. Through dynamic replanning and iterative workflow refinement, the framework effectively connects systematic simulations with targeted mechanistic analysis. Applied to 14 general asymmetric tilt GBs in nickel, GBClaw characterized interfacial defect structures and identified diverse deformation modes across these incommensurate boundaries. Comparing boundary sliding with and without secondary GB misfit dislocations revealed substantial differences in shear strength, underscoring the critical role of defect hierarchies in sliding resistance. Furthermore, researcher-guided follow-up calculations traced an anomalously high shear resistance to metastable boundary structures. This work demonstrates that combining agentic AI with domain expertise bridges high-throughput computational screening with deep physical understanding of complex material interfaces.

镁化合物的紧结合与机器学习潜力模型相结合#

原题：A Combined Tight Binding with Machine Learning Potential Model for Magnesium Compounds

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential、density functional theory、interatomic potential、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种基于镁的系统模型，该模型将密度泛函紧结合 (DFTB) 与 MACE（一种机器学习原子间势 (DFTB+MACE)）相结合。在此模型中，传统的排斥势、对势被多体 MACE 势所取代。该模型的 MACE 组件根据密度泛函理论 (DFT) 能量和力之间的差异以及相应的 DFTB 值进行训练，但忽略了对的潜在贡献。使用该模型，我们进行了 MgO-CO2 吸附系统的结构弛豫、水簇的分子动力学计算以及稳定 fcc-MgO 和亚稳态 bcc-MgO 结构的声子谱计算。我们将模型的性能与纯 MACE 模型和 DFT 进行比较。我们证明，相对于具有配对潜力的 DFTB，DFTB+MACE 模型实现了更高的准确性，在许多情况下，计算成本仅略有增加。此外，它还可以提供大多数机器学习潜力无法提供的电子结构。最初为 MACE 开发的训练数据集可能无法完全代表我们在模拟过程中可能遇到的潜在表面的所有区域。扩展数据集以获得更广泛的潜在表面预计将进一步提高 DFTB+MACE 模型的预测准确性。总体而言，由此产生的 DFTB+MACE 框架能够在超出第一性原理方法范围的长度和时间尺度上进行模拟，同时保留对电子结构的明确描述，使其对于研究材料中的电荷转移特别有吸引力。

English Abstract

We present a model for magnesium-based systems that combines density functional tight binding (DFTB) with MACE, a machine learning interatomic potential (DFTB+MACE). In this model, the conventional repulsive potential, pair potential, is replaced by a many-body MACE potential. The MACE component of the model is trained on the difference between density functional theory (DFT) energies and forces and the corresponding DFTB values, but neglecting the pair potential contribution. Using this model we performed structural relaxation of MgO-CO2 adsorption systems, molecular dynamics calculations of water clusters and phonon spectrum calculations of stable fcc-MgO and metastable bcc-MgO structures. We compare the performance of our model with a pure MACE model and with DFT. We demonstrate that the DFTB+MACE model achieves improved accuracy relative to DFTB with a pair potential, in many cases with only a moderate increase in computational cost. In addition, it can provide electronic structures that most of the machine learning potentials cannot. The training dataset, originally developed for MACE, may not fully represent all regions of the potential surface we may encounter during simulations. Expanding the dataset for a wider potential surface is expected to further enhance predictive accuracy of DFTB+MACE model. Overall, the resulting DFTB+MACE framework enables simulations at length and time scales beyond the reach of first-principles methods while retaining an explicit description of electronic structures, making it particularly attractive for studying charge-transfer in materials.

通过等变神经网络学习自能量#

原题：Learning the self-energy through equivariant neural networks
作者：Wudong Shen、Yang Zhong、Min-Ye Zhang、Xinyang Dong、Hongjun Xiang、Xinguo Ren。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-19
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：电子自能决定了单粒子格林函数，并通过它决定了准粒子能量和谱函数，但从第一原理计算它的成本很高，而且它是一个困难的机器学习目标：复值、局部轨道基础上的矩阵值，并且依赖于连续频率。我们预测了来自原子种类的分子的虚频 G₀W₀ 自能矩阵的动态相关部分，并与 E(3) 等变消息传递网络进行协调，轨道空间矩阵的旋转协方差由结构本身施加，静态交换部分取自密度泛函参考。频率作为显式网络输入或通过特定于频率的输出头进入，并且预测矩阵以因果关系和非负谱密…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子自能决定了单粒子格林函数，并通过它决定了准粒子能量和谱函数，但从第一原理计算它的成本很高，而且它是一个困难的机器学习目标：复值、局部轨道基础上的矩阵值，并且依赖于连续频率。我们预测了来自原子种类的分子的虚频 G₀W₀ 自能矩阵的动态相关部分，并与 E(3) 等变消息传递网络进行协调，轨道空间矩阵的旋转协方差由结构本身施加，静态交换部分取自密度泛函参考。频率作为显式网络输入或通过特定于频率的输出头进入，并且预测矩阵以因果关系和非负谱密度通过构造保持的表示延续至实轴。对于此处研究的系统，频率条件模型将虚轴上的矩阵元素复制到低于 1 meV，并且该精度在延续中得以保留：对于水簇（包括训练集中不存在的四聚体）以及来自 QM9 的化学不同分子，重建的光谱函数遵循 G₀W₀ 参考峰逐峰，并且前沿准粒子水平恢复到几十 meV。因此，学习虚轴上的自能是获得真实频谱的实用途径：一旦模型经过训练，并给定其所建立的密度泛函参考，新的几何形状就不需要显式的 G₀W₀ 计算。

English Abstract

The electronic self-energy determines the single-particle Green's function and, through it, quasiparticle energies and spectral functions, but computing it from first principles is expensive and it is a difficult machine-learning target: complex-valued, matrix-valued in a local-orbital basis, and dependent on a continuous frequency. We predict the dynamical correlation part of the imaginary-frequency G₀W₀ self-energy matrix of molecules from atomic species and coordinates with an E(3)-equivariant message-passing network, the rotational covariance of the orbital-space matrix being imposed by the architecture itself and the static exchange part taken from the density-functional reference. Frequency enters either as an explicit network input or through frequency-specific output heads, and the predicted matrices are continued to the real axis in a representation in which causality and a non-negative spectral density hold by construction. The frequency-conditioned model reproduces matrix elements to below 1 meV on the imaginary axis for the systems studied here, and that accuracy survives the continuation: for water clusters, including a tetramer absent from the training set, and for chemically diverse molecules from QM9, the reconstructed spectral functions follow the G₀W₀ reference peak by peak and the frontier quasiparticle levels are recovered to a few tens of meV. Learning the self-energy on the imaginary axis is therefore a practical route to real-frequency spectra: once the model is trained, and given the density-functional reference it is built on, a new geometry costs no explicit G₀W₀ calculation.

具有半经验轨道电荷的多任务学习可实现样本高效的 MLIP#

原题：Multitask learning with semiempirical orbital charges enables sample-efficient MLIPs
作者：Ihor Neporozhnii、Sjoerd Hoogland、Oleksandr Voznyy。arXiv · physics，2026-10-19

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 需要生成计算成本高昂的大规模训练数据集，以准确模拟材料和分子。使用多任务学习结合电子结构信息可以提高样本效率，但是，对于大型数据集来说，对随原子数量呈二次方缩放的完整哈密顿矩阵进行训练是很困难的。在这项工作中，我们表明，利用轨道解析的半经验电荷进行多任务学习可显着提高 MLIP 中的样本效率和准确性。为了有效地预测轨道电荷，我们实施了专门的等变模型，与不变基线相比，减少了电荷预测误差。通过使用计算成本低廉…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 需要生成计算成本高昂的大规模训练数据集，以准确模拟材料和分子。使用多任务学习结合电子结构信息可以提高样本效率，但是，对于大型数据集来说，对随原子数量呈二次方缩放的完整哈密顿矩阵进行训练是很困难的。在这项工作中，我们表明，利用轨道解析的半经验电荷进行多任务学习可显着提高 MLIP 中的样本效率和准确性。为了有效地预测轨道电荷，我们实施了专门的等变模型，与不变基线相比，减少了电荷预测误差。通过使用计算成本低廉的 GFN1-xTB 轨道电荷（随原子数量线性缩放）来增强训练，我们的模型相对于仅能量基线，能量平均绝对误差降低了 58%，并且需要五倍的数据来匹配其性能。与联合能量和力量训练的更强基线相比，轨道电荷仍然可以将能量误差降低12%。此外，我们的方法匹配或优于在昂贵的密度泛函理论（DFT）原子电荷上训练的模型，捕获轨道解析的电子复杂性并迫使网络学习物理上精确的潜在空间，该空间通过共享的化学性质自发地聚集金属。由于仅在训练期间需要轨道电荷，因此这种方法保留了推理效率，为复杂化学系统开发准确、数据高效的基础模型提供了可扩展的方法。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) require generating computationally expensive, large-scale training datasets to accurately simulate materials and molecules. Incorporating electronic structure information using multitask learning improves sample efficiency, however, training on full Hamiltonian matrices, which scale quadratically with the number of atoms, is intractable for large datasets. In this work, we show that multitask learning utilizing orbitally resolved semiempirical charges significantly improves sample efficiency and accuracy in MLIPs. To efficiently predict orbital charges, we implement a specialized equivariant model, reducing charge prediction error compared to an invariant baseline. By augmenting training with computationally inexpensive GFN1-xTB orbital charges, which scale linearly with the number of atoms, our model achieves a 58% reduction in energy mean absolute error relative to energy-only baselines, and requires five times less data to match their performance. Against the stronger baseline of joint energy-and-force training, orbital charges still reduce the energy error by 12%. Furthermore, our approach matches or outperforms models trained on expensive density functional theory (DFT) atomic charges, capturing orbitally resolved electronic complexity and forcing the network to learn a physically accurate latent space that spontaneously clusters metals by shared chemical properties. Because orbital charges are only required during training, this approach preserves inference efficiency, providing a scalable recipe for developing accurate, data-efficient foundation models for complex chemical systems.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电荷密度波 (CDW) 由电子和晶格畸变之间的相互作用产生，是低维系统的标志。在这里，我们研究了耦合的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 链，包括晶格匹配的梯子和晶格失配的莫尔超晶格，并发现了一组丰富的 CDW 相以及对称保护的束缚态。在晶格匹配链中，电子声子耦合和链间隧道效应之间的竞争驱动了一系列 CDW 相，包括交错的二聚体、三聚体和不通约态。这些相通常是有间隙的，主束缚态受到晶体对称性而不是手性对称性的保护，因此不会被固定到零能量。晶格失配进一步将这些相位重建为长周期超摩尔纹CDW，具有有序的缺陷阵列，固定在摩尔纹长度尺度上，承载局部电子态。因此，链间隧道效应和晶格失配成为一维链中 CDW 有序和束缚态的自然控制参数。

English Abstract

Charge density waves (CDWs) arise from the interplay between electrons and lattice distortions and are a hallmark of low-dimensional systems. Here we study coupled Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains, both lattice-matched ladders and lattice-mismatched moiré superlattices, and find a rich set of CDW phases together with symmetry-protected bound states. In lattice-matched chains, the competition between electron-phonon coupling and interchain tunneling drives a sequence of CDW phases, including staggered dimers, trimers, and incommensurate states. These phases are generally gapped and host bound states protected by crystalline symmetries rather than chiral symmetry, and are therefore not pinned to zero energy. Lattice mismatch further reconstructs these phases into long-period supermoiré CDWs, with an ordered array of defects, pinned to the moiré length scale, that host localized electronic states. Interchain tunneling and lattice mismatch thus emerge as natural control parameters for CDW order and bound states in one-dimensional chains.

中文摘要

本文通过将机器学习的原子间势（即矩张量势（MTP）模型）与第一性原理计算相结合来确定晶体和非晶氧化镓中的晶格热导率（LTC）和电子-声子相互作用。晶体 β-Ga2O3 在 700 K 时表现出 sim0.45 eV 的显着带隙重整化 (BGR)，其中 sim0.2 eV 是由零点 BGR 引起的。计算得出的β-Ga2O₃经典核运动引起的BGR对温度的依赖性强于非晶Ga2O₃，在900 K时BGR差异达到sim0.18 eV。热输运计算表明，在300 K到700 K之间，非晶Ga2O₃的LTC保持在0.9 W·m⁻1·K⁻1附近，大约比非晶Ga2O₃低一个数量级。结晶β-Ga2O₃。总体而言，所提出的框架提供了一种计算上易于处理且可靠的途径，用于预测与微电子和光电子相关的操作条件下的半导体（晶体和非晶）的特性。

English Abstract

The lattice thermal conductivity (LTC) and electron-phonon interactions in crystalline and amorphous gallium oxide are herein determined by coupling a machine-learned interatomic potential, namely the moment tensor potential (MTP) model, to first-principles calculations. Crystalline β-Ga₂O₃ exhibits a substantial band gap renormalization (BGR) of sim0.45 eV at 700 K, with sim0.2 eV caused by zero-point BGR. The computed temperature dependence of BGR induced by classical nuclear motion in β-Ga₂O₃ is stronger than that in amorphous Ga₂O₃, with the difference in BGR reaching sim0.18 eV at 900 K. Thermal transport calculations reveal that the LTC of amorphous Ga₂O₃ remains near 0.9 W· m⁻¹·K⁻¹ for temperatures between 300 K and 700 K, which is approximately an order of magnitude lower than that of crystalline β-Ga₂O₃. Overall, the presented framework provides a computationally tractable and reliable route for predicting properties of semiconductors (both crystalline and amorphous) under operating conditions relevant to microelectronics and optoelectronics.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

层状钠过渡金属 (TM) 氧化物 NaₓTMO2 是最有前途的钠离子正极材料之一，但其 Na/空位排序、层堆叠顺序和协同 Jahn-Teller 畸变仅在少数单 TM 化合物中为人所知。我们将早期研究中报告的Na/空位排序编译成包含41种独特排序的单一库，并将其统一应用于五个单TM和所有十个NaₓTM₀.₅TM'₀.₅O2氧化物（TM = Ti、Mn、Fe、Co、Ni），在1,025次准确的第一原理计算之前筛选了超过120,000个具有机器学习原子间势的结构。相图解释了观察到的 O3/P3/O1 序列和电压，并且 O3 到 P3 的竞争取决于每个原子几 meV 的有序能量，而标量描述符（例如阳离子势和层间板距离）错过了这一点。我们的广泛分析表明，氧化还原对形成了一个几乎与伙伴无关的梯子，Ti³⁺/⁴⁺ < Mn³⁺/⁴⁺ < Co³⁺/⁴⁺ < Ni²⁺/³⁺ < Fe³⁺/⁴⁺，解释了原始电荷转移和氧化顺序。 Van Vleck 对每个 TMO₆ 八面体的分析表明，TM 混合通过电荷转移到 Jahn-Teller 非活性状态或稀释 Jahn-Teller 活性中心来抑制 Mn3+ 和 Ni3⁺ 的协同 Jahn-Teller 畸变。我们制定了能量密度与应变图，得出了一个设计规则：没有 Jahn-Teller 应变的高压电偶，Mn⁴⁺/Ni2⁺ 电荷转移在最实用的组合物 NaₓMn₀.₅Ni₀.₅O2 中实现。我们发布了排序解析的数据集作为训练集，用于后续精确机器学习原子间势的训练。

English Abstract

Layered sodium transition-metal (TM) oxides, NaₓTMO₂, are among the most promising sodium-ion positive electrode materials, yet their Na/vacancy orderings, layer stacking sequences, and cooperative Jahn--Teller distortions are known only for a few single-TM compounds. We compile the Na/vacancy orderings reported in earlier studies into a single library of 41 unique orderings and apply this uniformly to five single-TM and all ten NaₓTM₀.₅TM'₀.₅O₂ oxides (TM = Ti, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni), screening more than 120,000 structures with machine-learning interatomic potentials prior to 1,025 accurate first-principles calculations. The phase diagrams explain the observed O3/P3/O1 sequences and voltages, and the O3-to-P3 competition hinges on ordering energies of a few meV per atom that scalar descriptors, such as the cationic potential and the interlayer-slab distance, miss. Our extensive analysis reveals that the redox couples form a nearly partner-independent ladder, Ti³⁺/⁴⁺ < Mn³⁺/⁴⁺ < Co³⁺/⁴⁺ < Ni²⁺/³⁺ < Fe³⁺/⁴⁺, explaining pristine charge transfer and the oxidation sequence. Van Vleck analysis of every TMO₆ octahedron reveals that TM mixing suppresses the cooperative Jahn--Teller distortions of Mn³⁺ and Ni³⁺ through charge transfer to Jahn--Teller-inactive states or dilution of Jahn--Teller-active centers. We formulate an energy-density-versus-strain map that yields a design rule: a high-voltage couple without Jahn--Teller strain, which Mn⁴⁺/Ni²⁺ charge transfer realizes in NaₓMn₀.₅Ni₀.₅O₂, the most practical composition. We release the ordering-resolved dataset as a training set for subsequent training of accurate machine-learning interatomic potentials.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

未来聚变反应堆中的面向等离子体组件（PFC）必须能够承受极端环境，因此钨（W）因其高导热性、高熔化温度、低溅射率、低氚吸收率而成为主要候选材料。然而，中子辐照引入了复杂的纳米结构缺陷，严重降低了这些关键性能。在这项工作中，我们使用非平衡分子动力学 (NEMD) 模拟与创造弛豫算法 (CRA) 相结合来研究 W 在各种辐照剂量下的晶格热导率 (kappa)。模拟用不同剂量处理的系统，最高可达每原子 1.8 规范位移 (cdpa)，以生成和研究不同的缺陷结构，包括空位和自填隙原子 (SIA) 簇。通过将空间分辨温度梯度与局部缺陷浓度相关联，我们发现即使缺陷类型和分布的微小变化也会显着影响热传输。我们采用机器学习 (ML) 模型来开发缺陷特征与由此产生的热导率下降之间的定量关系。 ML 分析将空位密度确定为控制热传输的主要结构因素。我们发现随着缺陷密度的增加，kappa 受到显着的非线性抑制。基于我们的 ML 模型构建的预测多缺陷映射在 0.05 at.% 的临界空位阈值处解决了阶梯式退化转变，超过该阈值声子散射完全饱和，并将最大相对晶格热导率退化限制在大约 30 at.%。这种集成的模拟和机器学习方法提供了一个强大的预测框架，用于量化辐照聚变材料中的结构和热导率之间的复杂关系。

English Abstract

Plasma-facing components (PFCs) in future fusion reactors must withstand extreme environments, making tungsten (W) a prime candidate material due to its high thermal conductivity, high melting temperature, low sputtering yield, low tritium absorption. However, neutron irradiation introduces complex nanostructural defects that severely degrade these critical properties. In this work, we use Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics (NEMD) simulations coupled with the Creation Relaxation Algorithm (CRA) to investigate the lattice thermal conductivity (kappa) of W across a wide range of irradiation doses. Systems treated with different doses up to 1.8 canonical displacements per atom (cdpa) were simulated to generate and study diverse defect structures, including vacancies and self-interstitial atom (SIA) clusters. By correlating spatially-resolved temperature gradients with local defect concentrations, we show that even small changes in defect type and distribution significantly affect thermal transport. We employ Machine Learning (ML) models to develop a quantitative relationship between the defect features and the resulting thermal conductivity degradation. The ML analysis identifies vacancy density as the dominant structural factor controlling thermal transport. We found a significant, nonlinear suppression of kappa with increasing defect density. Predictive multi-defect mapping built upon our ML model resolved a step-like degradation transition at a critical vacancy threshold of 0.05 at.%, beyond which phonon scattering completely saturated and capped the maximum relative lattice thermal conductivity degradation at approximately 30 at.%. This integrated simulation and ML approach provides a powerful, predictive framework for quantifying the complex relationship between structure and thermal conductivity in irradiated fusion materials.

深层生成晶体结构预测：基准研究和原型依赖性的受控测试#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：crystal structure prediction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

深度生成模型被广泛报道可以实现从头晶体结构预测（CSP），但其能力尚未与基于模板的方法进行一致的测量。我们在 180 个测试结构和 46 个泄漏控制子集上针对 TCSP 2.0 评估了 12 个代表性生成 CSP 模型，涵盖潜变量、扩散、流匹配、自回归和流形随机游走架构。所有方法都使用相同的结构匹配、对称性和共识标准。模板检索是最强的单一方法，达到68.3%的top-1成功率；对称感知的 EquiCSP (66.4%) 和 Uni-3DAR (62.9%) 形成下一层。然而，与 TCSP 2.0 的比较表明，生成模型正确预测的大多数结构也可以通过模板替换正确预测。因此，通过生成唯一可到达的结构集很小，限制了其发现现有原型库之外的结构的实际优势。为了测试这种性能的来源，我们从训练集中删除了整个化学计量原型系列，并重新训练了最强的生成模型。四个系列的准确度下降了 50-78%，表明性能在很大程度上取决于原型。一小部分结构在其原型族被移除后幸存下来，展示了真实但有限的独立于检索的预测能力。因此，当前的生成 CSP 模型在很大程度上充当隐式的、边缘较软的原型库，而不是真正的从头预测器。核心的开放问题是扩大剩余容量，而不仅仅是总匹配率。

English Abstract

Deep generative models are widely reported to enable de novo crystal structure prediction (CSP), but their capability has not been measured consistently against template-based methods. We evaluate 12 representative generative CSP models, spanning latent-variable, diffusion, flow-matching, autoregressive, and manifold random-walk architectures, against TCSP 2.0 on 180 test structures and a leakage-controlled subset of 46. All methods use identical structure-matching, symmetry, and consensus criteria. Template retrieval is the strongest single method, reaching 68.3% top-1 success; symmetry-aware EquiCSP (66.4%) and Uni-3DAR (62.9%) form the next tier. However, comparison with TCSP 2.0 shows that most structures correctly predicted by generative models are also correctly predicted by template substitution. Thus, the set of structures uniquely reachable by generation is small, limiting its practical advantage for discovering structures outside existing prototype libraries. To test the source of this performance, we removed entire stoichiometric prototype families from the training set and retrained the strongest generative model. Accuracy declined by 50-78% across four families, establishing that performance is substantially prototype-dependent. A small minority of structures survived removal of their prototype family, demonstrating a real but limited retrieval-independent predictive capacity. Present generative CSP models therefore function largely as implicit, softer-edged prototype libraries rather than genuinely de novo predictors. Enlarging this residual capacity, rather than aggregate match rate alone, is the central open problem.

多体哈密顿量的吉布斯态元学习及其在量子玻尔兹曼机中的应用#

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：量子吉布斯态的制备是量子计算中的一个基本挑战，对于从建模开放量子系统到量子机器学习等应用至关重要。基于 Cervera-Lierta 等人提出的元变分量子本征求解器框架。和问题驱动的 ansatz 设计，我们引入了两种元学习算法：元变分量子热化器 (Meta-VQT) 和神经网络 Meta-VQT (NN-Meta VQT)，用于在嘈杂的中尺度量子 (NISQ) 设备上进行参数化哈密顿量的高效热状态准备。 Meta-VQT 采用完全量子…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

量子吉布斯态的制备是量子计算中的一个基本挑战，对于从建模开放量子系统到量子机器学习等应用至关重要。基于 Cervera-Lierta 等人提出的元变分量子本征求解器框架。和问题驱动的 ansatz 设计，我们引入了两种元学习算法：元变分量子热化器 (Meta-VQT) 和神经网络 Meta-VQT (NN-Meta VQT)，用于在嘈杂的中尺度量子 (NISQ) 设备上进行参数化哈密顿量的高效热状态准备。 Meta-VQT 采用完全量子模拟，而 NN Meta-VQT 则集成了量子-经典混合架构。两者都对一组有限的训练参数值使用集体优化，以在采样参数域内的保留参数值处准备近似吉布斯状态。我们在具有多达八个量子位的横向场伊辛模型（TFIM）和具有所有场项的两量子位海森堡模型上验证了我们的方法。在这些有限尺寸的基准中，经过训练的模型可以在训练间隔内的测试点准确地准备热状态。对于 TFIM 的 6 个和 8 个量子位情况，元学习参数与适当设计的 ansatz 相结合可用作热启动初始化，在优化任务中显着优于随机初始化。此外，3 量子位 Kitaev 环示例展示了我们的算法在有限温度交叉机制中的有效性。最后，我们应用我们的算法在具有所有场项的 2 量子位海森堡模型上训练量子玻尔兹曼机 (QBM)，与现有技术（例如基于变分量子虚数时间 (VarQITE) 的 QBM）相比，实现了更高的训练效率、更高的吉布斯状态精度和 30 倍的运行时间加速，凸显了基于元算法的 QBM 的实用性。

English Abstract

The preparation of quantum Gibbs states is a fundamental challenge in quantum computing, essential for applications ranging from modeling open quantum systems to quantum machine learning. Building on the Meta-Variational Quantum Eigensolver framework proposed by Cervera-Lierta et al. and a problem-driven ansatz design, we introduce two meta-learning algorithms: Meta-Variational Quantum Thermalizer (Meta-VQT) and Neural Network Meta-VQT (NN-Meta VQT) for efficient thermal state preparation of parametrized Hamiltonians on Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. Meta-VQT utilizes a fully quantum ansatz, while NN Meta-VQT integrates a quantum--classical hybrid architecture. Both use collective optimization over a finite set of training parameter values to prepare approximate Gibbs states at held-out parameter values within the sampled parameter domain. We validate our methods on Transverse Field Ising Models (TFIM) with up to eight qubits and on a two-qubit Heisenberg model with all field terms. Within these finite-size benchmarks, the trained models accurately prepare thermal states at test points inside the training interval. For the six and eight qubit cases of TFIM, meta-learned parameters when combined with appropriately designed ansatz serve as warm-start initializations, significantly outperforming random initializations in the optimization tasks. Furthermore, a 3 qubit Kitaev ring example showcases our algorithm's effectiveness across finite-temperature crossover regimes. Finally, we apply our algorithms to train a Quantum Boltzmann Machine (QBM) on a 2-qubit Heisenberg model with all field terms, achieving enhanced training efficiency, improved Gibbs state accuracy, and a 30-fold runtime speedup over existing techniques such as variational quantum imaginary time (VarQITE)-based QBM highlighting the practicality of meta-algorithm-based QBMs.

用于筛选分子结构的空间统计#

原题：Spatial statistics for screening molecular structures

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

计算材料发现的主导范式依赖于高度参数化的深度架构，包括消息传递图网络和等变量模型，这些架构需要数百万个DFT标记的训练结构，并产生非凸潜在表示，从而使逆向设计的连续优化复杂化。这些架构在数据稀缺的情况下是不切实际的，这是分子筛选的典型情况，并且在捕获化学无序配置和手性几何形状方面表现出有据可查的局限性。本综述将基于空间统计的特征工程作为一种物理严谨且可立即部署的替代方案。分子结构被编码为体素化标量场，并通过快速傅里叶变换确定性地评估两点自相关和互相关，明确地将空间模式识别的负担从学习算法转移到封闭形式的物理信息操作。由此产生的相关图的主成分分析产生了支持精益神经网络（ < 100k可训练参数）和非参数代理模型的低维、严格凸表示，只需10个训练样本即可实现低于2%的预测误差。该框架在周期性晶体、化学无序高熵合金和非周期性有机分子上进行了演示，实现了贝叶斯主动学习和商品硬件上的零点外推，目前的大规模架构无法以等效的数据预算进行复制。

English Abstract

The dominant paradigm in computational materials discovery relies on heavily parameterized deep architectures, including message-passing graph networks and equivariant models, that require millions of DFT-labeled training structures and produce non-convex latent representations that complicate continuous optimization for inverse design. These architectures are impractical in data-scarce regimes, which is the typical case in molecular screening, and exhibit well-documented limitations in capturing chemically disordered configurations and chiral geometries. This review presents feature engineering based on spatial statistics as a physically rigorous and immediately deployable alternative. Molecular structures are encoded as voxelized scalar fields, and two-point auto- and cross-correlations are evaluated deterministically via Fast Fourier Transforms, explicitly transferring the burden of spatial pattern recognition from the learning algorithm to a closed-form, physics-informed operation. Principal component analysis of the resulting correlation maps yields low-dimensional, strictly convex representations that support lean neural networks (<100k trainable parameters) and non-parametric surrogate models, achieving sub-2% prediction error with as few as 10 training samples. Demonstrated across periodic crystals, chemically disordered high-entropy alloys, and non-periodic organic molecules, this framework enables Bayesian active learning and zero-shot extrapolation on commodity hardware, which current large-scale architectures cannot replicate at equivalent data budgets.

石墨烯的应变相关润湿#

作者：Darren Wayne Lim、Xavier R. Advincula、William C. Witt、Angelos Michaelides、Fabian L. Thiemann、Christoph Schran。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-21

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：了解水如何润湿石墨烯对于预测和控制其在纳米流体、传感和能源应用中的行为至关重要。润湿的一个关键指标是液滴与表面形成的接触角，但石墨烯的实验测量范围很广，对于独立式石墨烯尚未达成共识。在这里，我们使用从头算精度的机器学习潜力，为这个未解决的问题提供原子第一原理预测，发现弱亲水接触角为 72.1 ± 1.5 度。更重要的是，我们发现石墨烯的润湿特性对机械应变高度敏感：拉伸应变使石墨烯的亲水性显着降低，而压缩应变会在液滴周围引起相干波纹，从而…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

了解水如何润湿石墨烯对于预测和控制其在纳米流体、传感和能源应用中的行为至关重要。润湿的一个关键指标是液滴与表面形成的接触角，但石墨烯的实验测量范围很广，对于独立式石墨烯尚未达成共识。在这里，我们使用从头算精度的机器学习潜力，为这个未解决的问题提供原子第一原理预测，发现弱亲水接触角为 72.1 ± 1.5 度。更重要的是，我们发现石墨烯的润湿特性对机械应变高度敏感：拉伸应变使石墨烯的亲水性显着降低，而压缩应变会在液滴周围引起相干波纹，从而导致明显的各向异性润湿和接触角滞后。我们表明，三相接触线和独立石墨烯的固有热波纹之间存在很强的耦合，这导致了这种应变敏感性。我们的结果表明，二维膜的润湿性不仅受其化学性质的影响，还受其动态形态的影响，引入了原子薄材料特有的一类新的润湿行为，为实验测量的可变性提供了额外的解释。这些发现表明，机械应变可能是控制基于二维纳米材料的技术中润湿的实用途径，对纳米流体和纳米过滤应用具有良好的效果。

English Abstract

Understanding how water wets graphene is critical for predicting and controlling its behaviour in nanofluidic, sensing, and energy applications. A key measure of wetting is the contact angle made by a liquid droplet against the surface, yet experimental measurements for graphene span a wide range, with no consensus for free-standing graphene. Here, we use a machine learning potential with ab initio accuracy to provide an atomistic first-principles prediction for this unsolved problem, finding a weakly hydrophilic contact angle of 72.1 ± 1.5 deg. More importantly, we unveil that graphene's wetting properties are highly sensitive to mechanical strain: tensile strain makes graphene significantly less hydrophilic, while compressive strain induces coherent ripples around the droplet, resulting in pronounced anisotropic wetting and contact angle hysteresis. We show that there is a strong coupling between the three-phase contact line and the intrinsic thermal ripples of free-standing graphene, which contributes to this strain sensitivity. Our results demonstrate that the wettability of 2D membranes are governed not only by their chemistry but also by their dynamic morphology, introducing a new class of wetting behaviour unique to atomically thin materials that offers an additional explanation for variability in experimental measurements. These findings reveal that mechanical strain may be a practical route to controlling wetting in 2D nanomaterials-based technologies, with promising consequences for nanofluidic and nano-filtration applications.

Mamba 量子本征求解器 (MQE)：用于量子化学中可扩展基态模拟的选择性状态空间模型#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

使用量子计算方法预测分子电子结构是计算化学中的一个挑战。传统的变分量子本征求解器 (VQE) 通常会受到优化挑战的阻碍。另一种生成量子本征解算器 (GQE) 通过将基态搜索重新表述为离散序列生成来缓解这一问题，但其 Transformer 主干网络面临着二次计算复杂性的问题，成为较大分子的可扩展性的瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 Mamba Quantum Eigensolver (Mamba-QE)，它用选择性状态空间模型 (SSM) 代替 Transformer，以处理具有线性 O(L) 复杂度的算子序列。此外，我们还集成了距离调节机制，可以直接预测完整的势能面（PES）。在 H2 分子上进行评估后，Mamba-QE 在化学精度范围内重现了完全构型相互作用 (FCI) 参考能量，在 16 个几何形状中实现了低于 3×10⁻⁶ Ha 的能量误差，且变分违规为零。 Mamba-QE 保持高表达能力，同时为复杂分子系统的量子模拟建立强大、可扩展的基础。

English Abstract

Predicting molecular electronic structure using Quantum Computing approaches is a challenge in Computational Chemistry. Traditional Variational Quantum Eigensolvers (VQEs) are typically hindered by optimization challenges. The alternative Generative Quantum Eigensolver (GQE) mitigates this by reformulating ground-state search as discrete sequence generation, but its Transformer backbone suffers from quadratic computational complexity, bottlenecking scalability for larger molecules. To address this, we introduce the Mamba Quantum Eigensolver (Mamba-QE), which replaces the Transformer with a Selective State Space Model (SSM) to process operator sequences with linear O(L) complexity. Additionally, we integrate a distance-conditioned mechanism enabling the direct prediction of complete potential energy surfaces (PES). Evaluated on the H₂ molecule, Mamba-QE reproduces Full Configuration Interaction (FCI) reference energies well within chemical accuracy, achieving an energy error below 3×10⁻⁶ Ha across 16 geometries with zero variational violations. Mamba-QE maintains high expressive power while establishing a robust, scalable foundation for quantum simulations of complex molecular systems.
